TL;DR: 本文提出Retina-RAG，一个低成本模块化框架，联合执行糖尿病视网膜病变严重程度分级、黄斑水肿检测和报告生成。该框架通过解耦高性能视网膜分类器与基于LoRA微调的视觉语言模型，并利用检索增强生成模块注入眼科知识，在单个消费级GPU上实现了超越多个基线的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有糖尿病视网膜病变自动筛查系统大多局限于图像级分类，缺乏临床结构化的报告生成能力，且现有方法在诊断一致性和幻觉抑制方面存在不足。

Method: Retina-RAG采用模块化架构，将高性能视网膜分类器与经LoRA适配的Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct视觉语言模型解耦；引入检索增强生成（RAG）模块，在推理时注入精选眼科知识和结构化分类器输出，以提高诊断一致性并减少幻觉。

Result: 在视网膜疾病检测数据集上，DR分级F1-score达0.731，ME检测F1-score达0.948，显著优于零样本Qwen（0.096, 0.732）和MMed-RAG（0.541, 0.641）；报告生成的ROUGE-L为0.429，SBERT相似度为0.884，超越所有基线。整个框架可在单张消费级GPU上运行。

Conclusion: 临床结构化的视网膜AI可以在适度计算资源下实现，证明了低成本、模块化框架在眼科AI辅助诊断中的可行性和有效性。

Original Abstract: Diabetic Retinopathy (DR) is a leading cause of preventable blindness among working-age adults worldwide, yet most automated screening systems are limited to image-level classification and lack clinically structured reporting. We propose Retina-RAG, a low-cost modular framework that jointly performs DR severity grading, macular edema (ME) detection, and report generation. The architecture decouples a high-performance retinal classifier and a parameter-efficient vision-language model (Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct) adapted via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), enabling flexible component integration. A retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) module injects curated ophthalmic knowledge together with structured classifier outputs at inference time to improve diagnostic consistency and reduce hallucinations. Retina-RAG achieves an F1-score of 0.731 for DR grading and 0.948 for ME detection, substantially outperforming zero-shot Qwen (0.096, 0.732) and MMed-RAG (0.541, 0.641) on a retinal disease detection dataset with captions. For report generation, Retina-RAG attains ROUGE-L 0.429 and SBERT similarity 0.884, exceeding all baselines. The full framework operates on a single consumer-grade GPU, demonstrating that clinically structured retinal AI can be achieved with modest computational resources.

中文摘要翻译: 糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）是全球劳动年龄人口可预防性失明的主要原因，但目前大多数自动化筛查系统仅限于图像级分类，缺乏临床结构化的报告生成能力。本文提出Retina-RAG，一种低成本的模块化框架，可联合执行DR严重程度分级、黄斑水肿（ME）检测和报告生成。该架构将高性能视网膜分类器与通过低秩适配（LoRA）微调的参数高效视觉语言模型（Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct）解耦，实现灵活的组件集成。检索增强生成（RAG）模块在推理时注入精选眼科知识及结构化的分类器输出，以提高诊断一致性并减少幻觉。Retina-RAG在带有标注说明的视网膜疾病检测数据集上，DR分级F1-score达到0.731，ME检测达到0.948，大幅优于零样本Qwen（0.096, 0.732）和MMed-RAG（0.541, 0.641）。在报告生成方面，Retina-RAG的ROUGE-L达到0.429，SBERT相似度达到0.884，超越所有基线。整个框架可在单个消费级GPU上运行，表明临床结构化的视网膜AI可以在适度计算资源下实现。

</details>


### [61] [SuperFace: Preference-Aligned Facial Expression Estimation Beyond Pseudo Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06179)
*Zejian Kang,Xuanyang Xu,Wentao Yang,Kai Zheng,Yuanchen Fei,Hongyuan Zou,Hui Shan,Shuo Yang,Xiangru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SuperFace框架，通过人类偏好反馈优化ARKit面部表情系数预测，取代传统的伪标签监督方法，实现更逼真的面部动画。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ARKit blendshape系数预测方法依赖Live Link Face等软件提供伪标签，这些伪标签存在噪声激活、系数偏差、不准确的面部动作等问题，导致模型复现了有缺陷的伪标签而非优化感知保真度。

Method: 提出SuperFace偏好驱动框架：将软件估计的系数仅作为初始化，通过人类对渲染面部表情的偏好反馈来优化系数预测，从伪标签模仿转向与人类对齐的感知优化。

Result: 实验表明，SuperFace在表情保真度上优于Live Link Face监督方法，证明了偏好驱动优化在语义面部动作预测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过人类偏好反馈对齐模型与感知判断，而非拟合数值化的伪标签，能够生成视觉上更逼真、更具表现力的面部动画。

Original Abstract: Accurate facial estimation is crucial for realistic digital human animation, and ARKit blendshape coefficients offer an interpretable representation by mapping facial motions to semantic animation controls. However, learning high-quality ARKit coefficient prediction remains limited by the absence of reliable ground-truth supervision. Existing methods typically rely on capture software such as Live Link Face to provide pseudo labels, which may contain noisy activations, biased coefficient magnitudes, and missing or inaccurate facial actions. Consequently, models trained with supervised learning tend to reproduce imperfect pseudo labels rather than optimize for perceptual expression fidelity. In this paper, we propose SuperFace, a preference-driven framework that moves ARKit facial expression estimation from pseudo-label imitation toward human-aligned perceptual optimization. Instead of treating software-estimated coefficients as fixed ground truth, SuperFace uses them only as an initialization and further improves coefficient prediction through human preference feedback on rendered facial expressions. By aligning the model with perceptual judgments rather than numerical pseudo labels, SuperFace enables more visually faithful and expressive facial animation. Experiments show that SuperFace improves expression fidelity over Live Link Face supervision, demonstrating the effectiveness of preference-driven optimization for semantic facial action prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的面部估计对于逼真的数字人动画至关重要，ARKit blendshape系数通过将面部运动映射到语义动画控制，提供了一种可解释的表示。然而，学习高质量的ARKit系数预测仍受限于缺乏可靠的真实标签监督。现有方法通常依赖Live Link Face等捕捉软件提供伪标签，这些伪标签可能包含噪声激活、系数偏差以及缺失或不准确的面部动作。因此，基于监督学习的模型倾向于复现有缺陷的伪标签，而非针对感知表达保真度进行优化。本文提出SuperFace，一种偏好驱动框架，将ARKit面部表情估计从伪标签模仿转向与人类对齐的感知优化。SuperFace不将软件估计的系数视为固定真实标签，而是仅将其作为初始化，并通过人类对渲染面部表情的偏好反馈进一步改进系数预测。通过使模型与感知判断而非数值化伪标签对齐，SuperFace能够生成视觉上更逼真、更具表现力的面部动画。实验表明，SuperFace在表情保真度上优于Live Link Face监督，证明了偏好驱动优化在语义面部动作预测中的有效性。

</details>


### [62] [EA-WM: Event-Aware Generative World Model with Structured Kinematic-to-Visual Action Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06192)
*Zhaoyang Yang,Yurun Jin,Lizhe Qi,Cong Huang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EA-WM提出了一种事件感知生成式世界模型，通过将运动控制信号转换为结构化的运动-视觉动作场，并引入事件感知双向融合模块，实现了机器人视频生成中精确的空间几何与交互动态建模，在WorldArena基准上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型将视频生成视为策略学习的辅助表征，未能充分利用动作信号指导视频合成，导致生成的机器人轨迹中空间几何结构和细粒度交互动态保留不足。

Method: 提出EA-WM：1) 将动作和运动状态投影到目标相机视角，形成结构化的运动-视觉动作场（Structured Kinematic-to-Visual Action Fields）；2) 引入事件感知双向融合模块（event-aware bidirectional fusion blocks），通过调节跨分支注意力来捕捉物体状态变化和交互动态。

Result: 在WorldArena基准测试上，EA-WM达到了最先进的性能，显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将运动控制信号以几何可解释的方式注入视频生成过程，EA-WM有效弥合了运动控制与视觉感知之间的鸿沟，证明了结构化动作表征在机器人世界模型中的关键作用。

Original Abstract: Pretrained video diffusion models provide powerful spatiotemporal generative priors, making them a natural foundation for robotic world models. While recent world-action models jointly optimize future videos and actions, they predominantly treat video generation as an auxiliary representation for policy learning. Consequently, they insufficiently explore the inverse problem: leveraging action signals to guide video synthesis, thereby often failing to preserve precise robot spatial geometry and fine-grained robot-object interaction dynamics in the generated rollouts. To bridge this gap, we present EA-WM, an Event-Aware Generative World Model that effectively closes the loop between kinematic control and visual perception. Rather than injecting joint or end-effector actions as abstract, low-dimensional tokens, EA-WM projects actions and kinematic states directly into the target camera view as Structured Kinematic-to-Visual Action Fields. To fully exploit this geometrically grounded representation, we introduce event-aware bidirectional fusion blocks that modulate cross-branch attention, capturing object state changes and interaction dynamics. Evaluated on the comprehensive WorldArena benchmark, EA-WM achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing baselines by a significant margin.

中文摘要翻译: 预训练的视频扩散模型提供了强大的时空生成先验，使其成为机器人世界模型的自然基础。尽管最近的世界-动作模型联合优化了未来视频和动作，但它们主要将视频生成视为策略学习的辅助表征。因此，它们未能充分探索逆问题：利用动作信号引导视频合成，从而在生成的轨迹中常常无法保留精确的机器人空间几何结构以及细粒度的机器人-物体交互动态。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了EA-WM，一种事件感知生成式世界模型，有效闭环了运动控制与视觉感知之间的联系。EA-WM不是将关节或末端执行器动作注入为抽象的、低维的令牌，而是将动作和运动状态直接投影到目标相机视角，形成结构化的运动-视觉动作场。为充分利用这种几何可解释的表征，我们引入了事件感知双向融合模块，通过调节跨分支注意力来捕捉物体状态变化和交互动态。在全面的WorldArena基准测试上，EA-WM取得了最先进的性能，以显著优势超越了现有基线方法。

</details>


### [63] [Taming the Entropy Cliff: Variable Codebook Size Quantization for Autoregressive Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06207)
*Bowen Zheng,Weijian Luo,Guang Yang,Colin Zhang,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉分词器中统一码本设计存在信息理论极限（熵悬崖现象），并提出可变码本大小量化（VCQ），通过沿序列单调增大码本大小来突破该瓶颈，在不改变训练流程的情况下显著提升图像重建质量和自回归生成性能，并自然形成粗到细的语义层次。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有离散视觉分词器默认所有序列位置共享同一码本，研究者试图通过扩大码本大小K来提升重建性能，但这种恒定码本设计存在信息理论极限：训练集的逐位置条件熵沿序列快速衰减，导致大部分位置实际上变成了记忆化问题（熵悬崖）。这一现象在语言中不出现，因此需要为视觉模态设计新的分词策略。

Method: 提出可变码本大小量化（Variable Codebook Size Quantization, VCQ），让码本大小K_t沿序列位置单调增长，从K_min=2到K_max，保持损失函数、参数量和自回归训练流程不变。通过极小的早期码本（K=2）制造信息瓶颈，迫使模型学习粗到细的语义层次。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256上，基础版VCQ将gFID（无CFG）从27.98降至14.80；扩大到684M自回归参数后达到gFID 1.71，且无需语义正则化或因果对齐等额外技术。仅对前10个token做线性探测即可达到43.8%的top-1准确率（均匀码本为27.1%）。

Conclusion: 重要的不仅是码本的总容量，还包括容量如何分布和组织。VCQ通过沿序列动态分配码本容量，有效解决了视觉分词中的熵悬崖问题，同时自然引入了语义层次结构。

Original Abstract: Most discrete visual tokenizers rely on a default design: every position in the sequence shares the same codebook. Researchers try to scale the codebook size $K$ to get better reconstruction performance. Such a constant-codebook design hits a fundamental information-theoretic limit. We observe that the per-position conditional entropy of the training set decays so quickly along the sequence that, after a few positions, the conditional distribution becomes essentially deterministic. On ImageNet with $K=16384$, this happens within only 2 out of 256 positions, turning the remaining 254 into a memorization problem. We call this phenomenon the Entropy Cliff and formalize it with a simple expression: $t^{*} = \lceil \log_2 N / \log_2 K \rceil$. Interestingly, this phenomenon is not observed in language, as its natural structure keeps the effective entropy per position well below the codebook capacity. To address this, we propose Variable Codebook Size Quantization (VCQ), where the codebook size $K_t$ grows monotonically along the sequence from $K_{\min}=2$ to $K_{\max}$, leaving the loss function, parameter count, and AR training procedure unchanged. With a vanilla autoregressive Transformer and standard next-token prediction, a base version of VCQ reduces gFID w/o CFG from 27.98 to 14.80 on ImageNet $256\times256$ over the baseline. Scaled up, it reaches gFID 1.71 with 684M autoregressive parameters, without any extra training techniques such as semantic regularization or causal alignment. The extreme information bottleneck at $K_{\min}=2$ naturally induces a coarse-to-fine semantic hierarchy: a linear probe on only the first 10 tokens reaches 43.8% top-1 accuracy on ImageNet, compared to 27.1% for uniform codebooks. Ultimately, these results show that what matters is not only the total capacity of the codebook, but also how that capacity is distributed and organized.

中文摘要翻译: 大多数离散视觉分词器都采用一种默认设计：序列中的每个位置共享同一个码本。研究者尝试通过扩大码本大小K来获得更好的重建性能。但这种恒定码本的设计触及了一个基础的信息理论极限。我们观察到，训练集的逐位置条件熵沿序列衰减得非常快，以至于在经过少量位置后，条件分布变得基本确定。在ImageNet上，当K=16384时，这种情况在256个位置中仅前2个位置内就会发生，使得剩余254个位置变成一个记忆化问题。我们将这种现象称为熵悬崖，并用一个简单的公式形式化：t^{*} = \lceil \log_2 N / \log_2 K \rceil。有趣的是，这种现像在语言中并未观察到，因为语言的自然结构使每个位置的有效熵远低于码本容量。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了可变码本大小量化（VCQ），其中码本大小K_t沿序列从K_min=2到K_max单调增长，且不改变损失函数、参数量和自回归训练流程。使用普通的自回归Transformer和标准的下一token预测，VCQ的基础版本在ImageNet 256×256上将无CFG的gFID从27.98降至14.80。扩展后，它在684M自回归参数下达到了gFID 1.71，无需任何额外训练技术（如语义正则化或因果对齐）。K_min=2处的极端信息瓶颈自然地诱导出从粗到细的语义层次：仅对前10个token进行线性探测即可在ImageNet上达到43.8%的top-1准确率，而均匀码本为27.1%。最终，这些结果表明，重要的不仅是码本的总容量，还有这些容量如何分布和组织。

</details>


### [64] [Differentiable Adaptive 4D Structured Illumination for Joint Capture of Shape and Reflectance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06214)
*Huakeng Ding,Yaowen Chen,Kun Zhou,Hongzhi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种可微分的自适应4D光照计算框架，结合统一的空间-角度结构光和单相机，实现物体形状与反射率的高效同步采集，深度重建结果优于现有技术，反射率结果经照片验证具有可比性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 为了实现高效、高质量的物体形状与反射率同步采集，并且能够在采集过程中自适应地优化下一次光照条件以降低深度不确定性。

Method: 基于直方图的像素级概率模型（用于深度和反射率），通过可微分方式将下一次光照条件与鼓励降低深度不确定性的损失函数关联起来；在施加新的结构光照后，利用图像测量更新每个像素的不确定性；最后通过微调方式最小化物理测量与模拟结果之间的差异，重建深度图和反射率参数图。

Result: 在形状和外观差异较大的物理物体上验证了方法的有效性，深度重建结果优于现有技术，反射率结果经与照片对比验证具有可比性。

Conclusion: 该可微分框架能够实现物体形状与反射率的同步高效高质量采集，深度估计达到先进水平，反射率估计可靠。

Original Abstract: We present a differentiable framework to adaptively compute 4D illumination conditions with respect to an object, for efficient, high-quality simultaneous acquisition of its shape and reflectance, with a unified spatial-angular structured light and a single camera. Using a simple histogram-based pixel-level probability model for depth and reflectance, we differentiably link the next illumination condition(s) with a loss that encourages the reduction in depth uncertainty. As new structured illumination is cast, corresponding image measurements are used to update the uncertainty at each pixel. Finally, a fine-tuning-based approach reconstructs the depth map and reflectance parameter maps, by minimizing the differences between all physical measurements and their simulated counterparts. The effectiveness of our framework is demonstrated on physical objects with wide variations in shape and appearance. Our depth results compare favorably with state-of-the-art techniques, while our reflectance results are comparable when validated against photographs.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种可微分框架，用于根据物体自适应计算4D光照条件，以实现其形状与反射率的高效、高质量同步采集，仅需统一的空间-角度结构光与单个相机。通过一个基于直方图的简单像素级深度与反射率概率模型，我们将下一次光照条件与鼓励降低深度不确定性的损失函数可微地关联起来。随着新的结构光照投射，相应的图像测量用于更新每个像素的不确定性。最后，通过最小化所有物理测量与其模拟对应值之间的差异，采用基于微调的方法重建深度图和反射率参数图。我们在形状和外观差异较大的物理物体上验证了该框架的有效性。深度结果与最先进技术相比具有竞争力，而反射率结果经照片验证也达到了可比水平。

</details>


### [65] [Look Beyond Saliency: Low-Attention Guided Dual Encoding for Video Semantic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06229)
*Faisal Aljehrai,Mohammed A. Alkhrashi,Alreem Almuhrij,Sarah Abuhimed,Noorh Aldossary,Abdullah Aldwyish,Raied Aljadaany,Huda Alamri,Muhammad Kamran J Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种逆注意力嵌入机制，通过显式捕获视觉编码器忽略的背景区域，与传统视觉嵌入结合，无需额外训练即可显著提升密集拥挤场景下的视频语义搜索性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在密集拥挤场景中，视觉编码器倾向于关注显著前景区域而忽略上下文重要的背景区域，导致视频语义搜索性能下降。

Method: 提出逆注意力嵌入（Inverse Attention Embedding）机制，显式捕获并突出被传统视觉编码器忽略的背景区域，并将其与传统的视觉嵌入相结合，无需额外训练。

Result: 在密集拥挤场景下的视频语义搜索任务中，初始实验和消融研究表明该方法在召回率上比现有方法有显著提升。

Conclusion: 逆注意力嵌入机制能够有效弥补视觉编码器对背景区域关注不足的问题，在不增加训练成本的前提下提升视频语义检索性能。

Original Abstract: Video semantic search in densely crowded scenes remains a challenging task due to visual encoders tendency to prioritize salient foreground regions while neglecting contextually important, background areas. We propose an Inverse Attention Embedding mechanism that explicitly captures and highlights these overlooked regions. By combining inverse attention embeddings with traditional visual embeddings, our method significantly enhances semantic retrieval performance without additional training. Initial experiments and ablation studies demonstrate promising improvements over existing approaches in recall for video semantic search in crowded environments.

中文摘要翻译: 在密集拥挤场景下，由于视觉编码器倾向于优先关注显著的前景区域而忽略上下文重要的背景区域，视频语义搜索仍然是一项具有挑战性的任务。我们提出了一种逆注意力嵌入机制，该机制能够显式地捕获并突出这些被忽略的区域。通过将逆注意力嵌入与传统视觉嵌入相结合，我们的方法无需额外训练即可显著提升语义检索性能。在密集拥挤环境下的视频语义搜索任务中，初始实验和消融研究表明，该方法在召回率上相较于现有方法取得了令人鼓舞的提升。

</details>


### [66] [Spark3R: Asymmetric Token Reduction Makes Fast Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06270)
*Zecheng Tang,Jiaye Fu,Qiankun Gao,Haijie Li,Yanmin Wu,Jiaqi Zhang,Siwei Ma,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spark3R提出了一种免训练加速框架，通过解耦查询令牌和键值令牌的压缩策略（查询令牌用组内令牌合并，键值令牌用轻量级令牌剪枝），在前馈式3D重建模型中实现高达28倍的速度提升，同时保持重建质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 前馈式3D重建模型在处理视频级（数百到数千帧）输入时，全局注意力层的二次复杂度导致计算开销过大。现有令牌合并方法对查询令牌和键值令牌采用统一的压缩策略，忽略了它们在3D重建中功能角色的根本差异，导致压缩效果不佳。

Method: Spark3R是一种免训练加速框架，核心包括：(1) 解耦查询令牌与键值令牌的压缩，查询令牌采用组内令牌合并（intra-group token merging），键值令牌采用轻量级令牌剪枝（lightweight token pruning），并分配不同的缩减因子；(2) 跨层自适应调整键值缩减因子，以优化质量-效率权衡。

Result: Spark3R作为即插即用框架，可直接集成到VGGT、π³、Depth-Anything-3等多个预训练模型中，在1000帧输入上实现高达28倍加速，同时保持具有竞争力的重建质量。

Conclusion: 通过识别查询令牌对压缩敏感而键值令牌可容忍激进压缩这一关键特性，Spark3R以解耦压缩策略有效解决了前馈式3D重建模型在视频级输入上的扩展性问题，且无需重新训练。

Original Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models based on Vision Transformers can directly estimate scene geometry and camera poses from a small set of input images, but scaling them to video inputs with hundreds or thousands of frames remains challenging due to the quadratic cost of global attention layers. Recent token-merging methods accelerate these models by compressing the token sequence within the global attention layers, but they apply a uniform reduction to query tokens and key-value tokens, ignoring their functionally distinct roles in 3D reconstruction. In this work, we identify a key property of feed-forward 3D reconstruction models: query tokens encode view-specific geometric requests and are sensitive to compression, while key-value tokens represent shared scene context and tolerate aggressive compression. Guided by this insight, we propose Spark3R, a training-free acceleration framework that decouples the compression of query tokens and key-value tokens by assigning distinct reduction factors, with intra-group token merging applied to query tokens and lightweight token pruning to key-value tokens. Additionally, Spark3R adaptively adjusts the key-value reduction factor across layers, further improving the quality-efficiency trade-off. As a plug-and-play framework requiring no retraining, Spark3R integrates directly into multiple pretrained feed-forward 3D reconstruction models, including VGGT, $π^3$, and Depth-Anything-3, and achieves up to $28\times$ speedup on 1,000-frame inputs while maintaining competitive reconstruction quality.

中文摘要翻译: 基于Vision Transformer的前馈式3D重建模型可以直接从少量输入图像中估计场景几何和相机位姿，但由于全局注意力层的二次复杂度，将其扩展到包含数百或数千帧的视频输入仍然具有挑战性。最近的令牌合并方法通过压缩全局注意力层中的令牌序列来加速这些模型，但这些方法对查询令牌和键值令牌施加了统一的缩减策略，忽略了它们在3D重建中功能角色的根本差异。在本工作中，我们识别了前馈式3D重建模型的一个关键特性：查询令牌编码了视角特定的几何请求，对压缩较为敏感；而键值令牌代表了共享的场景上下文，能够容忍激进的压缩。基于这一洞察，我们提出Spark3R，一种免训练的加速框架，通过分配不同的缩减因子来解耦查询令牌和键值令牌的压缩，其中查询令牌采用组内令牌合并，键值令牌采用轻量级令牌剪枝。此外，Spark3R在不同层之间自适应调整键值缩减因子，进一步优化质量-效率权衡。作为一个无需重新训练的即插即用框架，Spark3R可直接集成到多个预训练的前馈式3D重建模型中，包括VGGT、π³和Depth-Anything-3，在1000帧输入上实现高达28倍加速，同时保持具有竞争力的重建质量。

</details>


### [67] [On-Orbit Real-Time Wildfire Detection Under On-Board Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06273)
*Matthias Rötzer,Veronika Pörtge,Martin Ickerott,Jayendra Praveen Kumar Chorapalli,Dimitri Scheftelowitsch,Max Bereczky,Dmitry Rashkovetsky,Sai Manoj Appalla,Julia Gottfriedsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出并部署了一套基于9颗卫星的商业热红外星座在轨 wildfire 检测系统，在严格资源约束（模型<1MB，推理<150ms，端到端<10分钟）下，采用DenseMAE自监督预训练方法处理未校准MWIR单波段图像中的亚像素级火灾异常，在极端类别不平衡下实现了优于监督基线的检测性能（最佳AP 0.699，Fire-F1 0.744）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有主流火灾监测系统（如MODIS、VIIRS）依赖上下文热阈值处理管线，无法有效处理200米地面采样距离下亚像素或单像素级热异常以及极端类别不平衡问题；同时需要在轨部署的模型面临严格的存储、延迟和端到端时间联合约束（<1MB, <150ms, <10分钟），亟需一种轻量级且高效的密集表示学习方法。

Method: 采用密集掩码自编码（DenseMAE）及其与指数移动平均（EMA）蒸馏结合的混合变体进行自监督预训练，在9颗卫星专有MWIR数据集上学习密集表示；随后通过线性探测和全分布像素级平均精度（AP）评估表示质量；最终在NVIDIA Jetson Xavier NX上使用TensorRT FP16推理部署紧凑型下游模型。

Result: 最快的SSL预训练模型在65.34 ms/批延迟和0.52 MB引擎大小下达到0.640 AP和0.69 Fire-F1；最佳配置在低于1 MB模型下达到0.699 AP和0.744 Fire-F1，均优于在可比约束下的监督基线（0.650 AP）。

Conclusion: DenseMAE自监督预训练能够在严格的在轨部署约束下，实现紧凑下游模型在延迟-精度Pareto前沿上的最优折中，为基于热红外卫星星座的实时野火检测提供了一种可行且高效的技术路径。

Original Abstract: We present a deployed system for on-orbit wildfire detection aboard a nine-satellite commercial thermal infrared constellation, operating under demanding joint constraints: sub-megabyte model footprint, sub-150 ms per-batch TensorRT FP16 inference on an NVIDIA Jetson Xavier NX, and an end-to-end alert pipeline targeting under 10 minutes from satellite overpass to fire event communication. The system operates on uncalibrated mid-wave infrared (MWIR) single-band imagery at 200 m ground sampling distance, where fires frequently appear as sub-pixel or single-pixel thermal anomalies under extreme class imbalance -- challenges not addressed by the contextual thermal-thresholding pipelines (MODIS, VIIRS) that currently dominate operational fire monitoring. We present an empirical study of lightweight dense representation learning for this regime using a proprietary nine-satellite MWIR dataset. We compare dense masked autoencoding (DenseMAE) and a hybrid DenseMAE+EMA (exponential moving average) distillation variant, and evaluate representations via linear probing and full-distribution pixel-level average precision (AP) under extreme class imbalance. DenseMAE pretraining enables compact downstream models on the latency-accuracy Pareto frontier: our fastest SSL-pretrained model achieves 0.640 test AP and 0.69 event-level Fire-F1 with 65.34 ms latency per batch and a 0.52 MB engine, without pruning or compression. The best configuration reaches 0.699 AP and 0.744 Fire-F1 below 1 MB, outperforming a supervised baseline (0.650 AP) under comparable constraints.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出并部署了一套用于在轨野火检测的系统，该系统运行于一个由九颗卫星组成的商业热红外星座上，并在以下严苛的联合约束下工作：模型大小低于1 MB，在NVIDIA Jetson Xavier NX上使用TensorRT FP16推理每批处理时间低于150 ms，以及从卫星过境到火灾事件通信的端到端告警管线目标在10分钟以内。该系统在200米地面采样距离下对未校准的中波红外（MWIR）单波段图像进行处理，在此场景下，火灾经常以亚像素或单像素热异常的形式出现，并伴有极端类别不平衡——这些问题是以往主导业务化火灾监测的上下文热阈值处理管线（MODIS、VIIRS）所无法解决的。我们针对该场景，利用一个专有的九卫星MWIR数据集，对轻量级密集表示学习进行了实证研究。我们比较了密集掩码自编码（DenseMAE）及其与指数移动平均（EMA）蒸馏相结合的混合变体，并通过线性探测和极端类别不平衡下的全分布像素级平均精度（AP）来评估表示质量。DenseMAE预训练使得紧凑型下游模型能够位于延迟-精度Pareto前沿上：我们最快的自监督预训练模型在每批处理延迟为65.34 ms、引擎大小为0.52 MB（无需剪枝或压缩）的条件下，达到了0.640的测试AP和0.69的事件级Fire-F1。最佳配置在低于1 MB的模型下达到了0.699的AP和0.744的Fire-F1，优于在可比约束下的监督基线（0.650 AP）。

</details>


### [68] [Eulerian Motion Guidance: Robust Image Animation via Bidirectional Geometric Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06280)
*Thong Nguyen,Khoi M. Le,Cong-Duy Nguyen,Luu Anh Tuan,See-Kiong Ng,Chunyan Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于相邻帧欧拉运动场的图像动画方法，通过双向几何一致性机制遮挡处理，实现了并行化训练、有界误差监督和更优的时间一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的可控图像动画框架通常依赖拉格朗日运动引导（基于初始帧的光流估计），存在训练难以并行化、误差累积无界等问题。本文旨在通过更局部的监督设计来解决这些局限。

Method: 方法包含两个核心：1）使用相邻帧欧拉运动场替代初始帧拉格朗日光流，实现并行化训练和有界误差监督；2）引入双向几何一致性机制，通过前向-后向循环校验数学识别并遮挡被遮蔽区域，防止模型学习错误的扭曲目标。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法相比基于参考帧的基线方法，加快了训练速度，保持了时间一致性，并减少了动态伪影。

Conclusion: 通过局部欧拉运动监督和双向几何一致性约束，可以有效提升扩散模型在图像动画任务中的训练效率和生成质量。

Original Abstract: Recent advancements in image animation have utilized diffusion models to breathe life into static images. However, existing controllable frameworks typically rely on Lagrangian motion guidance, where optical flow is estimated relative to the initial frame. This paper revisits the same optical-flow primitive through a more local supervision design: we use adjacent-frame Eulerian motion fields to guide generation, where the motion signal always describes a short temporal hop. This shift enables parallelized training and provides bounded-error supervision throughout the generation process. To mitigate the drift artifacts common in adjacent frame generation, we introduce a Bidirectional Geometric Consistency mechanism, which computes a forward-backward cycle check to mathematically identify and mask occluded regions, preventing the model from learning incorrect warping objectives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach accelerates training, preserves temporal coherence, and reduces dynamic artifacts compared to reference-based baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，图像动画领域利用扩散模型为静态图像注入活力取得了显著进展。然而，现有的可控框架通常依赖于拉格朗日运动引导，即光流相对于初始帧进行估计。本文通过一种更局部的监督设计重新审视了相同的光流基元：我们使用相邻帧的欧拉运动场来指导生成，其中运动信号始终描述一个短时间跳步。这一转变实现了并行化训练，并在整个生成过程中提供了有界误差监督。为了缓解相邻帧生成中常见的漂移伪影问题，我们引入了一种双向几何一致性机制，该机制通过计算前向-后向循环校验来数学识别并遮蔽被遮挡区域，防止模型学习错误的扭曲目标。大量实验表明，与基于参考帧的基线方法相比，我们的方法加速了训练，保持了时间一致性，并减少了动态伪影。

</details>


### [69] [Render, Don't Decode: Weight-Space World Models with Latent Structural Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06298)
*Roussel Desmond Nzoyem,Mauro Comi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NOVA提出了一种基于坐标隐式神经表示（INR）的世界建模框架，将系统状态表示为INR的权重和偏置，通过解析渲染消除解码器瓶颈，实现紧凑、可移植、零样本超分辨率，并能解耦场景中的背景、前景和帧间运动，支持可控视频生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有世界模型将原始像素编码到不透明的潜空间并依赖重型解码器进行重建，导致计算成本高昂且缺乏可解释性。本文旨在解决这一瓶颈，提出一种更紧凑、可解释且高效的世界建模方法。

Method: 提出NOVA框架，使用辅助的基于坐标的隐式神经表示（INR）来表示系统状态（即INR的权重和偏置）。该结构化表示通过解析渲染方式生成图像，消除了传统解码器瓶颈。通过动作匹配目标，NOVA可被蒸馏为上下文相关的视频生成器，并实现场景组分的解耦（背景、前景、帧间运动）。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的数据集上验证，NOVA在单个消费级GPU上（约4000万参数）实现了强可控预测能力；无需辅助损失或对抗目标即可解耦结构场景组件；支持零样本超分辨率。

Conclusion: 像INR这样的结构化表示不仅增强了对潜在动态的理解，还为沉浸式和可定制的虚拟体验铺平了道路，代表了世界建模的一种新范式。

Original Abstract: Training world models on vast quantities of unlabelled videos is a critical step toward fully autonomous intelligence. However, the prevailing paradigm of encoding raw pixels into opaque latent spaces and relying on heavy decoders for reconstruction leaves these models computationally expensive and uninterpretable. We address this problem by introducing NOVA, a world modelling framework that represents the system state as the weights and biases of an auxiliary coordinate-based implicit neural representation (INR). This structured representation is analytically rendered, which eliminates the decoder bottleneck while conferring compactness, portability, and zero-shot super-resolution. Furthermore, like most latent action models, NOVA can be distilled into a context-dependent video generator via an action-matching objective. Surprisingly, without resorting to auxiliary losses or adversarial objectives, NOVA can disentangle structural scene components such as background, foreground, and inter-frame motion, enabling users to edit either content or dynamics without compromising the other. We validate our framework on several challenging datasets, achieving strong controllable forecasting while operating on a single consumer GPU at $\sim$40M parameters. Ultimately, structured representations like INRs not only enhance our understanding of latent dynamics but also pave the way for immersive and customisable virtual experiences.

中文摘要翻译: 在大量无标注视频上训练世界模型是实现完全自主智能的关键一步。然而，将原始像素编码到不透明潜空间并依赖重型解码器进行重建的主流范式使得这些模型计算成本高昂且缺乏可解释性。我们通过引入NOVA来解决这一问题，这是一种世界建模框架，它将系统状态表示为辅助的基于坐标的隐式神经表示（INR）的权重和偏置。这种结构化表示通过解析渲染生成图像，消除了解码器瓶颈，同时具备紧凑性、可移植性和零样本超分辨率能力。此外，与大多数潜在动作模型类似，NOVA可以通过动作匹配目标蒸馏为上下文相关的视频生成器。令人惊讶的是，在无需辅助损失或对抗目标的情况下，NOVA能够解耦结构化的场景组件，如背景、前景和帧间运动，使用户能够编辑内容或动态而不影响另一方。我们在多个具有挑战性的数据集上验证了该框架，在单个消费级GPU上以约4000万参数实现了强大的可控预测。最终，像INR这样的结构化表示不仅增强了我们对潜在动态的理解，也为沉浸式和可定制的虚拟体验铺平了道路。

</details>


### [70] [NavOne: One-Step Global Planning for Vision-Language Navigation on Top-Down Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06317)
*Dijia Zhan,Jinyi Li,Chenxi Zheng,Shaoyu Huang,Yong Li,Jie Tang,Xuemiao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TD-VLN范式，将视觉语言导航重构为基于预建俯视地图的单步全局路径规划问题，并构建R2R-TopDown数据集，提出NavOne框架通过端到端前向传播直接预测稠密路径概率，在规划速度上比现有地图方法提升8倍，比自我中心方法提升80倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLN方法采用自我中心逐步范式，存在误差累积和效率低下问题；而基于地图的方法依赖增量更新的记忆图或离散路径评分，限制了连续空间推理并产生离散瓶颈。

Method: 提出NavOne统一框架，包含俯视地图融合器（Top-Down Map Fuser）实现多模态地图的联合表示，并扩展注意力残差（Attention Residuals）实现空间感知的深度混合，以端到端单次前向传播直接预测稠密路径概率。

Result: 在R2R-TopDown数据集上，NavOne在基于地图的VLN方法中达到最优性能，规划阶段速度相比现有地图基线提升8倍，相比自我中心方法提升80倍。

Conclusion: 将VLN重构为基于俯视地图的全局路径规划问题，能够有效避免误差累积，大幅提升导航效率和空间推理能力。

Original Abstract: Existing Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) methods typically adopt an egocentric, step-by-step paradigm, which struggles with error accumulation and limits efficiency. While recent approaches attempt to leverage pre-built environment maps, they often rely on incrementally updating memory graphs or scoring discrete path proposals, which restricts continuous spatial reasoning and creates discrete bottlenecks. We propose Top-Down VLN (TD-VLN), reformulating navigation as a one-step global path planning problem on pre-built top-down maps, supported by our newly constructed R2R-TopDown dataset. To solve this, we introduce NavOne, a unified framework that directly predicts dense path probabilities over multi-modal maps in a single end-to-end forward pass. NavOne features a Top-Down Map Fuser for joint multi-modal map representation, and extends Attention Residuals for spatial-aware depth mixing. Extensive experiments on R2R-TopDown show that NavOne achieves state-of-the-art performance among map-based VLN methods, with a planning-stage speedup of 8x over existing map-based baselines and 80x over egocentric methods, enabling highly efficient global navigation.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的视觉语言导航（VLN）方法通常采用自我中心的逐步骤范式，难以应对误差累积问题且效率受限。尽管近期方法尝试利用预建的环境地图，但它们往往依赖于增量更新的记忆图或对离散路径提案进行评分，这限制了连续空间推理并造成了离散瓶颈。本文提出Top-Down VLN（TD-VLN），将导航重构为在预建俯视地图上的单步全局路径规划问题，并以此为基础构建了新的R2R-TopDown数据集。为解决该问题，我们引入NavOne——一个统一的框架，能够通过单次端到端前向传播直接在多模态地图上预测稠密路径概率。NavOne配备了俯视地图融合器（Top-Down Map Fuser）用于联合多模态地图表示，并扩展了注意力残差（Attention Residuals）以实现空间感知的深度混合。在R2R-TopDown上的大量实验表明，NavOne在基于地图的VLN方法中达到了最先进的性能，规划阶段速度相比现有地图基线提升8倍，相比自我中心方法提升80倍，实现了高效的全局导航。

</details>


### [71] [Earth-o1: A Grid-free Observation-native Atmospheric World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06337)
*Junchao Gong,Kaiyi Xu,Wangxu Wei,Siwei Tu,Jingyi Xu,Zili Liu,Hang Fan,Zhiwang Zhou,Tao Han,Yi Xiao,Xinyu Gu,Zhangrui Li,Wenlong Zhang,Hao Chen,Xiaokang Yang,Yaqiang Wang,Lijing Cheng,Pierre Gentine,Wanli Ouyang,Feng Zhang,Zhe-Min Tan,Bowen Zhou,Fenghua Ling,Ben Fei,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Earth-o1 是一种无需网格化预处理、直接从非网格观测数据学习地球系统三维物理演化的连续大气世界模型，在表面预报技能上达到与 IFS 相当的水平，为数据驱动的数字孪生提供了新范式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统建模框架将异构观测数据强制映射到预定空间网格，既限制了对原始传感器数据的充分利用，也造成了严重的计算瓶颈，亟需一种能够直接处理非网格观测数据的大气建模新方法。

Method: Earth-o1 摒弃了传统大气动力学建模和数据同化，直接从非网格观测数据学习地球系统连续的三维物理演化，将多种传感器输入整合为统一的、无网格的动力学场，自主推进大气状态在时间和空间上的演化。

Result: 在后验评估中，Earth-o1 在地表预报技能上达到了与业务化综合预报系统（IFS）相当的水平。

Conclusion: 连续、观测驱动的世界模型——一类全新的完全观测原生的地球物理模拟器——能够达到与已建立的物理框架相当的保真度，为地球数字孪生提供了可扩展的数据驱动基础。

Original Abstract: Despite the unprecedented volume of multimodal data provided by modern Earth observation systems, our ability to model atmospheric dynamics remains constrained. Traditional modeling frameworks force heterogeneous measurements into predefined spatial grids, inherently limiting the full exploitation of raw sensor data and creating severe computational bottlenecks. Here we present Earth-o1, an observation-native atmospheric world model that overcomes these structural limitations. Rather than relying on conventional atmospheric dynamical modeling systems or traditional data assimilation, Earth-o1 directly learns the continuous, three-dimensional physical evolution of the Earth system from ungridded observational data. By integrating diverse sensor inputs into a unified, grid-free dynamical field, the model autonomously advances the atmospheric state in space and time. We show that this fundamentally distinct paradigm enables direct, real-time forecasting and cross-sensor inference without the overhead of explicit numerical solvers. In hindcast evaluations, Earth-o1 achieves surface forecast skill comparable to the operational Integrated Forecasting System (IFS). These results establish that continuous, observation-driven world models -- a new class of fully observation-native geophysical simulators -- can match the fidelity of established physical frameworks, providing a scalable data-driven foundation for a digital twin of the Earth.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管现代地球观测系统提供了前所未有规模的多模态数据，但我们模拟大气动力学的能力仍然受限。传统的建模框架将异构测量数据强制映射到预定义的空间网格上，本质上限制了对原始传感器数据的充分利用，并造成了严重的计算瓶颈。本文提出了 Earth-o1，一种观测原生的大气世界模型，克服了这些结构性限制。该模型不依赖于传统的天气动力学建模系统或数据同化方法，而是直接从非网格观测数据学习地球系统连续的三维物理演化。通过将多种传感器输入整合到统一的、无网格的动力学场中，该模型自主推进大气状态在时空上的演化。我们表明，这种根本不同的范式能够实现直接、实时的预报和跨传感器推理，而无需显式数值求解器的开销。在后验评估中，Earth-o1 在地表预报技能上达到了与业务化综合预报系统（IFS）相当的水平。这些结果证明了连续、观测驱动的世界模型——一类全新的完全观测原生的地球物理模拟器——能够达到与已建立的物理框架相当的保真度，为地球数字孪生提供了可扩展的数据驱动基础。

</details>


### [72] [SwiftI2V: Efficient High-Resolution Image-to-Video Generation via Conditional Segment-wise Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06356)
*YaoYang Liu,Yuechen Zhang,Wenbo Li,Yufei Zhao,Rui Liu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SwiftI2V提出了一种高效的两阶段高分辨率图生视频框架，通过先低分辨率生成运动参考再强图像条件化的2K合成，在VBench-I2V上达到与端到端方法相当的性能，同时GPU总时间减少202倍，支持单卡（如H800或RTX 4090）运行2K I2V生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有高分辨率（2K）图生视频方法面临两难困境：端到端模型内存和延迟成本过高；级联方案中通用视频超分阶段未显式以输入图像为条件，容易产生幻觉细节并偏离输入特定的局部结构。亟需一种兼顾效率与保真度的高分辨率I2V框架。

Method: SwiftI2V采用两阶段设计：1）首先生成低分辨率运动参考，降低token成本与建模负担；2）在运动引导下进行强图像条件化的2K合成，恢复忠实于输入的细节。核心创新包括条件分段生成（CSG），以有界的每步token预算逐段合成视频，并在每段内采用双向上下文交互提升跨段连贯性与输入保真度。

Result: 在VBench-I2V的2K分辨率基准上，SwiftI2V取得与端到端基线相当的性能，同时GPU总时间减少202倍；支持在单块数据中心GPU（如H800）或消费级GPU（如RTX 4090）上实现实际的2K I2V生成。

Conclusion: SwiftI2V通过两阶段设计与条件分段生成机制，有效解决了高分辨率图生视频中效率与保真度的矛盾，实现了在有限硬件资源下高质量2K视频生成，为实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: High-resolution image-to-video (I2V) generation aims to synthesize realistic temporal dynamics while preserving fine-grained appearance details of the input image. At 2K resolution, it becomes extremely challenging, and existing solutions suffer from various weaknesses: 1) end-to-end models are often prohibitively expensive in memory and latency; 2) cascading low-resolution generation with a generic video super-resolution tends to hallucinate details and drift from input-specific local structures, since the super-resolution stage is not explicitly conditioned on the input image. To this end, we propose SwiftI2V, an efficient framework tailored for high-resolution I2V. Following the widely used two-stage design, it addresses the efficiency--fidelity dilemma by first generating a low-resolution motion reference to reduce token costs and ease the modeling burden, then performing a strongly image-conditioned 2K synthesis guided by the motion to recover input-faithful details with controlled overhead. Specifically, to make generation more scalable, SwiftI2V introduces Conditional Segment-wise Generation (CSG) to synthesize videos segment-by-segment with a bounded per-step token budget, and adopts bidirectional contextual interaction within each segment to improve cross-segment coherence and input fidelity. On VBench-I2V at 2K resolution, SwiftI2V achieves performance comparable to end-to-end baselines while reducing total GPU-time by 202x. Particularly, it enables practical 2K I2V generation on a single datacenter GPU (e.g., H800) or consumer GPU (e.g., RTX 4090).

中文摘要翻译: 高分辨率图像到视频（I2V）生成旨在合成逼真的时间动态，同时保留输入图像的细粒度外观细节。在2K分辨率下，这一问题极具挑战性，现有解决方案存在多种不足：1）端到端模型通常在内存和延迟方面代价过高；2）将低分辨率生成与通用视频超分进行级联的方法倾向于产生幻觉细节并偏离输入特定的局部结构，因为超分阶段并未显式以输入图像为条件。为此，我们提出SwiftI2V，一个专为高分辨率I2V设计的高效框架。它沿用了广泛使用的两阶段设计，通过首先生成低分辨率运动参考以降低token成本并简化建模负担，然后在运动引导下执行强图像条件化的2K合成，从而以可控开销恢复忠实于输入的细节，解决了效率与保真度之间的困境。具体而言，为让生成更具可扩展性，SwiftI2V引入了条件分段生成（Conditional Segment-wise Generation, CSG），以有界的每步token预算逐段合成视频，并在每个分段内采用双向上下文交互来提升跨段连贯性和输入保真度。在VBench-I2V的2K分辨率基准上，SwiftI2V取得了与端到端基线相当的性能，同时GPU总时间减少了202倍。尤其重要的是，它使得在单块数据中心GPU（如H800）或消费级GPU（如RTX 4090）上实现实际的2K I2V生成成为可能。

</details>


### [73] [eXplaining to Learn (eX2L): Regularization Using Contrastive Visual Explanation Pairs for Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06368)
*Paulo Mario P. Medina,Jose Marie Antonio Miñoza,Sebastian C. Ibañez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出eX2L框架，通过惩罚主分类器与混淆分类器的Grad-CAM激活图相似性来解耦混杂特征，在Spawrious基准上超越当前SOTA，分别提升AA 5.49%和WGA 10.90%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有分布偏移缓解算法性能不一致，常不敌基线ERM，且高算法复杂度限制了可解释性，只能间接处理虚假相关性。

Method: 提出eX2L框架，在训练过程中通过惩罚主标签分类器与同步训练的混淆分类器生成的Grad-CAM激活图之间的相似性，从分类器的潜在表示中解耦混杂特征。

Result: 在Spawrious Many-to-Many Hard Challenge基准上，eX2L的平均准确率（AA）达82.24%±3.87%，最差组准确率（WGA）达66.31%±8.73%，分别超越当前SOTA 5.49%和10.90%。

Conclusion: eX2L表明，通过在组级别显式解耦标签属性和干扰属性，可以实现函数域不变性，且具备竞争性性能和可解释性。

Original Abstract: Despite extensive research into mitigating distribution shifts, many existing algorithms yield inconsistent performance, often failing to outperform baseline Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) across diverse scenarios. Furthermore, high algorithmic complexity frequently limits interpretability and offers only an indirect means of addressing spurious correlations. We propose eXplaining to Learn (eX2L): an interpretable, explanation-based framework that decorrelates confounding features from a classifier's latent representations during training. eX2L achieves this by penalizing the similarity between Grad-CAM activation maps generated by a primary label classifier and those from a concurrently trained confounder classifier. On the rigorous Spawrious Many-to-Many Hard Challenge benchmark, eX2L achieves an average accuracy (AA) of 82.24% +/- 3.87% and a worst-group accuracy (WGA) of 66.31% +/- 8.73%, outperforming the current state-of-the-art (SOTA) by 5.49% and 10.90%, respectively. Beyond its competitive performance, eX2L demonstrates that functional domain invariance can be achieved by explicitly decoupling label and nuisance attributes at the group level.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管在缓解分布偏移方面已有大量研究，但许多现有算法表现不一致，通常在各种场景下无法超越基线经验风险最小化（ERM）。此外，高算法复杂性常常限制了可解释性，并且仅能间接处理虚假相关性。我们提出eX2L（eXplaining to Learn）：一个可解释的、基于解释的框架，在训练过程中从分类器的潜在表示中解耦混杂特征。eX2L通过惩罚主标签分类器生成的Grad-CAM激活图与同步训练的混淆分类器生成的Grad-CAM激活图之间的相似性来实现这一目标。在严格的Spawrious Many-to-Many Hard Challenge基准上，eX2L实现了82.24%±3.87%的平均准确率（AA）和66.31%±8.73%的最差组准确率（WGA），分别超越当前最先进方法（SOTA）5.49%和10.90%。除了其具有竞争力的性能之外，eX2L还表明，通过在组级别显式解耦标签属性和干扰属性，可以实现函数域不变性。

</details>


### [74] [Continuous-Time Distribution Matching for Few-Step Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06376)
*Tao Liu,Hao Yan,Mengting Chen,Taihang Hu,Zhengrong Yue,Zihao Pan,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Ming-Ming Cheng,Bo Zheng,Yaxing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出连续时间分布匹配（CDM），将DMD框架从离散锚点扩展到连续优化，通过动态连续时间调度和连续时间对齐目标，无需复杂辅助模块即可实现少步图像生成的高保真度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）依赖离散时间步的稀疏监督，其逆KL散度的模式寻求特性导致视觉伪影和过度平滑输出，需要GAN或奖励模型等复杂辅助模块来恢复视觉保真度。

Method: CDM包含两个关键设计：(1) 用随机长度的动态连续调度替代固定离散调度，在采样轨迹的任意点施加分布匹配而非固定锚点；(2) 提出连续时间对齐目标，在学生速度场外推的潜变量上执行主动离轨匹配，提升泛化能力并保留细粒度视觉细节。

Result: 在SD3-Medium和Longcat-Image等不同架构上的大量实验表明，CDM在无需复杂辅助目标的情况下，为少步图像生成提供了极具竞争力的视觉保真度。

Conclusion: CDM通过连续时间优化有效克服了离散DMD的局限性，在保持简洁框架的同时实现了高质量的少步图像生成。

Original Abstract: Step distillation has become a leading technique for accelerating diffusion models, among which Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) and Consistency Distillation are two representative paradigms. While consistency methods enforce self-consistency along the full PF-ODE trajectory to steer it toward the clean data manifold, vanilla DMD relies on sparse supervision at a few predefined discrete timesteps. This restricted discrete-time formulation and mode-seeking nature of the reverse KL divergence tends to exhibit visual artifacts and over-smoothed outputs, often necessitating complex auxiliary modules -- such as GANs or reward models -- to restore visual fidelity. In this work, we introduce Continuous-Time Distribution Matching (CDM), migrating the DMD framework from discrete anchoring to continuous optimization for the first time. CDM achieves this through two continuous-time designs. First, we replace the fixed discrete schedule with a dynamic continuous schedule of random length, so that distribution matching is enforced at arbitrary points along sampling trajectories rather than only at a few fixed anchors. Second, we propose a continuous-time alignment objective that performs active off-trajectory matching on latents extrapolated via the student's velocity field, improving generalization and preserving fine visual details. Extensive experiments on different architectures, including SD3-Medium and Longcat-Image, demonstrate that CDM provides highly competitive visual fidelity for few-step image generation without relying on complex auxiliary objectives. Code is available at https://github.com/byliutao/cdm.

中文摘要翻译: 步骤蒸馏已成为加速扩散模型的前沿技术，其中分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）和一致性蒸馏是两种代表性范式。一致性方法沿完整PF-ODE轨迹施加自一致性以引导其向干净数据流形收敛，而原始DMD仅依赖少数预定义离散时间步的稀疏监督。这种受限的离散时间公式和逆KL散度的模式寻求特性往往导致视觉伪影和过度平滑输出，通常需要复杂的辅助模块——如GAN或奖励模型——来恢复视觉保真度。本文提出连续时间分布匹配（CDM），首次将DMD框架从离散锚点迁移至连续优化。CDM通过两个连续时间设计实现这一点。首先，我们将固定的离散调度替换为随机长度的动态连续调度，使得分布匹配在采样轨迹的任意点而非仅在少数固定锚点处被强制执行。其次，我们提出一个连续时间对齐目标，在学生速度场外推的潜变量上执行主动离轨匹配，从而改善泛化能力并保留精细视觉细节。在包括SD3-Medium和Longcat-Image在内的不同架构上的大量实验表明，CDM在无需依赖复杂辅助目标的情况下，为少步图像生成提供了极具竞争力的视觉保真度。代码已开源：https://github.com/byliutao/cdm。

</details>


### [75] [GeoStack: A Framework for Quasi-Abelian Knowledge Composition in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06477)
*Pranav Mantini,Shishir K. Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoStack 提出一种几何堆叠模块化框架，通过约束适配器流形实现多领域专家的无损组合，在保持基础模型知识的同时实现恒定时间推理复杂度 O(1)，有效缓解灾难性遗忘。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在跨多个领域或任务积累知识时面临灾难性遗忘问题，缺乏高效的模块化知识组合机制。

Method: 提出 GeoStack 框架，对适配器流形施加几何与结构约束，确保基础模型知识得以保留；通过数学推导证明了权重折叠性质，使得集成任意数量专家后推理复杂度仍为 O(1)。

Result: 在多领域适应和类增量学习实验中，GeoStack 提供了长期知识组合的高效机制，显著缓解了灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: GeoStack 为 VLM 中的知识组合提供了一种高效的模块化方案，在保证推理效率的同时有效缓解了灾难性遗忘。

Original Abstract: We address the challenge of knowledge composition in Vision-Language Models (VLMs), where accumulating expertise across multiple domains or tasks typically leads to catastrophic forgetting. We introduce GeoStack (Geometric Stacking), a modular framework that allows independently trained domain experts to be composed into a unified model. By imposing geometric and structural constraints on the adapter manifold, GeoStack ensures the foundational knowledge of the base model is preserved. Furthermore, we mathematically demonstrate a weight-folding property that achieves constant-time inference complexity ($O(1)$), regardless of the number of integrated experts. Experimental results across multi-domain adaptation and class-incremental learning show that GeoStack provides an efficient mechanism for long-term knowledge composition while significantly mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Code is available at https://github.com/QuantitativeImagingLaboratory/GeoStack.

中文摘要翻译: 我们解决了视觉语言模型中知识组合的挑战，即在多个领域或任务中积累专业知识通常会导致灾难性遗忘。我们提出 GeoStack（几何堆叠），一种模块化框架，允许将独立训练的领域专家组合成统一模型。通过对适配器流形施加几何和结构约束，GeoStack 确保了基础模型的基础知识得以保留。此外，我们从数学上证明了一种权重折叠性质，实现了与集成专家数量无关的恒定时间推理复杂度 O(1)。跨多领域适应和类增量学习的实验结果表明，GeoStack 为长期知识组合提供了一种高效机制，同时显著缓解了灾难性遗忘。代码已开源。

</details>


### [76] [3D MRI Image Pretraining via Controllable 2D Slice Navigation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06487)
*Yu Wang,Qingchao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种将3D MRI体素转换为可控2D渲染序列的动作条件预训练方法，作为传统静态预训练的有效补充。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的自监督预训练目标大多将每个扫描看作切片、补丁或体积的静态聚合，缺乏利用MRI扫描中固有动态导航信号的方式。

Method: 将3D体素通过在不同连续位置、方向和尺度渲染切片转换为可控视频-动作序列，使用tokenizer编码切片观测，并训练潜在动力学模型预测潜在特征的演化。

Result: 在多个解剖和空间下游任务上，该方法优于标准的静态体积基线、仅tokenizer预训练以及无对齐动作的动力学变体。

Conclusion: 可控MRI切片导航为从大规模未标注MRI集合中学习解剖和空间表示提供了一种有用的互补预训练接口。

Original Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining has become the mainstream approach for learning MRI representations from unlabeled scans. However, most existing objectives still treat each scan primarily as static aggregations of slices, patches or volumes. We ask whether there exists an intrinsic form of self-supervision signal that is different from reconstructing the masked patches, through transforming the 3D volumes into controllable 2D rendered sequences: by rendering slices at continuous positions, orientations, and scales, a 3D volume can be converted into dense video-action sequences whose controls are the action trajectories. We study this formulation with an action-conditioned pretraining objective, where a tokenizer encodes slice observations and a latent dynamics model predicts the evolution of latent features. Across representative anatomical and spatial downstream tasks, the proposed pretraining is evaluated against standard static-volume baselines, tokenizer-only pretraining, and dynamics variants without aligned actions. These results suggest that controllable MRI slice navigation provides a useful complementary pretraining interface for learning anatomical and spatial representations from large unlabeled MRI collections.

中文摘要翻译: 自监督预训练已成为从无标注扫描中学习MRI表示的主流方法。然而，现有的大多数预训练目标仍将每个扫描主要视为切片、补丁或体积的静态聚合。我们提出一个问题：是否存在一种内在形式的自监督信号，不同于通过将3D体素转换为可控的2D渲染序列来重建掩蔽补丁——通过在连续位置、方向和尺度上渲染切片，一个3D体素可以被转换为密集的视频-动作序列，其控制信号是动作轨迹。我们使用一种动作条件的预训练目标来研究这种公式化方法，其中tokenizer编码切片观测，潜在动力学模型预测潜在特征的演化。在多个代表性的解剖和空间下游任务中，所提出的预训练方法相较于标准的静态体积基线、仅tokenizer预训练以及无对齐动作的动力学变体进行了评估。这些结果表明，可控MRI切片导航为从大规模未标注MRI集合中学习解剖和空间表示提供了一种有用的互补预训练接口。

</details>


### [77] [MARBLE: Multi-Aspect Reward Balance for Diffusion RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06507)
*Canyu Zhao,Hao Chen,Yunze Tong,Yu Qiao,Jiacheng Li,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MARBLE框架，通过梯度空间优化解决扩散模型多奖励微调中加权求和导致的样本级不匹配问题，在SD3.5 Medium上同时提升五个奖励维度，训练速度达到基线0.97X。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型多奖励微调方法（如每奖励训练专家模型、加权求和奖励、分阶段微调）存在以下问题：无法产生统一模型同时对多奖励联合训练，或需要大量手动调参的序贯训练。其根本原因是朴素加权求和奖励聚合存在样本级不匹配——大部分采样轨迹是特定维度的专家样本，对某些奖励维度信息丰富但对其他维度无关，加权求和稀释了其监督信号。

Method: 提出MARBLE框架：(1) 为每个奖励维护独立的优势估计器，计算每个奖励的策略梯度；(2) 通过求解二次规划问题，将多个梯度协调为单一更新方向，无需手动调参的奖励加权；(3) 提出摊销公式利用DiffusionNFT损失的仿射结构，将每步成本从K+1次反向传播降至接近单奖励基线；(4) 引入EMA平滑稳定平衡系数。

Result: 在SD3.5 Medium上使用五个奖励进行实验，MARBLE同时提升了所有五个奖励维度；将最弱对齐奖励的梯度余弦从加权求和下80%小批量为负值转为持续正值；训练速度为基线训练速度的0.97X。

Conclusion: 证明了加权求和奖励聚合在多奖励微调中的根本缺陷，并提出了一种高效、无需手动调参的梯度协调框架MARBLE，能够在几乎不增加训练成本的情况下同时优化多个奖励维度。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning fine-tuning has become the dominant approach for aligning diffusion models with human preferences. However, assessing images is intrinsically a multi-dimensional task, and multiple evaluation criteria need to be optimized simultaneously. Existing practice deal with multiple rewards by training one specialist model per reward, optimizing a weighted-sum reward $R(x)=\sum_k w_k R_k(x)$, or sequentially fine-tuning with a hand-crafted stage schedule. These approaches either fail to produce a unified model that can be jointly trained on all rewards or necessitates heavy manually tuned sequential training. We find that the failure stems from using a naive weighted-sum reward aggregation. This approach suffers from a sample-level mismatch because most rollouts are specialist samples, highly informative for certain reward dimensions but irrelevant for others; consequently, weighted summation dilutes their supervision. To address this issue, we propose MARBLE (Multi-Aspect Reward BaLancE), a gradient-space optimization framework that maintains independent advantage estimators for each reward, computes per-reward policy gradients, and harmonizes them into a single update direction without manually-tuned reward weighting, by solving a Quadratic Programming problem. We further propose an amortized formulation that exploits the affine structure of the loss used in DiffusionNFT, to reduce the per-step cost from K+1 backward passes to near single-reward baseline cost, together with EMA smoothing on the balancing coefficients to stabilize updates against transient single-batch fluctuations. On SD3.5 Medium with five rewards, MARBLE improves all five reward dimensions simultaneously, turns the worst-aligned reward's gradient cosine from negative under weighted summation in 80% of mini-batches to consistently positive, and runs at 0.97X the training speed of baseline training.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习微调已成为将扩散模型与人类偏好对齐的主流方法。然而，图像评估本质上是一项多维度任务，需要同时优化多个评价标准。现有方法通过为每个奖励训练一个专家模型、优化加权求和奖励 $R(x)=\sum_k w_k R_k(x)$，或使用手工设计的阶段调度进行序贯微调来处理多奖励问题。这些方法要么不能产生一个可以在所有奖励上联合训练的统一模型，要么需要大量手动调参的序贯训练。我们发现其失败源于使用朴素的加权求和奖励聚合。该方法存在样本级不匹配问题，因为大多数采样轨迹是专家样本，对某些奖励维度信息丰富但对其他维度无关；因此加权求和稀释了其监督信号。为解决此问题，我们提出MARBLE（多方面奖励平衡），一个梯度空间优化框架，它为每个奖励维护独立的优势估计器，计算每个奖励的策略梯度，并通过求解二次规划问题将它们协调为单一更新方向，无需手动调参的奖励加权。我们进一步提出一种摊销公式，利用DiffusionNFT中所用损失的仿射结构，将每步成本从K+1次反向传播降低到接近单奖励基线的成本，同时使用EMA平滑平衡系数以稳定更新，防止单批次瞬态波动。在SD3.5 Medium上使用五个奖励进行实验，MARBLE同时改进了所有五个奖励维度，将最弱对齐奖励的梯度余弦从加权求和中80%小批量为负值转变为持续正值，且训练速度达到基线训练的0.97倍。

</details>


### [78] [FreeSpec: Training-Free Long Video Generation via Singular-Spectrum Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06509)
*Fangda Chen,Shanshan Zhao,Longrong Yang,Chuanfu Xu,Zhigang Luo,Long Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeSpec提出了一种无需训练的频谱重构框架，通过奇异值分解将全局分支作为低秩频谱引导、局部分支作为高秩重构基础，解决了视频扩散模型在长视频生成中的内容漂移、时序不一致和过度平滑问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法通过全局与局部分支结合来提升时序一致性，但依赖预定义准则分解表观一致性与时序动态，在相机运动和序列运动等表观与动作紧密耦合的场景下不可靠。此外，自注意力窗口增大导致频谱能量集中于少数低秩奇异方向，抑制了高秩空间细节和运动丰富的时序变化。

Method: 提出FreeSpec，一种无需训练的频谱重构框架。利用奇异值分解（SVD）将全局和局部特征分解，以全局分支作为低秩频谱引导，局部分支作为高秩重构基础，实现频谱级融合，避免刚性特征划分。

Result: 在Wan2.1和LTX-Video上的实验表明，FreeSpec提升了长视频生成质量，尤其在时序动态方面表现突出，同时保持了良好的视觉质量和时序一致性。

Conclusion: 从奇异谱角度分析了视频时序扩展问题，证明了自注意力窗口增大导致的频谱集中现象，并提出的FreeSpec框架通过频谱级融合有效兼顾了长程一致性与细节动态保留。

Original Abstract: Video diffusion models perform well in short-video synthesis, but their training-free extension to long videos often suffers from content drift, temporal inconsistency, and over-smoothed dynamics. Existing methods improve temporal consistency by combining a global branch with a local branch, but they often further decompose appearance consistency and temporal dynamics within each branch using predefined criteria. This assignment is unreliable when appearance and action progression are tightly coupled, such as in camera motion and sequential motion. We analyze the video temporal extension issue from a singular-spectrum perspective and show that enlarged self-attention windows induce spectral concentration: spectral energy becomes dominated by a few low-rank singular directions, preserving coarse structure but suppressing high-rank spatial details and motion-rich temporal variations. To mitigate this problem, we propose FreeSpec, a training-free spectral reconstruction framework for long-video generation. FreeSpec decomposes global and local features with singular value decomposition, and uses the global branch as low-rank spectral guidance and the local branch as a high-rank reconstruction basis. This spectrum-level fusion avoids the rigid feature partitioning of previous decomposition rules, preserving long-range consistency while better retaining spatial details and temporal dynamics. Experiments on Wan2.1 and LTX-Video demonstrate that FreeSpec improves long-video generation, especially for temporal dynamics, while maintaining strong visual quality and temporal consistency. Project demo: https://fdchen24.github.io/FreeSpec-Website/.

中文摘要翻译: 视频扩散模型在短视频合成中表现良好，但其无需训练地扩展到长视频时，常常面临内容漂移、时序不一致和动态过度平滑的问题。现有方法通过结合全局分支与局部分支来提升时序一致性，但往往依赖预定义准则在每个分支内进一步分解表观一致性和时序动态。当表观与动作进程紧密耦合时，例如相机运动和序列运动，这种分配方式变得不可靠。本文从奇异谱角度分析了视频时序扩展问题，并揭示出自注意力窗口增大会诱发频谱集中现象：频谱能量集中于少数低秩奇异方向，保留了粗粒度结构，但抑制了高秩空间细节和运动丰富的时序变化。为解决这一问题，我们提出FreeSpec——一种用于长视频生成的无需训练的频谱重构框架。FreeSpec利用奇异值分解将全局和局部特征分解，将全局分支作为低秩频谱引导，局部分支作为高秩重构基础。这种频谱级融合避免了先前分解规则中刚性的特征划分，在保持长程一致性的同时，更好地保留了空间细节和时序动态。在Wan2.1和LTX-Video上的实验表明，FreeSpec提升了长视频生成质量，尤其是时序动态方面，同时维持了较强的视觉质量和时序一致性。项目演示：https://fdchen24.github.io/FreeSpec-Website/。

</details>


### [79] [DCR: Counterfactual Attractor Guidance for Rare Compositional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06512)
*Taewon Kang,Matthias Zwicker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Default Completion Repulsion (DCR)框架，针对扩散模型在生成稀有但合理的组合（如雪地海滩或夜间彩虹）时出现的默认完成偏差（默认完成偏差）问题，通过构建反事实吸引子并设计基于投影的排斥机制，在无训练的条件下抑制模型偏向高频语义配置的倾向，提升罕见组合的生成保真度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩散模型在生成视觉内容时，对于训练数据中有效但低频的组合（如雪地海滩、夜间彩虹）会出现生成崩溃，倾向于生成更常见的语义配置。现有引导机制未能显式建模这种竞争性倾向，无法有效防止生成崩塌。

Method: DCR框架通过以下步骤实现：（1）放松罕见组合因子同时保留周围语义，构建反事实吸引子以产生反映模型偏好完成的反事实去噪轨迹；（2）定义目标轨迹与吸引子轨迹之间的差异为反事实漂移（counterfactual drift）；（3）提出基于投影的排斥机制，去除与该漂移方向对齐的引导分量，从而抑制不期望的频繁完成，同时保留其他语义分量。

Result: 在稀有组合提示的实验上，DCR在保持视觉质量的同时提升了组合保真度；分析表明该框架能够揭示并抵消模型的内在偏差。

Conclusion: DCR作为一种无需训练、无需修改架构的框架，为可控生成提供了超越显式约束执行的新视角，有效缓解了扩散模型中的默认完成偏差问题。

Original Abstract: Diffusion models generate realistic visual content, yet often fail to produce rare but plausible compositions. When prompted with combinations that are valid but underrepresented in training data, such as a snowy beach or a rainbow at night, the generation process frequently collapses toward more common alternatives. We identify this failure mode as default completion bias, where denoising trajectories are implicitly attracted toward high-frequency semantic configurations. Existing guidance mechanisms do not explicitly model this competing tendency and therefore struggle to prevent such collapse. We introduce Default Completion Repulsion (DCR), a training-free framework that explicitly models and suppresses default completion behavior. DCR constructs a counterfactual attractor by relaxing the rare compositional factor while preserving surrounding semantics, inducing an alternative denoising trajectory reflecting the model's preferred completion. We define the discrepancy between target and attractor trajectories as a counterfactual drift, and propose a projection-based repulsion mechanism that removes guidance components aligned with this drift direction. This suppresses undesired frequent completions while preserving other semantic components. DCR operates entirely within the standard diffusion sampling process without retraining or architectural modification. Experiments on rare compositional prompts show that DCR improves compositional fidelity while maintaining visual quality. Our analysis further shows that the framework exposes and counteracts intrinsic model biases, offering a new perspective on controllable generation beyond explicit constraint enforcement.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型能够生成逼真的视觉内容，但在面对训练数据中有效但低频的组合（如雪地海滩或夜间彩虹）时，常常无法生成这些罕见但合理的组合。当提示包含这类低频但有效的组合时，生成过程往往会坍缩向更常见的替代方案。我们将这种失败模式识别为默认完成偏差（default completion bias），即去噪轨迹被隐式地吸引向高频语义配置。现有的引导机制未能显式建模这种竞争性倾向，因此难以防止此类崩塌。我们提出默认完成排斥（Default Completion Repulsion, DCR），这是一种无需训练的框架，能够显式地建模并抑制默认完成行为。DCR通过放松罕见组合因子同时保留周围语义来构建一个反事实吸引子，诱导出一条反映模型偏好完成的替代去噪轨迹。我们将目标轨迹与吸引子轨迹之间的差异定义为反事实漂移（counterfactual drift），并提出一种基于投影的排斥机制，去除与该漂移方向对齐的引导分量。这能在抑制不期望的频繁完成的同时保留其他语义分量。DCR完全在标准扩散采样过程中运行，无需重新训练或修改架构。在稀有组合提示上的实验表明，DCR在保持视觉质量的同时提升了组合保真度。我们的进一步分析显示，该框架能够揭示并抵消模型的内在偏差，为超越显式约束执行的可控生成提供了新的视角。

</details>


### [80] [Sparkle: Realizing Lively Instruction-Guided Video Background Replacement via Decoupled Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06535)
*Ziyun Zeng,Yiqi Lin,Guoqiang Liang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对视频背景替换任务缺乏高质量训练数据的问题，提出了解耦式前景/背景引导生成与严格质量过滤的可扩展数据合成管线，构建了包含约14万视频对的Sparkle数据集和最大评估基准Sparkle-Bench，实验表明该数据集及训练模型在现有基准上显著优于所有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前公开视频编辑数据集主要聚焦局部编辑或风格迁移，背景替换这一关键任务因需要合成全新且时序一致的场景、同时保持精确的前后景交互，导致大规模数据生成极具挑战，高质量训练数据稀缺，现有模型（如Kiwi-Edit）表现不佳，开源的OpenVE-3M数据集常产生静态不自然的背景。本文追溯质量退化原因为数据合成过程中缺乏精确的背景引导。

Method: 设计了一种可扩展的数据合成管线，采用解耦方式分别生成前景引导和背景引导，并搭配严格的质量过滤机制。基于该管线构建了Sparkle数据集（约14万视频对，覆盖五种常见背景更换主题）和Sparkle-Bench评估基准。

Result: 在OpenVE-Bench和Sparkle-Bench两个基准上，Sparkle数据集及在其上训练的模型在所有现有基线上取得了显著更优的性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦式前景-背景引导生成和严格质量过滤，可以有效解决背景替换任务中训练数据质量低下的问题，显著提升视频背景替换的性能。数据集、基准和模型均已开源。

Original Abstract: In recent years, open-source efforts like Senorita-2M have propelled video editing toward natural language instruction. However, current publicly available datasets predominantly focus on local editing or style transfer, which largely preserve the original scene structure and are easier to scale. In contrast, Background Replacement, a task central to creative applications such as film production and advertising, requires synthesizing entirely new, temporally consistent scenes while maintaining accurate foreground-background interactions, making large-scale data generation significantly more challenging. Consequently, this complex task remains largely underexplored due to a scarcity of high-quality training data. This gap is evident in poorly performing state-of-the-art models, e.g., Kiwi-Edit, because the primary open-source dataset that contains this task, i.e., OpenVE-3M, frequently produces static, unnatural backgrounds. In this paper, we trace this quality degradation to a lack of precise background guidance during data synthesis. Accordingly, we design a scalable pipeline that generates foreground and background guidance in a decoupled manner with strict quality filtering. Building on this pipeline, we introduce Sparkle, a dataset of ~140K video pairs spanning five common background-change themes, alongside Sparkle-Bench, the largest evaluation benchmark tailored for background replacement to date. Experiments demonstrate that our dataset and the model trained on it achieve substantially better performance than all existing baselines on both OpenVE-Bench and Sparkle-Bench. Our proposed dataset, benchmark, and model are fully open-sourced at https://showlab.github.io/Sparkle/.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，诸如Senorita-2M等开源工作推动了视频编辑向自然语言指令方向发展。然而，当前公开可用的数据集主要聚焦局部编辑或风格迁移，这些任务在很大程度上保留了原始场景结构，因此更容易规模化。相比之下，背景替换——这一在电影制作和广告等创意应用中处于核心地位的任务——需要合成全新的、时序一致的场景，同时保持精确的前后景交互，这使得大规模数据生成极具挑战性。因此，由于高质量训练数据的匮乏，这一复杂任务在很大程度上仍未得到充分探索。这一差距体现在现有最优模型（如Kiwi-Edit）表现不佳上，因为包含该任务的主要开源数据集OpenVE-3M常常产生静态、不自然的背景。在本文中，我们将这种质量退化追溯到数据合成过程中缺乏精确的背景引导。据此，我们设计了一种可扩展的管线，以解耦方式生成前景和背景引导，并搭配严格的质量过滤。基于该管线，我们引入了Sparkle数据集——包含约14万视频对，覆盖五种常见的背景更换主题——以及Sparkle-Bench，这是迄今为止专为背景替换打造的最大评估基准。实验表明，我们的数据集及在其上训练的模型在OpenVE-Bench和Sparkle-Bench两个基准上均显著优于所有现有基线。我们提出的数据集、基准和模型已在https://showlab.github.io/Sparkle/完全开源。

</details>


### [81] [MedHorizon: Towards Long-context Medical Video Understanding in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06537)
*Bodong Du,Bowen Liu,Yang Yu,Xinpeng Ding,Zhiheng Wu,Shuning Wang,Shuo Nie,Naiming Liu,Qifeng Chen,Yangqiu Song,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedHorizon是一个面向长上下文医学视频理解的新基准，包含759小时完整临床手术视频和1253道多选题。当前最优模型准确率仅41.1%，揭示了现有MLLM在稀疏证据检索和多跳临床推理方面的严重不足。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现实临床审查需要完整手术流程视频理解，但现有基准假设证据已通过图像、短片段或预分割视频定位，忽略了“先检索再推理”的问题。医学视频中存在高度冗余的解剖视图，而决定性证据在时间上稀疏、空间上细微且依赖上下文，现有模型难以应对。

Method: 构建MedHorizon基准，收集759小时完整临床手术视频，标注1253道基于证据的多选题，证据帧占比平均仅0.166%。评估通用域、医学域和长视频MLLM模型，并进行详细的瓶颈分析。

Result: 最佳模型准确率仅41.1%；性能不会随帧数增加而可靠提升；证据检索和临床解释是主要瓶颈；瓶颈根源在于弱程序推理和冗余下的注意力漂移；通用采样方法只能部分平衡局部细节与全局覆盖。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM在完整手术流程理解上远未达到鲁棒水平，MedHorizon为评估模型在完整临床工作流中检索稀疏证据并进行推理提供了严格的测试平台。

Original Abstract: Medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced image understanding and short-video analysis, but real clinical review often requires full-procedure video understanding. Unlike general long videos, medical procedures contain highly redundant anatomical views, while decisive evidence is temporally sparse, spatially subtle, and context dependent. Existing benchmarks often assume this evidence has already been localized through images, short clips, or pre-segmented videos, leaving the retrieval-before-reasoning problem under-tested. We introduce MedHorizon, an in-the-wild benchmark for long-context medical video understanding. MedHorizon preserves 759 hours of full-length clinical procedures and provides 1,253 evidence-grounded multiple-choice questionsthat jointly evaluate sparse evidence understanding and multi-hop clinical reasoning. Its evidence is extremely sparse, with only 0.166% evidence frames on average, requiring models to search noisy procedural streams before interpreting and aggregating findings. We evaluate representative general-domain, medical-domain, and long-video MLLMs. The best model reaches only 41.1% accuracy, showing that current systems remain far from robust full-procedure understanding. Further analysis yields four key findings: performance does not scale reliably with more frames, evidence retrieval and clinical interpretation remain primary bottlenecks; these bottlenecks are rooted in weak procedural reasoning and attention drift under redundancy, and generic sampling methods only partially balances local detail with global coverage. MedHorizon provides a rigorous testbed for MLLMs that retrieve sparse evidence and reason over complete clinical workflows.

中文摘要翻译: 医学多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在图像理解和短视频分析方面取得了进展，但真实的临床审查通常需要完整手术流程的视频理解。与通用长视频不同，医学手术流程包含高度冗余的解剖视图，而决定性证据在时间上稀疏、空间上细微且依赖上下文。现有基准通常假设这些证据已通过图像、短片段或预分割视频定位，导致"先检索再推理"的问题未能得到充分测试。我们提出MedHorizon，一个面向长上下文医学视频理解的在野（in-the-wild）基准。MedHorizon保留了759小时的完整临床手术视频，并提供了1253道基于证据的多选题，共同评估稀疏证据理解和多跳临床推理能力。其证据极为稀疏，平均仅有0.166%的证据帧，要求模型在噪声过程流中进行搜索，然后解释和聚合发现。我们评估了具有代表性的通用域、医学域和长视频MLLM模型，最佳模型仅达到41.1%的准确率，表明当前系统在鲁棒的完整流程理解方面仍相距甚远。进一步分析得出四个关键发现：性能不会随更多帧数可靠扩展；证据检索和临床解释仍是主要瓶颈；这些瓶颈根植于弱的程序推理和冗余条件下的注意力漂移；通用采样方法仅能部分平衡局部细节与全局覆盖。MedHorizon为MLLM在完整临床工作流中检索稀疏证据并进行推理提供了严格的测试平台。

</details>


### [82] [Solving Minimal Problems Without Matrix Inversion Using FFT-Based Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06572)
*Haidong Wu,Snehal Bhayani,Janne Heikkilä*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于稀疏隐变量结式和采样的相机几何估计方法，避免矩阵求逆，通过逆快速傅里叶插值重构行列式多项式，利用Cramer法则和GCD准则求解，在数值稳定性和运行时间上具有竞争力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于Gröbner基或结式的方法需要矩阵求逆，计算负担大，尤其在在线求解器中存在数值不稳定问题。

Method: 采用稀疏隐变量结式构造求解器；通过采样求值并利用逆快速傅里叶变换插值重构隐变量行列式多项式，避免符号展开；求解该多项式得到隐变量后，通过识别秩-1亏损子阵并结合Cramer法则恢复剩余未知量；引入基于最大公约数的准则确保噪声下的鲁棒子阵识别。

Result: 在多种最小问题上实验表明，所提求解器数值稳定性强，运行时间具有竞争力，尤其适用于小规模问题。

Conclusion: 该方法为传统Gröbner基和结式求解器提供了一种实用的替代方案。

Original Abstract: Estimating camera geometry typically involves solving minimal problems formulated as systems of multivariate polynomial equations, which often pose computational challenges when using existing Gröbner-basis or resultant-based methods due to matrix inversion needed in the online solver. Here we propose a sampling-based, matrix inversion-free method that constructs the solvers using sparse hidden-variable resultants. The determinant polynomial in the hidden variable is efficiently reconstructed via inverse fast Fourier transform interpolation from sampled evaluations, avoiding symbolic expansion. Solving this polynomial yields the hidden variable, and the remaining unknowns are recovered by identifying rank-1 deficient submatrices and applying Cramer's rule. A greatest common divisor-based criterion ensures robust submatrix identification under noise. Experiments on diverse minimal problems demonstrate that the proposed solver achieves strong numerical stability and competitive runtime, particularly for small-scale problems, providing a practical alternative to traditional Gröbner-basis and resultant-based solvers.

中文摘要翻译: 估计相机几何通常需要求解由多元多项式方程组形式化的最小问题，而现有基于Gröbner基或结式的方法由于在线求解器中需要矩阵求逆，往往带来计算挑战。本文提出一种基于采样的、无需矩阵求逆的方法，利用稀疏隐变量结式构造求解器。通过从采样评估中利用逆快速傅里叶变换插值高效重构隐变量中的行列式多项式，避免了符号展开。求解该多项式得到隐变量后，通过识别秩-1亏损子阵并应用Cramer法则恢复剩余未知量。基于最大公约数的准则确保了噪声下子阵识别的鲁棒性。在多种最小问题上的实验表明，所提求解器具有强数值稳定性和有竞争力的运行时间，尤其适用于小规模问题，为传统Gröbner基和结式求解器提供了一种实用的替代方案。

</details>


### [83] [DPM++: Dynamic Masked Metric Learning for Occluded Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06637)
*Lei Tan,Yingshi Luan,Pincong Zou,Pingyang Dai,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DPM++动态掩码度量学习框架，通过输入自适应的掩码度量选择可靠身份子空间，结合CLIP双阶段监督和显著性引导的补丁传输策略，有效解决遮挡行人重识别中不完整样本与全局身份表示不匹配的问题，在多项基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 遮挡导致被遮挡样本与全局身份表示不匹配，严重遮挡会去除判别性身体线索并引入背景和遮挡物的干扰，使全局度量学习不可靠。现有方法依赖额外预训练模型或数据增强，缺乏统一框架来学习对真实遮挡模式鲁棒的可见性一致性匹配。

Method: 提出DPM++动态掩码度量学习框架：1) 学习输入自适应的掩码度量，动态选择可靠的识别子空间；2) 基于分类器-原型空间，引入CLIP双阶段监督方案，从文本分支学习ID级语义先验并迁移到分类器-原型空间；3) 提出显著性引导的补丁传输策略合成可控、逼真的遮挡样本；4) 引入遮挡感知样本配对和掩码引导优化。

Result: 在遮挡和整体行人重识别基准上，DPM++在遮挡和整体场景下均一致超越先前最先进方法。

Conclusion: DPM++通过动态掩码度量学习有效解决了遮挡行人重识别问题，在真实遮挡模式下实现了鲁棒的可见性一致性匹配，为遮挡场景下的行人重识别提供了统一且有效的框架。

Original Abstract: Although person re-identification has made impressive progress, occlusion caused by obstacles remains an unsettled issue in real applications. The difficulty lies in the mismatch between incomplete occluded samples and holistic identity representations. Severe occlusion removes discriminative body cues and introduces interference from background clutter and occluders, making global metric learning unreliable. Existing methods mainly rely on extra pre-trained models to estimate visible parts for alignment or construct occluded samples via data augmentation, but still lack a unified framework that learns robust visibility-consistent matching under realistic occlusion patterns. In this paper, we propose DPM++, a Dynamic Masked Metric Learning framework for occluded person re-identification. DPM++ learns an input-adaptive masked metric that dynamically selects reliable identity subspaces for each occluded instance, enabling matching to emphasize visibility-consistent evidence while suppressing unreliable components. Built upon the classifier-prototype space, DPM++ introduces a CLIP-based two-stage supervision scheme, where ID-level semantic priors are learned from the text branch and transferred into the classifier-prototype space for dynamic masked matching. To strengthen the masked metric, we introduce a saliency-guided patch transfer strategy to synthesize controllable and photo-realistic occluded samples during training. Exploiting real scene priors, this strategy exposes the model to realistic partial observations and provides richer supervision than random erasing. In addition, occlusion-aware sample pairing and mask-guided optimization improve the stability and effectiveness of the framework. Experiments on occluded and holistic person re-identification benchmarks show that DPM++ consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods in both holistic and occlusion scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管行人重识别已取得令人瞩目的进展，但由障碍物引起的遮挡在实际应用中仍是一个未解决的问题。其困难在于不完整的被遮挡样本与全局身份表示之间的不匹配。严重遮挡会去除判别性的身体线索，并引入背景杂波和遮挡物的干扰，使得全局度量学习不可靠。现有方法主要依赖额外的预训练模型来估计可见部分以进行对齐，或通过数据增强构造被遮挡样本，但缺乏一个统一的框架来学习在真实遮挡模式下鲁棒的可见性一致性匹配。在本文中，我们提出了DPM++，一种用于遮挡行人重识别的动态掩码度量学习框架。DPM++学习一种输入自适应的掩码度量，为每个被遮挡实例动态选择可靠的身份子空间，使得匹配能够强调可见性一致的证据，同时抑制不可靠的成分。基于分类器-原型空间，DPM++引入了一种基于CLIP的两阶段监督方案，其中ID级语义先验从文本分支学习并迁移到分类器-原型空间，用于动态掩码匹配。为了增强掩码度量，我们引入了一种显著性引导的补丁传输策略，在训练过程中合成可控且逼真的遮挡样本。利用真实场景先验，该策略使模型暴露于真实的部分观测中，并提供比随机擦除更丰富的监督。此外，遮挡感知的样本配对和掩码引导优化提高了框架的稳定性和有效性。在遮挡和整体行人重识别基准上的实验表明，DPM++在整体和遮挡场景下均一致优于先前的最先进方法。

</details>


### [84] [Relit-LiVE: Relight Video by Jointly Learning Environment Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06658)
*Weiqing Xiao,Hong Li,Xiuyu Yang,Houyuan Chen,Wenyi Li,Tianqi Liu,Shaocong Xu,Chongjie Ye,Hao Zhao,Beibei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Relit-LiVE框架，通过将原始参考图像显式引入渲染过程并联合预测重光照视频与每帧环境贴图，实现了无需相机位姿先验的物理一致、时间稳定的视频重光照，在合成与真实基准上均超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于视频扩散模型的重光照方法依赖精确的内在分解，而真实视频的内在分解不可靠，常导致外观畸变、材质破损和时序伪影积累。

Method: 1) 显式引入原始参考图像到渲染过程，恢复内在表示中丢失或损坏的关键场景线索；2) 提出环境视频预测方案，在单一扩散过程中同时生成重光照视频和每帧与相机视角对齐的环境贴图，实现几何-光照的强对齐，支持动态光照与相机运动，无需已知每帧相机位姿。

Result: 在合成与真实基准上，Relit-LiVE在视频重光照和神经渲染任务中持续优于现有最先进方法，并支持场景级渲染、材质编辑、物体插入和流式视频重光照等下游应用。

Conclusion: 通过引入原始参考图像和联合环境视频预测，有效解决了内在分解不可靠导致的物理不一致与时序不稳定问题，显著提升了视频重光照的物理一致性和实用性。

Original Abstract: Recent advances have shown that large-scale video diffusion models can be repurposed as neural renderers by first decomposing videos into intrinsic scene representations and then performing forward rendering under novel illumination. While promising, this paradigm fundamentally relies on accurate intrinsic decomposition, which remains highly unreliable for real-world videos and often leads to distorted appearances, broken materials, and accumulated temporal artifacts during relighting. In this work, we present Relit-LiVE, a novel video relighting framework that produces physically consistent, temporally stable results without requiring prior knowledge of camera pose. Our key insight is to explicitly introduce raw reference images into the rendering process, enabling the model to recover critical scene cues that are inevitably lost or corrupted in intrinsic representations. Furthermore, we propose a novel environment video prediction formulation that simultaneously generates relit videos and per-frame environment maps aligned with each camera viewpoint in a single diffusion process. This joint prediction enforces strong geometric-illumination alignment and naturally supports dynamic lighting and camera motion, significantly improving physical consistency in video relighting while easing the requirement of known per-frame camera pose. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Relit-LiVE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art video relighting and neural rendering methods across synthetic and real-world benchmarks. Beyond relighting, our framework naturally supports a wide range of downstream applications, including scene-level rendering, material editing, object insertion, and streaming video relighting. The Project is available at https://github.com/zhuxing0/Relit-LiVE.

中文摘要翻译: 近期研究表明，大规模视频扩散模型可被重新用作神经渲染器，其方法是将视频先分解为内在场景表示，再在新光照下进行前向渲染。尽管这一范式前景广阔，但它从根本上依赖于精确的内在分解，而真实视频的内在分解高度不可靠，在重光照过程中常导致外观畸变、材质破损以及时序伪影的累积。在本工作中，我们提出Relit-LiVE，一个新颖的视频重光照框架，能够在无需相机位姿先验的情况下生成物理一致、时间稳定的结果。我们的关键洞察是将原始参考图像显式引入渲染过程，使模型能够恢复内在表示中不可避免丢失或损坏的关键场景线索。此外，我们提出一种新颖的环境视频预测方案，在单一扩散过程中同时生成重光照视频和与每个相机视角对齐的逐帧环境贴图。这种联合预测强化了几何-光照的对齐，自然地支持动态光照和相机运动，显著提升了视频重光照的物理一致性，同时降低了对已知逐帧相机位姿的要求。大量实验表明，Relit-LiVE在合成和真实基准上持续优于最先进的视频重光照和神经渲染方法。除重光照外，我们的框架自然支持广泛的下游应用，包括场景级渲染、材质编辑、物体插入和流式视频重光照。项目地址：https://github.com/zhuxing0/Relit-LiVE。

</details>


### [85] [BAMI: Training-Free Bias Mitigation in GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06664)
*Borui Zhang,Bo Zhang,Bo Wang,Wenzhao Zheng,Yuhao Cheng,Liang Tang,Yiqiang Yan,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出偏差感知操作推理（BAMI）方法，通过粗到细聚焦和候选选择两种操作，有效缓解GUI grounding中因高图像分辨率导致的精度偏差和复杂界面元素导致的模糊偏差，在无需训练的情况下显著提升多种模型的准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有GUI grounding模型在复杂场景（如ScreenSpot-Pro基准）中表现不佳，作者利用所提出的掩码预测分布（MPD）归因方法识别出错误主要来源于两类偏差：高图像分辨率导致的精度偏差和复杂界面元素导致的模糊偏差，因而需要针对性地加以解决。

Method: 提出偏差感知操作推理（BAMI）方法，包含两种关键操作：(1) 粗到细聚焦（coarse-to-fine focus），逐步缩小关注区域以缓解精度偏差；(2) 候选选择（candidate selection），从多个候选结果中筛选最优解以缓解模糊偏差。该方法无需训练即可应用于现有GUI grounding模型。

Result: 在ScreenSpot-Pro基准上，将BAMI应用于TianXi-Action-7B模型后，准确率从51.9%提升至57.8%。消融实验验证了该方法在不同参数配置下的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: BAMI方法能够在不进行额外训练的情况下，有效提升多种GUI grounding模型在复杂场景中的定位精度，具有较好的稳定性和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: GUI grounding is a critical capability for enabling GUI agents to execute tasks such as clicking and dragging. However, in complex scenarios like the ScreenSpot-Pro benchmark, existing models often suffer from suboptimal performance. Utilizing the proposed \textbf{Masked Prediction Distribution (MPD)} attribution method, we identify that the primary sources of errors are twofold: high image resolution (leading to precision bias) and intricate interface elements (resulting in ambiguity bias). To address these challenges, we introduce \textbf{Bias-Aware Manipulation Inference (BAMI)}, which incorporates two key manipulations, coarse-to-fine focus and candidate selection, to effectively mitigate these biases. Our extensive experimental results demonstrate that BAMI significantly enhances the accuracy of various GUI grounding models in a training-free setting. For instance, applying our method to the TianXi-Action-7B model boosts its accuracy on the ScreenSpot-Pro benchmark from 51.9\% to 57.8\%. Furthermore, ablation studies confirm the robustness of the BAMI approach across diverse parameter configurations, highlighting its stability and effectiveness. Code is available at https://github.com/Neur-IO/BAMI.

中文摘要翻译: GUI grounding是实现GUI智能体执行点击、拖拽等任务的关键能力。然而，在如ScreenSpot-Pro基准等复杂场景中，现有模型往往表现欠佳。利用本文提出的掩码预测分布（MPD）归因方法，我们发现误差的主要来源有两个：高图像分辨率（导致精度偏差）和复杂界面元素（导致模糊偏差）。为解决这些问题，我们引入了偏差感知操作推理（BAMI），该方法包含两种关键操作——粗到细聚焦和候选选择——以有效缓解上述偏差。大量实验结果表明，BAMI在无需训练的条件下显著提升了多种GUI grounding模型的准确率。例如，将我们的方法应用于TianXi-Action-7B模型后，其在ScreenSpot-Pro基准上的准确率从51.9%提升至57.8%。此外，消融研究证实了BAMI方法在不同参数配置下的鲁棒性，突显了其稳定性和有效性。代码已开源在https://github.com/Neur-IO/BAMI。

</details>


### [86] [ActCam: Zero-Shot Joint Camera and 3D Motion Control for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06667)
*Omar El Khalifi,Thomas Rossi,Oscar Fossey,Thibault Fouque,Ulysse Mizrahi,Philip Torr,Ivan Laptev,Fabio Pizzati,Baptiste Bellot-Gurlet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActCam是一种零样本视频生成方法，通过结合姿态和深度条件，在无需训练的情况下实现角色运动与相机参数的联合控制，尤其在大视角变化下表现优越。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成方法难以同时精细控制角色运动（表演）和相机轨迹（ cinematography），尤其是缺少对相机内外参数的逐帧控制能力。

Method: 基于预训练的图像到视频扩散模型，提出两阶段条件调度：早期去噪步骤同时使用姿态和稀疏深度条件以保持几何一致性，后期丢弃深度仅用姿态引导以细化高频细节，无需额外训练。

Result: 在多个基准上，相比纯姿态控制及其他姿态-相机方法，ActCam在相机遵从度和运动保真度上更优，在大视角变化下的人类偏好评估中尤其突出。

Conclusion: 精心设计的相机一致性条件和分阶段引导可以在无需训练的情况下实现强大的联合相机与运动控制。

Original Abstract: For artistic applications, video generation requires fine-grained control over both performance and cinematography, i.e., the actor's motion and the camera trajectory. We present ActCam, a zero-shot method for video generation that jointly transfers character motion from a driving video into a new scene and enables per-frame control of intrinsic and extrinsic camera parameters. ActCam builds on any pretrained image-to-video diffusion model that accepts conditioning in terms of scene depth and character pose. Given a source video with a moving character and a target camera motion, ActCam generates pose and depth conditions that remain geometrically consistent across frames. We then run a single sampling process with a two-phase conditioning schedule: early denoising steps condition on both pose and sparse depth to enforce scene structure, after which depth is dropped and pose-only guidance refines high-frequency details without over-constraining the generation. We evaluate ActCam on multiple benchmarks spanning diverse character motions and challenging viewpoint changes. We find that, compared to pose-only control and other pose and camera methods, ActCam improves camera adherence and motion fidelity, and is preferred in human evaluations, especially under large viewpoint changes. Our results highlight that careful camera-consistent conditioning and staged guidance can enable strong joint camera and motion control without training. Project page: https://elkhomar.github.io/actcam/.

中文摘要翻译: 对于艺术类应用，视频生成需要对表演（角色运动）和 cinematography（相机轨迹）进行精细控制。我们提出 ActCam，一种零样本视频生成方法，它能将驱动视频中的角色运动联合迁移到新场景中，并支持对相机内参和外参进行逐帧控制。ActCam 构建在任意接受场景深度和角色姿态作为条件输入的预训练图像到视频扩散模型之上。给定包含运动角色的源视频和目标相机运动，ActCam 生成在帧间保持几何一致的姿态和深度条件。然后我们采用单一采样过程，并配以两阶段条件调度：早期去噪步骤同时依赖姿态和稀疏深度条件以强化场景结构，随后丢弃深度条件，仅用姿态引导来细化高频细节，避免对生成结果过度约束。我们在涵盖多样化角色运动和挑战性视角变化的多个基准上评估了 ActCam。结果表明，与纯姿态控制及其他姿态与相机方法相比，ActCam 在相机遵从度和运动保真度方面更优，并且在人类评估中更受青睐，尤其是在大视角变化场景下。我们的结果强调，精心设计的相机一致性条件和分阶段引导可以在无需训练的情况下实现强大的联合相机与运动控制。项目页面：https://elkhomar.github.io/actcam/。

</details>

### [87] [Histogramless Time-Domain Sketched Fluorescence Lifetime Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06532)
*Zhenya Zang,Istvan Gyongy,Mike Davies*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Fisher信息最优分配节点的稀疏样条压缩策略，直接从TCSPC模块处理光子时间戳用于荧光寿命成像，在高达256倍压缩比下达到与全直方图NLSF/MLE相当的精度，并验证了在FPGA固件中实现的可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 时间相关单光子计数（TCSPC）在时域荧光寿命成像（FLIM）中产生大量数据，每个像素的全直方图存储或传输面临巨大的数据吞吐压力，尤其在高空间分辨率SPAD阵列中更为突出，因此需要一种高效的压缩方法。

Method: 提出一种统计感知的压缩策略：将光子时间戳直接投影到稀疏、非均匀的一维样条草图上，样条节点位置基于Fisher信息进行最优分配，使草图通道集中于统计判别性最强的衰变信号区域，而非均匀分配。

Result: 在合成单指数和双指数衰变数据以及实验荧光染料数据上验证，该方法在高达256倍压缩比下，精度与全直方图非线性最小二乘拟合（NLSF）和泊松最大似然估计（MLE）相当；同时通过定点查找表模拟验证了将时间戳到草图的投影集成到固件的可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Fisher信息优化的稀疏样条压缩方法能在大幅降低数据吞吐量的同时保持荧光寿命拟合精度，且具备在固件中实现的硬件可行性，适用于高空间分辨率SPAD阵列的FLIM系统。

Original Abstract: We present a statistics-aware compression strategy that processes photon timestamps directly from time-correlated single-photon counting (TCSPC) modules for time-domain fluorescence lifetime imaging (FLIM). Rather than storing or transmitting the full histogram per pixel, timestamps are projected onto sparse, non-uniform one-dimensional spline sketches, with knot positions optimally allocated based on Fisher information. This knot allocation concentrates sketch channels where the decay signal exhibits the greatest statistical discriminability, rather than using a uniform allocation. The proposed approach is extensively validated on synthetic mono- and bi-exponential decay data and on experimental fluorescent dye data, demonstrating comparable accuracy to full-histogram non-linear least-squares fitting (NLSF) and Poisson maximum-likelihood estimation (MLE) at compression ratios of up to 256x. We further validate the feasibility of integrating the timestamp-to-sketch projection directly into firmware via fixed-point (FXP) lookup-table (LUT) simulation, targeting high-spatial-resolution single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) arrays subject to significant data-throughput constraints.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种统计感知的压缩策略，直接从时间相关单光子计数（TCSPC）模块处理光子时间戳，用于时域荧光寿命成像（FLIM）。该方法无需存储或传输每个像素的全直方图，而是将时间戳投影到稀疏、非均匀的一维样条草图上，样条节点位置基于Fisher信息进行最优分配。这种节点分配将草图通道集中于衰变信号具有最大统计判别性的区域，而非采用均匀分配。该方法在合成单指数和双指数衰变数据以及实验荧光染料数据上进行了广泛验证，结果表明在高达256倍的压缩比下，其精度与全直方图非线性最小二乘拟合（NLSF）和泊松最大似然估计（MLE）相当。我们进一步通过定点查找表（FXP LUT）模拟，验证了将时间戳到草图的投影直接集成到固件中的可行性，旨在应用于受显著数据吞吐量约束的高空间分辨率单光子雪崩二极管（SPAD）阵列。

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>
