We study this question in the number game, a controlled setting in which a learner infers the hypothesis supported by a few positive integers, such as $\{16, 8, 2, 64\}$: a rule like powers of 2 or an interval like numbers near 20. We measure the posterior over hypotheses using three complementary probes: posterior prediction, hypothesis evaluation, and hypothesis generation. We then compare LLM behavior with an optimal Bayesian model and human behavior, and test whether the same posterior is expressed across probes. LLMs are often well described by a two-parameter Bayesian fit, but with systematic offsets: by default they show a strong-sampling assumption that creates an implicit Occam's razor, favoring narrower hypotheses, while thinking mode shifts them toward greater prior reliance. We also find a robust evaluation--generation gap: LLMs select more correct hypotheses during hypothesis evaluation but generate simpler, more rule-like hypotheses. Finally, this Bayesian-with-bias pattern does not extrapolate. Models can behave as if they hold rule-like hypotheses over observed examples, yet generalize poorly to parts of the hypothesis domain not covered by those examples. Our results highlight a limitation of LLMs as general problem solvers, especially for scientific inference, where hypotheses must go beyond the data.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地帮助人们解决问题，从调试代码到修理机械设备。这一过程需要从部分描述中生成合理的假设，然后随着更多信息的到来不断更新。然而，LLM如何执行这种形式的推理，以及它与最优推理之间的差距，尚不清楚。我们在数字游戏中研究这一问题，这是一个受控环境，其中学习者从几个正整数（例如{16, 8, 2, 64}）中推断支持的假设：可能是像"2的幂"这样的规则，或是像"接近20的数字"这样的区间。我们使用三种互补探针测量假设的后验概率：后验预测、假设评估和假设生成。然后我们将LLM的行为与最优贝叶斯模型和人类行为进行比较，并检验同一后验是否在不同探针间一致表达。LLM通常可以用双参数贝叶斯拟合来很好地描述，但存在系统性偏差：在默认情况下，它们表现出强采样假设，这产生了隐含的奥卡姆剃刀效应，偏好更狭窄的假设；而思维链模式则使它们更依赖先验。我们还发现了一个稳健的评估-生成差距：LLM在假设评估时选择更正确的假设，但在生成时却产生更简单、更规则化的假设。最后，这种带偏差的贝叶斯模式无法外推。模型在观测示例上表现得好像持有规则性假设，但对这些示例未覆盖的假设域部分泛化能力很差。我们的结果凸显了LLM作为通用问题求解器的局限性，特别是在需要超越数据进行假设推断的科学推理中。

</details>


### [150] [Measuring Learning Progress via Gradient-Momentum Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05856)
*Samuel Blad,Martin Längkvist,Amy Loutfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出梯度-动量耦合（GMC）信号替代预测误差，通过优化动力学衡量样本梯度对学习的贡献，在好奇心驱动探索中提升对噪声的鲁棒性并实现自发的课程学习。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 强化学习中广泛使用的预测误差信号无法区分有意义的可学习模式与随机噪声，导致好奇心驱动探索效率低下，亟需更鲁棒的信号来衡量学习进度。

Method: 提出GMC信号，计算每个样本梯度与历史动量在逐参数上的归一化绝对乘积，利用动量对噪声和振荡的天然过滤能力，量化样本梯度对持续学习的贡献程度。

Result: 在受控实验中验证了噪声鲁棒性和自发课程学习能力（信号按学习速度而非难度优先排序任务）；在MiniGrid环境中，用GMC替代预测误差可提升现有好奇心驱动架构对观测噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GMC是一种有效的学习进度信号，能更好地区分可学习模式与噪声，为好奇心驱动探索提供更鲁棒的引导。

Original Abstract: Measuring learning progress is essential for curiosity-driven exploration in reinforcement learning, but widely used signals such as prediction error often fail to distinguish meaningful, learnable patterns from random noise. This paper proposes Gradient-Momentum Coupling (GMC), a signal derived from optimization dynamics that quantifies how useful each sample's gradient is for ongoing learning by measuring its per-parameter normalized absolute product with the momentum from previous gradients. By leveraging momentum's natural filtering of noise and oscillations, GMC identifies samples that contribute to ongoing parameter updates. Controlled experiments demonstrate noise robustness and emergent curriculum learning, with the signal prioritizing tasks by learning speed rather than difficulty. Experiments on MiniGrid suggest that replacing prediction error with GMC within existing curiosity-driven architectures can improve robustness to observation noise.

中文摘要翻译: 衡量学习进度对于强化学习中的好奇心驱动探索至关重要，但广泛使用的信号（如预测误差）常常无法区分有意义的可学习模式与随机噪声。本文提出梯度-动量耦合（Gradient-Momentum Coupling, GMC），一种源自优化动力学的信号，通过测量每个样本梯度与先前梯度动量在逐参数上的归一化绝对乘积，量化该样本梯度对持续学习的有用程度。利用动量对噪声和振荡的天然过滤能力，GMC能够识别对持续参数更新有贡献的样本。受控实验证明了其噪声鲁棒性和自发课程学习能力——该信号按学习速度而非难度优先排序任务。在MiniGrid上的实验表明，在现有好奇心驱动架构中用GMC替代预测误差，可以提升对观测噪声的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [151] [Offline Reinforcement Learning for Rotation Profile Control in Tokamaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05857)
*Rohit Sonker,Hiro Josep Farre Kaga,Jiayu Chen,Andrew Rothstein,Ian Char,Ricardo Shousha,Egemen Kolemen,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用离线强化学习和离线基于模型的强化学习算法，仅基于DIII-D托卡马克的历史数据来控制等离子体旋转剖面，并在真实装置上部署策略取得了有前景的结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 托卡马克中的等离子体旋转剖面控制是一个高维度、多输入多输出且依赖等离子体条件的复杂问题，传统方法难以有效解决，而基于学习的控制方法受限于缺乏精确的旋转剖面动力学模拟器，因此需要探索仅利用历史数据进行离线强化学习的方法。

Method: 使用离线强化学习和离线基于模型的强化学习算法，利用等离子体动力学的概率模型生成轨迹用于强化学习训练，策略仅在DIII-D托卡马克的历史数据上训练。

Result: 将训练好的策略部署到DIII-D托卡马克上，观察到了有前景的真实世界实验结果。

Conclusion: 总结了在仅使用有限历史数据的情况下，在复杂物理装置上训练和部署强化学习策略的关键挑战和洞见。

Original Abstract: Tokamaks remain leading candidates for achieving practical fusion energy, yet many important control problems inside these devices are still difficult or unsolved. One such challenge is controlling the plasma rotation profile, which strongly influences stability, confinement, and transport. While the average rotation can be controlled, controlling the full profile is challenging due to high dimensionality, response to multiple actuators and dependence on plasma condition. Learning-based control methods, such as reinforcement learning (RL), provide a potential solution to this challenging problem with ability to model complex interactions leading to effective multi-input multi-output control. However, learning such policies is challenging due to the lack of accurate simulators that can model the rotation profile dynamics. In this work, we investigate the use of offline RL and offline model-based RL algorithms for rotation profile control, training them solely on historical data from the DIII-D tokamak. Our final method uses probabilistic models of plasma dynamics to generate rollouts for RL training. We deploy this policy on the DIII-D Tokamak and observe promising real-world results. We conclude by highlighting key challenges and insights from training and deploying an RL policy on a complex physical device while using only limited past data.

中文摘要翻译: 托卡马克仍然是实现实用聚变能的主要候选装置，但其内部的许多重要控制问题仍然困难或尚未解决。其中一个挑战是控制等离子体旋转剖面，该剖面强烈影响稳定性、约束和输运。虽然平均旋转可以被控制，但由于高维度、对多个执行器的响应以及对等离子体条件的依赖，控制完整的旋转剖面具有挑战性。基于学习的控制方法（如强化学习）具有建模复杂交互的能力，能够实现有效的多输入多输出控制，为解决这一难题提供了潜在方案。然而，由于缺乏能够准确建模旋转剖面动力学的模拟器，学习此类策略具有挑战性。在这项工作中，我们研究了使用离线强化学习和离线基于模型的强化学习算法来控制旋转剖面，这些算法仅基于DIII-D托卡马克的历史数据进行训练。我们的最终方法使用等离子体动力学的概率模型来生成用于强化学习训练的轨迹。我们将该策略部署在DIII-D托卡马克上，并观察到了有前景的真实世界结果。最后，我们总结了在仅使用有限历史数据的情况下，在复杂物理装置上训练和部署强化学习策略的关键挑战和洞见。

</details>


### [152] [Do Neural Operators Forget Geometry? The Forgetting Hypothesis in Deep Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05862)
*Yanming Xia,Angelica I. Aviles-Rivero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“几何遗忘假说”，指出神经算子因其马尔可夫层结构在深度增加时逐渐丢失域几何信息，并设计轻量级几何记忆注入机制来缓解该问题，揭示了几何保持是算子架构的结构性需求。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 神经算子在结构化域上表现良好，但在不规则几何上的行为尚不清楚；作者发现这一问题不仅是编码层面的缺陷，而是深层算子架构中固有的深度失效模式。

Method: 1. 形式化提出“几何遗忘假说”；2. 通过逐层几何探测实验验证谱算子和注意力算子系统性丢失几何保真度；3. 提出轻量级几何记忆注入机制，在中间深度恢复几何约束，架构开销极小。

Result: 1. 谱算子和注意力算子均随深度增加系统性地丢失几何保真度；2. 几何遗忘会降低精度、稳定性和泛化能力；3. 所提几何记忆注入有效缓解遗忘；4. 发现Transformer算子中存在几何捷径不稳定性。

Conclusion: 几何保持是神经算子的结构性需求而非设计选择；轻量级几何记忆注入是一种简单有效的缓解策略。

Original Abstract: Neural operators perform well on structured domains, yet their behaviour on irregular geometries remains poorly understood. We show that this limitation is not merely an encoding issue, but a depth-wise failure mode inherent to deep operator architectures.
  We formalise the Geometric Forgetting Hypothesis: due to the Markovian structure of operator layers and their reliance on global mixing mechanisms, neural operators progressively lose access to domain geometry as depth increases. Using layer-wise geometric probing, we demonstrate that both spectral and attention-based operators systematically lose geometric fidelity.
  We show that this geometric forgetting degrades accuracy, stability, and generalisation. To counteract it, we introduce a lightweight geometry memory injection mechanism that restores geometric constraints at intermediate depths with minimal architectural overhead. This simple intervention consistently mitigates forgetting and exposes a geometric shortcut instability in transformer-based operators, revealing that geometric retention is a structural requirement rather than a design choice.

中文摘要翻译: 神经算子在结构化域上表现良好，但其在不规则几何上的行为仍鲜为人知。我们表明，这一局限不仅仅是编码层面的问题，而是深层算子架构中固有的深度失效模式。我们形式化提出了“几何遗忘假说”：由于算子层的马尔可夫结构及其对全局混合机制的依赖，神经算子随着深度增加逐渐丢失域几何信息。通过逐层几何探测，我们证明谱算子和注意力算子均系统性丢失几何保真度。我们表明，这种几何遗忘会降低精度、稳定性和泛化能力。为应对这一问题，我们引入了一种轻量级的几何记忆注入机制，以极小的架构开销在中间深度恢复几何约束。这一简单干预能持续缓解遗忘，并暴露出基于Transformer的算子中存在一种几何捷径不稳定性，从而揭示了几何保持是一项结构性需求，而非设计选择。

</details>


### [153] [SOPE: Stabilizing Off-Policy Evaluation for Online RL with Prior Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05863)
*Carlo Romeo,Girolamo Macaluso,Alessandro Sestini,Andrew D. Bagdanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOPE利用演员对齐的离策略策略评估信号作为自动早停机制，动态控制离线训练阶段长度，在25个连续控制任务上提升性能高达45.6%，同时减少高达22倍的计算量，无需手动调参。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在将先验数据融入在线强化学习时，面临高计算成本与多阶段训练管线的困难权衡；固定长度的稳定阶段虽计算高效但需要任务相关的手动调参，会导致先验知识浪费或严重过拟合。

Method: 提出SOPE算法，使用演员对齐的离策略策略评估（OPE）信号作为自动早停机制，在留出的验证集上评估评论家在当前策略动作分布下的表现，当分布外收益饱和时精确停止梯度更新，无需手动调度调参。

Result: 在Minari基准套件的25个连续控制任务上，SOPE将基线性能提升最高45.6%，同时将所需TFLOPs减少最高22倍，平衡了样本效率与计算效率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 自适应、评估驱动的更新调度比依赖静态、穷举的更新调度更有效。

Original Abstract: Incorporating prior data into online reinforcement learning accelerates training but typically forces a difficult trade-off between high computational costs and long, multi-stage training pipelines. While fixed-length stabilization phases are significantly more computationally efficient than static update schedules, they require task-dependent manual tuning, risking either the waste of prior knowledge or severe overfitting. To address this, we propose SOPE, an algorithm that uses an actor-aligned Off-Policy Policy Evaluation (OPE) signal as an automated early-stopping mechanism to dynamically control the length of offline training phases. By evaluating the critic on a held-out validation split under the current policy's action distribution, SOPE halts gradient updates exactly when out-of-distribution benefits saturate, eliminating the need for manual schedule tuning. Evaluated on 25 continuous control tasks from the Minari benchmark suite, SOPE improves baseline performance by up to 45.6% while reducing the required TFLOPs by up to 22x, thus balancing the tradeoff between sample and computational efficiency. These findings demonstrate that adaptive, evaluation-driven update schedules are more effective than relying on static, exhaustive update schedules.

中文摘要翻译: 将先验数据融入在线强化学习可以加速训练，但通常会在高计算成本与冗长的多阶段训练管线之间陷入艰难的权衡。虽然固定长度的稳定阶段在计算效率上显著优于静态更新调度，但它们需要依赖任务的手动调参，存在浪费先验知识或导致严重过拟合的风险。为解决这一问题，我们提出SOPE算法，该算法使用演员对齐的离策略策略评估（OPE）信号作为自动早停机制，动态控制离线训练阶段的长度。通过在当前策略的动作分布下，对留出的验证集上的评论家进行评估，SOPE能在分布外收益饱和时精确停止梯度更新，从而消除了手动调度调参的需要。在Minari基准套件的25个连续控制任务上评估，SOPE将基线性能提升高达45.6%，同时将所需TFLOPs减少高达22倍，从而平衡了样本效率与计算效率之间的权衡。这些发现表明，自适应、评估驱动的更新调度比依赖静态、穷举的更新调度更为有效。

</details>


### [154] [QuadraSHAP: Stable and Scalable Shapley Values for Product Games via Gauss-Legendre Quadrature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05870)
*Majid Mohammadi,Grigory Reznikov,Pavel Sinitcyn,Krikamol Muandet,Siu Lun Chau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对乘积博弈（产品游戏）中Shapley值的高效计算问题，提出了一种精确的一维积分表示，并基于Gauss–Legendre求积法设计了QuadraSHAP算法，实现了O(d m_q)计算复杂度和O(log d)并行时间，实验表明该方法是所有测试配置中最快的数值稳定方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在机器学习可解释性中，当价值函数继承自底层模型的乘法结构（如乘积核的核方法和树模型）时，Shapley值的计算面临指数级特征组合的求和难题，现有方法效率低下或数值不稳定。

Method: 提出乘积博弈中Shapley值的一维积分精确表示，将指数级特征组合的加权求和转化为[0,1]上d-1次多项式的积分；基于此设计Gauss–Legendre求积方案，当积分节点数m_q ≥ ⌈d/2⌉时精确可证明，否则误差几何衰减；通过对数空间评估实现数值稳定，并采用关联扫描原语实现O(d m_q)总工作量和O(log d)并行时间的并行算法QuadraSHAP。

Result: 理论分析表明，Gauss–Legendre求积在节点满足特定条件下精确，否则误差随节点数几何衰减；实践中数百节点即可在数千特征上达到高精度；实验证明QuadraSHAP在所有测试配置中是最快的数值稳定方法。

Conclusion: 乘积博弈中Shapley值的计算可简化为一个高效且数值稳定的一维积分问题，QuadraSHAP方法在计算速度和稳定性上均优于现有方案。

Original Abstract: We study the efficient computation of Shapley values for \emph{product games} -- cooperative games in which the coalition value factorizes as a product of per-player terms. Such games arise in machine learning explainability whenever the value function inherits a multiplicative structure from the underlying model, as in kernel methods with product kernels and tree-based models. Our key result is that the Shapley value of each player in a product game admits an exact one-dimensional integral representation: the weighted sum over exponentially many feature coalitions collapses to the integral of a degree-$(d-1)$ polynomial over $[0,1]$, where $d$ is the total number of features. This yields a Gauss--Legendre quadrature scheme that is \emph{provably exact} whenever the number of nodes satisfies $m_q \geq \lceil d/2 \rceil$, and otherwise provides a \emph{near-exact} approximation with error provably decaying geometrically in $m_q$. In practice, a few hundred nodes can achieve highly precise estimates even with thousands of features. Building on this formulation, we derive a numerically stable implementation via log-space evaluation, together with an efficient parallel implementation based on associative scan primitives that achieves $O(d\,m_q)$ total work and $O(\log d)$ parallel time. Experiments show that \textsc{QuadraSHAP} is the fastest numerically stable method across all tested configurations.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究乘积博弈中Shapley值的高效计算问题——乘积博弈是指联盟值可分解为各参与者项乘积的一类合作博弈。当价值函数从底层模型中继承乘法结构时（如具有乘积核的核方法以及基于树的模型），这类博弈在机器学习可解释性中自然出现。我们的关键发现是：乘积博弈中每个参与者的Shapley值存在精确的一维积分表示——指数多个特征组合的加权求和可坍缩为[0,1]上d-1次多项式的积分，其中d是特征总数。由此导出一个Gauss–Legendre求积方案，当节点数m_q ≥ ⌈d/2⌉时该方案可证明精确，否则提供近精确近似且误差随m_q几何衰减。实践中，即使面对数千个特征，仅需几百个节点即可获得高精度估计。基于这一公式，我们通过对数空间评估推导出数值稳定的实现，并基于关联扫描原语设计了高效的并行实现，总工作量为O(d m_q)，并行时间为O(log d)。实验表明，QuadraSHAP在所有测试配置中是最快的数值稳定方法。

</details>


### [155] [Retain-Neutral Surrogates for Min-Max Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05871)
*Junhao Cai,Dohun Kim,Dowon Kim,Sung Il Choi,Chengjun Jin,Juhyun Park,Changhee Joo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ROSU方法，通过约束内部替代点构建为保留正交扰动，在保持保留损失不变的同时最大化遗忘增益，实现了更优的机器遗忘效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在最小-最大机器遗忘中，当遗忘梯度和保留梯度高度对齐时，无约束的遗忘最大化扰动会移动到增加保留损失的替代点，导致保留性能下降。现有方法未有效解决这一梯度对齐带来的副作用。

Method: 提出Retain-Orthogonal Surrogate Unlearning (ROSU)，在固定扰动预算下，约束内部替代点构造为最大化一阶遗忘增益的同时保持一阶保留变化为零。这产生了一个闭式保留正交扰动、轻量级传输外更新以及沿保留中性方向的放大。

Result: 建立了曲率控制的保留损伤二阶上界；证明了正对齐机制下ROSU严格优于标准最小-最大扰动；证明了梯度接近正交时两者近似等价。在CIFAR-10/100、Tiny-ImageNet、TOFU、WMDP等视觉和语言基准上，ROSU在高耦合场景下增益最明显，在其他场景下保持竞争力。

Conclusion: ROSU通过保留正交约束有效解决了梯度对齐导致的保留损失增加问题，在保持保留性能的同时实现高效遗忘，且几何分析揭示了其优势条件。

Original Abstract: Machine unlearning seeks to remove the influence of designated training data while preserving performance on the remaining data. Approximate unlearning can be viewed as a local editing problem; in min-max unlearning, the key local object is the surrogate point at which the retain objective is evaluated. When forget and retain gradients are strongly aligned, an unconstrained forget-maximizing perturbation can move to a surrogate point that increases retain loss. We propose Retain-Orthogonal Surrogate Unlearning (ROSU), which constrains the inner surrogate construction by maximizing first-order forget gain subject to zero first-order retain change under a fixed perturbation budget. This yields a closed-form retain-orthogonal perturbation, a lightweight transported outer update, and amplification along the retain-neutral direction. Our analysis establishes (i) a curvature-controlled second-order bound on retain damage, (ii) a positive-alignment regime in which ROSU strictly reduces surrogate retain loss relative to standard min-max perturbations, and (iii) near-equivalence when the two gradients are nearly orthogonal. Across vision and language benchmarks (CIFAR-10/100, Tiny-ImageNet, TOFU, WMDP), the empirical pattern follows this geometry: ROSU gives its clearest gains in high-coupling regimes while remaining competitive elsewhere.

中文摘要翻译: 机器遗忘旨在移除指定训练数据的影响，同时保持对剩余数据的性能。近似遗忘可视为局部编辑问题；在最小-最大遗忘中，关键局部对象是用于评估保留目标的替代点。当遗忘梯度和保留梯度高度对齐时，无约束的遗忘最大化扰动可能移动到增加保留损失的替代点。我们提出保留正交替代遗忘（ROSU），该方法通过约束内部替代点构造，在固定扰动预算下最大化一阶遗忘增益，同时保持一阶保留变化为零。这产生了一个闭式保留正交扰动、轻量级传输外更新以及沿保留中性方向的放大。我们的分析建立了：(i) 曲率控制的保留损伤二阶上界，(ii) 正对齐机制下ROSU严格降低相对于标准最小-最大扰动的替代保留损失，以及(iii) 当两个梯度接近正交时两者近乎等价。在视觉和语言基准（CIFAR-10/100、Tiny-ImageNet、TOFU、WMDP）上，实证模式符合这一几何分析：ROSU在高耦合场景下增益最明显，同时在其它场景下保持竞争力。

</details>


### [156] [RepFlow: Representation Enhanced Flow Matching for Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05890)
*Yifei Xie,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RepFlow框架，将表征学习与条件流匹配(CFM)结合，通过联合优化解决因果效应估计中的选择偏差和分布建模问题，在点估计和分布估计上均优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有因果效应估计方法大多局限于点估计，缺乏分布建模能力，且难以同时处理缺失反事实和选择偏差问题。

Method: 将因果效应估计建模为联合优化问题，融合表征学习与条件流匹配(CFM)；通过最小化熵正则化Wasserstein距离来缓解处理组与对照组之间的选择偏差；引入L2归一化约束增强数值稳定性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，RepFlow在点因果效应估计和分布因果效应估计上均一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RepFlow通过平衡表征使流模型能够准确捕捉潜在结果的分布，有效提升了因果效应估计的性能。

Original Abstract: Estimating causal effects from observational data has become increasingly critical in diverse fields including healthcare, economics, and social policy. The fundamental challenge in causal inference arises from the missing counterfactuals and the selection bias. Existing methods are largely limited to point estimates and lack the capacity for distribution modeling. In this work, we propose RepFlow, a novel framework that formulates causal effect estimation as a joint optimization problem integrating representation learning with Conditional Flow Matching (CFM).
  RepFlow mitigates selection bias by minimizing the entropically regularized Wasserstein distance between treated and control representations.
  To enhance numerical stability, we further introduce an $L_2$ normalization constraint on latent representations.
  This balanced representation enables the flow model to accurately capture the distribution of potential outcomes. Extensive experiments across a wide range of benchmarks demonstrate that RepFlow consistently outperforms existing methods in both point and distributional causal effect estimation.

中文摘要翻译: 从观测数据中估计因果效应在医疗保健、经济学和社会政策等多个领域中变得越来越重要。因果推断的根本挑战源于缺失反事实和选择偏差。现有方法大多局限于点估计，缺乏分布建模能力。在本文中，我们提出RepFlow，这是一个新颖的框架，将因果效应估计建模为一个联合优化问题，融合了表征学习与条件流匹配(Conditional Flow Matching, CFM)。RepFlow通过最小化处理组与对照组表征之间的熵正则化Wasserstein距离来缓解选择偏差。为了增强数值稳定性，我们进一步在潜在表征上引入了L2归一化约束。这种平衡的表征使得流模型能够准确捕捉潜在结果的分布。在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，RepFlow在点因果效应估计和分布因果效应估计上均一致优于现有方法。

</details>


### [157] [VisMMOE: Exploiting Visual-Expert Affinity for Efficient Visual-Language MoE Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05899)
*Cheng Xu,Xiaofeng Hou,Jiacheng Liu,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VisMMoE是一个针对视觉-语言混合专家模型（VL-MoE）的推理卸载系统，核心思路是通过修剪冗余视觉token来重塑专家访问模式（即“视觉-专家亲和力”），结合亲和力感知的token压缩、前瞻性专家预测和缓存/流水线编排，在有限内存下提升专家局部性和预取效率，实现最高2.68倍和1.61倍的端到端推理加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的MoE卸载系统主要针对文本密集型任务设计，在处理视觉密集输入（大量视觉token）时效果显著下降，因为视觉token会导致专家访问分散且难以预测。需要一种针对VL-MoE模型视觉特点的卸载优化方法。

Method: 1) 提出"视觉-专家亲和力"概念，发现token修剪可使专家访问在层内更集中、跨层更稳定；2) 亲和力感知的token压缩；3) 前瞻性专家预测；4) 缓存与流水线编排，共同提升专家局部性和预取效率。

Result: 在多个框架和代表性VL-MoE模型上，VisMMoE相较于强基线实现了最高2.68倍和1.61倍的端到端推理性能提升，同时保持有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: 通过利用视觉token修剪对专家访问模式的积极影响（视觉-专家亲和力），可以显著优化VL-MoE模型在内存受限平台上的推理卸载效率，实现性能与精度的良好权衡。

Original Abstract: Large-scale vision-language mixture-of-experts (VL-MoE) models provide strong multimodal capability, but efficient deployment on memory-constrained platforms remains difficult. Existing MoE offloading systems are largely designed for text-centric workloads and become much less effective for visual-heavy inputs, where large numbers of visual tokens induce broader and less predictable expert accesses. We present VisMMoE, a VL-MoE offloading system built on a single systems insight: pruning redundant visual tokens can improve offloading not only by reducing computation, but also by reshaping expert demand. We refer to this effect as \textit{visual-expert affinity}: token pruning makes expert accesses more concentrated within layers and more stable across layers, producing a smaller and more predictable expert working set. Guided by this insight, VisMMoE combines affinity-aware token compression, lookahead expert prediction, and cache/pipeline orchestration to improve expert locality and prefetch effectiveness under tight memory budgets. We implement VisMMoE on multiple frameworks and evaluate it on representative VL-MoE models and benchmarks. VisMMoE improves end-to-end inference performance by up to 2.68x and 1.61x, respectively, over strong baselines for today's VL-MoE deployments while maintaining competitive accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模视觉-语言混合专家（VL-MoE）模型具备强大的多模态能力，但在内存受限平台上的高效部署仍然困难。现有的MoE卸载系统主要针对文本密集型负载设计，在处理视觉密集输入时效果显著下降——大量视觉token会引发更广泛且更不可预测的专家访问。我们提出VisMMoE，一个基于单一系统洞察构建的VL-MoE卸载系统：修剪冗余视觉token不仅能通过减少计算来提升卸载性能，还能重塑专家需求模式。我们将此效应称为"视觉-专家亲和力"：token修剪使专家访问在层内更加集中、在层间更加稳定，从而产生更小且更可预测的专家工作集。在此洞察指导下，VisMMoE结合了亲和力感知的token压缩、前瞻性专家预测以及缓存/流水线编排，以在紧内存预算下提升专家局部性和预取有效性。我们在多个框架上实现VisMMoE，并在代表性VL-MoE模型和基准测试上进行评估。相较于当前VL-MoE部署的强基线，VisMMoE在保持有竞争力精度的同时，将端到端推理性能分别提升了最高2.68倍和1.61倍。

</details>


### [158] [Quadratic Objective Perturbation: Curvature-Based Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05905)
*Daniel Cortild,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出二次目标扰动（QOP）机制，通过添加随机二次型扰动来实现差分隐私，放宽了对损失函数梯度有界的强假设，并在插值区域和近似求解下提供了隐私与效用保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 线性目标扰动（LOP）要求损失函数梯度有界的强假设，这排除了许多现代机器学习模型。本文旨在提出一种更弱假设下仍能保证差分隐私的扰动机制。

Method: 提出二次目标扰动（QOP），通过向目标函数添加随机二次型扰动来引入强凸性和稳定性，利用扰动的谱性质而非梯度假设来控制灵敏度，从而在插值区域实现差分隐私。同时扩展分析以覆盖近似求解情形。

Result: 在更弱的假设下（插值区域）实现了(ε, δ)-差分隐私；隐私保证在非精确求解下仍得以保持；推导了经验超额风险的效用界；理论分析和数值比较表明曲率基扰动相比LOP具有优势。

Conclusion: QOP通过曲率诱导稳定性的方式有效替代了梯度有界假设，能够在更广泛的模型上实现差分隐私，且求解高效，为差分隐私经验风险最小化提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Objective perturbation is a standard mechanism in differentially private empirical risk minimization. In particular, Linear Objective Perturbation (LOP) enforces privacy by adding a random linear term, while strong convexity and stability are ensured by an additional deterministic quadratic term. However, this approach requires the strong assumption of bounded gradients of the loss function, which excludes many modern machine learning models. In this work, we introduce Quadratic Objective Perturbation (QOP), which perturbs the objective with a random quadratic form. This perturbation induces strong convexity and enforces stability of the problem through curvature, thereby enabling privacy and allowing sensitivity to be controlled through spectral properties of the perturbation rather than assumptions on the gradients. As a result, we obtain $(\varepsilon, δ)$-differential privacy under weaker assumptions, in the interpolation regime. Furthermore, we extend the analysis to account for approximate solutions, showing that privacy guarantees are preserved under inexact solves. Additionally, we derive utility guarantees in terms of empirical excess risk, and provide a theoretical and numerical comparison to LOP, highlighting the advantages of curvature-based perturbations. Finally, we discuss algorithmic aspects and show that the resulting problems can be solved efficiently using modern splitting schemes.

中文摘要翻译: 目标扰动是差分隐私经验风险最小化中的一种标准机制。具体而言，线性目标扰动（LOP）通过添加随机线性项来实施隐私保护，同时通过额外的确定性二次项确保强凸性和稳定性。然而，该方法要求损失函数梯度有界这一强假设，这排除了许多现代机器学习模型。在本文中，我们提出了二次目标扰动（QOP），该方法通过添加随机二次型来扰动目标函数。这种扰动通过曲率诱导强凸性并增强问题的稳定性，从而使得隐私保护成为可能，并且灵敏度可以通过扰动的谱性质而非梯度假设来控制。由此，我们在插值区域（interpolation regime）内、在更弱假设下获得了(ε, δ)-差分隐私。此外，我们将分析扩展至近似求解情形，表明非精确求解下隐私保证仍得以保持。同时，我们推导了关于经验超额风险的效用界，并与LOP进行了理论和数值比较，凸显了基于曲率的扰动的优势。最后，我们讨论了算法方面的实现，并表明所得问题可通过现代分裂方法高效求解。

</details>


### [159] [From Drops to Grid: Noise-Aware Spatio-Temporal Neural Process for Rainfall Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05912)
*Rafael Pablos Sarabia,Joachim Nyborg,Morten Birk,Ira Assent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DropsToGrid，一种基于神经过程的方法，融合不规则分布的私有气象站时序数据和雷达空间上下文，生成高分辨率、带有不确定性量化的降雨场，在真实数据集上优于传统和深度学习方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统降雨观测存在偏差大、分辨率低的问题，难以捕捉局部降雨；现有深度学习稠密化方法受限于降雨数据的偏态分布、噪声以及时空融合能力不足。

Method: 基于神经过程（Neural Process）框架，结合多尺度特征提取、时序注意力机制和多模态融合技术，融合噪声且不规则分布的私有气象站时序数据与雷达空间信息，生成随机连续降雨场并显式量化不确定性。

Result: 在真实数据集上，DropsToGrid在仅少量站点可用和跨区域场景下均优于操作基线（operational baselines）和深度学习基线，生成准确的、校准良好的高分辨率降雨图。

Conclusion: DropsToGrid通过有效融合多模态数据并量化不确定性，能够生成高精度、高分辨率的降雨场，具有实际业务应用潜力。

Original Abstract: High-resolution rainfall observations are crucial for weather forecasting, water management, and hazard mitigation. Traditional operational measurements are often biased and low-resolution, limiting their ability to capture local rainfall. Accurate high-resolution rainfall maps require integrating sparse surface observations, yet existing deep learning densification methods are hindered by rainfall's skewed, localized nature, noise, and limited spatio-temporal fusion. We present DropsToGrid, a Neural Process-based method that generates dense rainfall fields by fusing temporal sequences from noisy, irregularly distributed private weather stations with spatial context from radar. Leveraging multi-scale feature extraction, temporal attention, and multi-modal fusion, the model produces stochastic, continuous rainfall estimates and explicitly quantifies uncertainty. Evaluations on real-world datasets demonstrate that DropsToGrid outperforms both operational and deep learning baselines, generating accurate high-resolution rainfall maps with well-calibrated uncertainty, even when only few stations are available and in cross-regional scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 高分辨率降雨观测对于天气预报、水资源管理和减灾至关重要。传统业务测量往往存在偏差且分辨率低，难以捕捉局部降雨。生成准确的高分辨率降雨图需要融合稀疏的地面观测数据，然而现有的深度学习稠密化方法受限于降雨数据的偏态分布、局部性强、噪声以及时空融合能力不足。我们提出DropsToGrid，一种基于神经过程（Neural Process）的方法，通过融合来自噪声、不规则分布的私有气象站的时序序列与雷达提供的空间上下文，生成稠密降雨场。该模型利用多尺度特征提取、时序注意力机制和多模态融合技术，产生随机连续的降雨估计并显式量化不确定性。在真实数据集上的评估表明，即使在仅有少量站点可用以及跨区域场景下，DropsToGrid性能均优于业务基线和深度学习基线，能够生成具有良好校准不确定性的准确高分辨率降雨图。

</details>


### [160] [Near-Policy: Accelerating On-Policy Distillation via Asynchronous Generation and Selective Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05940)
*Miao Rang,Zhenni Bi,Hang Zhou,Kai Han,Xuechun Wang,An Xiao,Xinghao Chen,Yunhe Wang,Hanting Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出近策略蒸馏（NPD），一种异步知识蒸馏框架，通过解耦学生模型的生成与训练过程，结合序列打包的监督微调（SFT）以及Δ-IFD样本过滤机制，在保证近策略学习的同时大幅提升训练效率，实现了8.1倍加速并显著优于SFT基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自回归模型的标准知识蒸馏常面临分布不匹配问题。在策略方法（on-policy）虽能缓解该问题，但依赖计算昂贵的强化学习框架。现有方法在效率与策略一致性之间难以兼顾。

Method: 提出近策略蒸馏（NPD），一种异步蒸馏方法：（1）将学生模型的生成与训练解耦，使得监督微调与序列打包成为可能；（2）引入稀疏学生更新策略，减少异步更新带来的策略滞后；（3）设计Δ-IFD过滤机制，启发式地筛选样本，剔除极端离群分布样本，防止噪声主导梯度，确保更新处于安全的近策略学习区域。

Result: 相比在策略基线方法，NPD实现8.1倍加速；相比SFT提升8.09%；使得openPangu-Embedded-1B模型达到68.73%的SOTA分数，超越参数规模更大的Qwen3-1.7B。

Conclusion: NPD通过异步解耦与样本过滤机制，在保持近策略学习优势的同时大幅提升知识蒸馏效率，并为后续强化学习有效缩小探索空间，实现了小模型对大模型的反超。

Original Abstract: Standard knowledge distillation for autoregressive models often suffers from distribution mismatch. While on-policy methods mitigate this by leveraging student-generated outputs, they rely on computationally expensive Reinforcement Learning (RL) frameworks. To improve efficiency, we propose Near-Policy Distillation (NPD), an asynchronous approach that decouples student generation from training. This reformulation enables Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with sequence packing. However, asynchronous updates inevitably introduce policy lag and sample noise, which can cause the behavior to drift from near-policy toward off-policy. To counteract this without sacrificing efficiency, NPD integrates sparse student updates and the $Δ$-IFD filtering mechanism, a heuristic sample selection mechanism that empirically stabilizes the optimization trajectory. By filtering extreme out-of-distribution samples, $Δ$-IFD prevents noise from dominating the gradients, ensuring updates remain within a safe proximal learning zone. Empirically, the NPD framework achieves a 8.1x speedup over on-policy baselines and outperforms SFT by 8.09%. Crucially, by effectively narrowing the exploration space for subsequent RL, our method enables openPangu-Embedded-1B to reach a state-of-the-art score of 68.73%, outperforming the substantially larger Qwen3-1.7B. Codes will be released soon.

中文摘要翻译: 自回归模型的标准知识蒸馏常面临分布不匹配问题。虽然在策略方法（on-policy methods）通过利用学生模型生成的输出来缓解这一问题，但其依赖于计算昂贵的强化学习框架。为了提高效率，我们提出近策略蒸馏（Near-Policy Distillation, NPD），一种异步方法，将学生模型的生成与训练过程解耦。这一重新公式化使得监督微调（Supervised Fine-Tuning, SFT）与序列打包（sequence packing）成为可能。然而，异步更新不可避免地引入策略滞后（policy lag）和样本噪声，可能导致行为从近策略（near-policy）漂移向离策略（off-policy）。为了在不牺牲效率的情况下抵消这一影响，NPD集成了稀疏学生更新（sparse student updates）和Δ-IFD过滤机制——一种启发式样本选择机制，能够经验性地稳定优化轨迹。通过过滤极端离群分布样本，Δ-IFD防止噪声主导梯度，确保更新保持在安全的近策略学习区域内。实验上，NPD框架相比在策略基线方法实现8.1倍加速，并比SFT提升8.09%。关键在于，通过有效缩小后续强化学习的探索空间，我们的方法使openPangu-Embedded-1B模型达到68.73%的最优分数，超越了参数规模更大的Qwen3-1.7B。代码将很快开源。

</details>


### [161] [Knowing but Not Correcting: Routine Task Requests Suppress Factual Correction in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05957)
*Zixuan Chen,Hao Lin,Zizhe Chen,Yizhou Tian,Garry Yang,Depeng Wang,Ya Guo,Huijia Zhu,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现并系统研究了大型语言模型中的“修正抑制”现象——模型在孤立呈现时能纠正错误陈述，但在任务导向的请求中却倾向于顺从而非纠正。作者构建了300个错误前提的基准，发现抑制率高达19%-90%，并揭示其机制是“知道但不纠正”（知识编码正常，但输出意图在中间层转向服从）。据此提出两种无需训练的方法：修正方向引导（CDS）和动态载荷放大（DPA），显著提升了模型的“事实严格性”。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有大语言模型在孤立场景下能可靠纠正错误信息，但在任务导向的上下文中却常常选择顺从用户预设的错误前提而非纠正，这种“修正抑制”现象尚未被系统研究。论文旨在揭示该现象的普遍性、严重性及其内部机制，并寻找无需训练的干预方法。

Method: (1) 构建300个错误前提的基准数据集，在8个模型上系统评估修正抑制率。(2) 通过注意力分析进行机制研究，发现抑制源于任务上下文使早期层注意力偏离错误陈述，中间层输出意图向服从结晶。(3) 提出两种训练无关干预：修正方向引导（CDS）从匹配正负样本估计修正-服从方向并在中间层注入；动态载荷放大（DPA）通过注意力的层间差异定位载荷词元并在最终层放大其表示。

Result: (1) 8个模型的修正抑制率范围为19%-90%，其中4个模型超过80%，证实该现象普遍且严重。(2) 机制上，模型内部仍编码了正确知识，但任务上下文导致输出意图向服从偏移。(3) 在Qwen3.5-9B和LLaMA3.1-8B上，CDS将Qwen3.5-9B的修正率从0%提升至58.2%；DPA是唯一在两个模型上都保持或提升推理能力的方法。

Conclusion: 论文提出了“事实严格性”这一衡量模型可靠性的新维度，即模型在面对上下文压力时坚持准确性的意愿。研究表明修正抑制是严重且普遍的现象，但可以通过理解其机制设计有效的无需训练的干预方法。这为构建更可靠的语言模型提供了新方向。

Original Abstract: LLMs reliably correct false claims when presented in isolation, yet when the same claims are embedded in task-oriented requests, they often comply rather than correct. We term this failure mode \emph{correction suppression} and construct a benchmark of 300 false premises to systematically evaluate it across eight models. Suppression rates range from 19\% to 90\%, with four models exceeding 80\%, establishing correction suppression as a prevalent and severe phenomenon. Mechanistic analysis reveals that suppression is not a knowledge failure: the model registers the error internally but task context diverts early-layer attention from the false claim as output intent crystallizes toward compliance at middle layers. We characterize this as \emph{knowing but not correcting} -- suppression occurs at response selection rather than knowledge encoding. Guided by this mechanism, we propose two training-free interventions. Correction Direction Steering (CDS) estimates a correction-compliance direction from matched pairs and injects it at middle layers before output intent crystallizes. Dynamic Payload Amplification (DPA) localizes payload tokens via attention divergence between early and late layers and amplifies their representation at the final layer, requiring no calibration data. Experiments on Qwen3.5-9B and LLaMA3.1-8B show both methods substantially improve factual strictness. CDS achieves the highest correction rate on Qwen3.5-9B (0\%$\to$58.2\%). DPA is the only method that preserves or improves reasoning capability on both models. These findings introduce \emph{factual strictness} -- the willingness to uphold accuracy against contextual pressures -- as a new dimension of model reliability.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型在孤立呈现时能够可靠地纠正错误陈述，但当相同的错误陈述被嵌入到任务导向的请求中时，它们往往选择服从而非纠正。我们将这种失败模式称为"修正抑制"，并构建了一个包含300个错误前提的基准数据集，在八个模型上对其进行了系统评估。抑制率从19%到90%不等，其中四个模型超过80%，证实修正抑制是一种普遍且严重的现象。机制分析表明，抑制并非知识层面的失败：模型内部已记录到错误，但任务上下文使早期层的注意力偏离了错误陈述，同时输出意图在中间层朝向服从结晶。我们将此特征描述为"知道但不纠正"——抑制发生在响应选择阶段而非知识编码阶段。基于这一机制，我们提出了两种无需训练的干预方法。修正方向引导（CDS）从匹配的正负样本对中估计修正-服从方向，并在输出意图结晶之前将其注入中间层。动态载荷放大（DPA）通过早期层与后期层之间的注意力差异来定位载荷词元，并在最终层放大其表示，无需任何校准数据。在Qwen3.5-9B和LLaMA3.1-8B上的实验表明，两种方法均显著提升了事实严格性。CDS在Qwen3.5-9B上取得了最高的修正率（0%→58.2%）。DPA是唯一在两个模型上都保持或提升了推理能力的方法。这些发现引入了"事实严格性"——即在上下文压力下坚持准确性的意愿——作为模型可靠性的一个新维度。

</details>


### [162] [Beyond Uniform Credit Assignment: Selective Eligibility Traces for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05965)
*Chaoli Mou,Zhan Zhuang,Xinning Chen,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出选择性资格迹（S-trace）方法，通过稀疏资格迹机制和低熵令牌掩码实现细粒度信用分配，在提升强化学习效率的同时超越GRPO等基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无评论家强化学习算法（如GRPO）采用"均匀信用分配"假设，将轨迹级优势 indiscriminately 广播到所有令牌，无法区分关键推理步骤，导致学习效率低下。

Method: 首先提出P-trace（基于部分信任域保留的样本高效无评论家资格迹方法），在此基础上构建S-trace，通过选择性掩码低熵令牌实现稀疏资格迹机制，从而达到细粒度信用分配和方差降低。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B上提升0.49%，Qwen3-4B上提升3.16%，Qwen3-8B上保持2.98%的提升（pass@16平均），同时实现更高的样本效率和令牌效率。

Conclusion: S-trace通过稀疏资格迹机制有效解决了均匀信用分配问题，在提升推理能力的同时兼顾了样本效率和令牌效率，并将GSPO方法理论化为资格迹框架下的一个特例。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a key approach for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models. However, widely used critic-free algorithms such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) necessitate a ``uniform credit assignment'' assumption that indiscriminately broadcast trajectory-level advantages, hindering learning efficiency by failing to distinguish critical reasoning steps. To address this limitation, we propose Selective Eligibility Traces (S-trace). Grounded in the intuition of partial trust region preservation, we initially introduce P-trace as a sample-efficient, critic-free eligibility traces method, upon which we build S-trace, implementing a sparse eligibility traces mechanism to further mitigate variance and achieve fine-grained credit assignment by selectively masking low-entropy tokens. Theoretically, we contextualize the recent Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO) method within the critic-free eligibility traces framework, identifying it as a special instance of the eligibility traces method operating under uniform credit assignment. Experiments demonstrate that S-trace not only outperforms GRPO, showing gains of 0.49\% on Qwen3-1.7B and 3.16\% on Qwen3-4B, and maintaining a robust 2.98\% improvement when scaled further to Qwen3-8B in average pass@16, but notably achieves this with simultaneously higher sample and token efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为提升大型语言模型推理能力的关键方法。然而，广泛使用的无评论家算法（如组相对策略优化，GRPO）需要"均匀信用分配"假设，即 indiscriminately 将轨迹级优势广播到所有令牌，由于无法区分关键推理步骤而阻碍了学习效率。为解决这一局限性，我们提出了选择性资格迹（S-trace）。基于部分信任域保留的直觉，我们首先引入P-trace作为一种样本高效、无评论家的资格迹方法，在此基础上构建S-trace，通过选择性掩码低熵令牌实现稀疏资格迹机制，以进一步降低方差并实现细粒度的信用分配。理论上，我们将最近的组序列策略优化（GSPO）方法置于无评论家资格迹框架中进行语境化，将其识别为在均匀信用分配下运作的资格迹方法的一个特例。实验表明，S-trace不仅优于GRPO，在Qwen3-1.7B上提升0.49%，Qwen3-4B上提升3.16%，并在进一步扩展到Qwen3-8B时保持稳健的2.98%提升（平均pass@16），而且值得注意的是，它同时实现了更高的样本效率和令牌效率。

</details>


### [163] [Sharper Guarantees for Misspecified Kernelized Bandit Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05967)
*Davide Maran,Csaba Szepesvári*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对误指定核化老虎机优化问题，提出通过谱局部化（离线）和域分裂（在线）机制，将误指定惩罚项从传统的核复杂度因子（如√d_eff或√γ_n）降低到对数或多项式对数增长。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有误指定核化老虎机优化方法中，误指定水平ε会被核复杂度因子（如√d_eff或√γ_n）放大，导致理论保证较差。作者希望消除或降低这种放大效应，获得更紧的边界。

Method: 离线场景中引入谱Lebesgue常数来刻画误指定项，利用谱局部化控制逼近算子的Lebesgue常数；在线场景中修改域分裂算法，基于局部化特征衰减假设，移除误指定项中的额外√γ_n因子。

Result: 对一维单调谱得到对数放大，对多维傅里叶对角乘积核得到多项式对数放大；在线场景中在温和的局部化特征衰减假设下实现了Õ(√γ_n n + nε)的累积遗憾界。

Conclusion: 谱局部化和空间域分裂是降低误指定放大效应的统一原则，能够显著改善核化老虎机优化在误指定场景下的理论保证。

Original Abstract: Existing guarantees for misspecified kernelized bandit optimization pay for misspecification through kernel complexity: in generic offline bounds, the misspecification level $\varepsilon$ is multiplied by $\sqrt{d_\mathrm{eff}}$, where $d_\mathrm{eff}$ is the kernel effective dimension, while in online regret bounds, the corresponding penalty is $\sqrt{γ_n}\,n\varepsilon$, where $γ_n$ is the maximum information gain after $n$ rounds of interaction.
  In this work, we show that, for a large class of kernels, the misspecification amplification can be reduced to logarithmic or polylogarithmic growth. In the offline setting, we first prove high-probability simple-regret bounds whose misspecification term is governed by a spectral Lebesgue constant. This yields logarithmic amplification for one-dimensional monotone spectra and polylogarithmic amplification for multivariate Fourier-diagonal product kernels. In the online setting, we modify a domain-splitting algorithm and prove a cumulative regret bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt{γ_n n}+n\varepsilon)$ under mild localized eigendecay assumptions, removing the extra $\sqrt{γ_n}$ factor from the misspecification term. The common principle is localization: spectral localization controls the Lebesgue constant of the offline approximation operator, while domain splitting implements the spatial analogue of this mechanism in the online setting, preventing local misspecification errors from being amplified globally.

中文摘要翻译: 现有针对误指定核化老虎机优化的理论保证通过核复杂度来为误指定付出代价：在通用离线界中，误指定水平ε乘以√d_eff，其中d_eff为核的有效维度；而在在线遗憾界中，相应的惩罚项为√γ_n·nε，其中γ_n是经过n轮交互后的最大信息增益。本文表明，对于一大类核，误指定的放大效应可以降低到对数或多项式对数增长。在离线场景中，我们首先证明了高概率的简单遗憾界，其中的误指定项由谱Lebesgue常数控制。对于一维单调谱，这产生对数放大；对于多维傅里叶对角乘积核，产生多项式对数放大。在在线场景中，我们修改了一个域分裂算法，并在温和的局部化特征衰减假设下证明了Õ(√γ_n n + nε)的累积遗憾界，移除了误指定项中的额外√γ_n因子。其共同原理是局部化：谱局部化控制了离线逼近算子的Lebesgue常数，而域分裂在在线场景中实现了该机制的时空类比，阻止了局部误指定误差被全局放大。

</details>


### [164] [Training Transformers for KV Cache Compressibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05971)
*Yoav Gelberg,Yam Eitan,Michael Bronstein,Yarin Gal,Haggai Maron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KV压缩感知训练(KV-CAT)，通过训练时强制KV稀疏化，引导模型学习更易压缩的表示，从而提升后验KV压缩方法的效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 长上下文语言模型中KV缓存的内存和延迟成本随前缀长度线性增长，现有后验压缩方法受限于模型内部表示的可压缩性，但可压缩性是学习到的表示属性而非上下文本身属性，因此需要从训练阶段引导模型学习可压缩表示。

Method: 提出KV-Compression Aware Training (KV-CAT)，一种持续预训练方法。在训练时引入KV稀疏化策略，对KV槽进行掩码，迫使模型使用更少的KV槽，从而学习到适合后验压缩的表示。

Result: 实验表明KV-CAT在检索、长上下文问答和基于困惑度的压缩前缀续接评估中，均能改善后验压缩方法的质量-预算权衡。

Conclusion: 训练时引导模型学习可压缩表示能有效提升后验KV压缩的性能，KV-CAT是一种有效的训练策略。

Original Abstract: Long-context language modeling is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value (KV) cache, whose memory and decode-time access costs scale linearly with the prefix length. This bottleneck has motivated a range of context-compression methods, from token-level summarization to recent optimization-based KV compression methods. These post-hoc methods operate on the KV cache of a fixed pretrained model, so their effectiveness is fundamentally limited by how well the model's internal representations can be compressed. In this work, we formalize the notion of KV compressibility and show that it is a property of the learned representations, rather than of the context alone. We prove that almost any sequence-to-vector function admits both highly compressible and inherently non-compressible transformer implementations, highlighting the need to guide transformers toward compressible representations during training. Motivated by this, we propose KV-Compression Aware Training (KV-CAT), a continued pretraining procedure that incentivizes the emergence of compressible representations. We introduce a train-time KV sparsification policy that masks KV slots during training. This forces the model to use fewer KV slots and encourages it to learn representations amenable to post-hoc compression. Empirically, we show that KV-CAT improves the quality-budget tradeoff of downstream compression methods across retrieval, long-context question answering, and perplexity-based evaluation of compressed-prefix continuation.

中文摘要翻译: 长上下文语言建模日益受到键值（KV）缓存的制约，其内存和解码时访问成本随前缀长度线性增长。这一瓶颈催生了多种上下文压缩方法，从令牌级摘要到近期基于优化的KV压缩方法。这些后验方法作用于固定预训练模型的KV缓存，因此其有效性根本上受限于模型内部表示的可压缩程度。本文形式化了KV可压缩性的概念，并证明它是学习到的表示属性，而非仅取决于上下文本身。我们证明几乎任意序列到向量的函数都可以同时拥有高度可压缩和本质不可压缩的Transformer实现，这凸显了在训练过程中引导Transformer朝向可压缩表示的必要性。受此启发，我们提出KV压缩感知训练（KV-CAT），一种持续预训练过程，它激励可压缩表示的出现。我们引入一种训练时KV稀疏化策略，在训练过程中对KV槽进行掩码。这迫使模型使用更少的KV槽，并鼓励其学习适合后验压缩的表示。实验表明，KV-CAT在检索、长上下文问答和基于困惑度的压缩前缀续接评估中，均能改善后验压缩方法的质量-预算权衡。

</details>


### [165] [Physical Fidelity Reconstruction via Improved Consistency-Distilled Flow Matching for Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05975)
*Sicheng Ma,Tianyue Yang,Xiuzhe Wu,Xiao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将最优传输流匹配生成模型蒸馏为单步一致性模型，用于快速高保真科学流场重建，在三个流体基准上实现了12倍推理加速，且学生模型在频谱指标上保持教师性能，参数减半，训练效率更高。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型和流匹配模型依赖迭代采样，推理成本高，难以应用于集合预报、实时可视化和仿真在环推理等延迟敏感的科学流场重建任务。

Method: 将最优传输流匹配教师模型蒸馏为单步一致性模型。推理时通过沿传输路径从加噪观测初始化生成轨迹，使无条件高保真流模型无需重新训练即可执行条件重建。

Result: 在烟雾浮力、湍流通道流和科尔莫戈罗夫流三个基准上，蒸馏学生模型在频谱指标上与教师模型性能相当，参数量减少约一半，推理速度提升12倍；在相同训练预算下，SSIM比从头训练的单步一致性模型高23.1%。

Conclusion: 教师蒸馏不仅加速采样，还能提升训练效率，为将高容量科学生成模型转化为更快速、更廉价、更易部署的紧凑重建模型提供了有前景的路径。

Original Abstract: Reconstructing high-fidelity flow fields from low-fidelity observations is a central problem in scientific machine learning, yet recent diffusion and flow-matching models typically rely on iterative sampling, making them costly for latency-sensitive workflows such as ensemble forecasting, real-time visualization, and simulation-in-the-loop inference. We study whether a high-fidelity flow-matching generative model can be compressed into a compact one-step model for fast scientific flow reconstruction. Our approach distills an optimal-transport flow-matching teacher into a one-step consistency model. Low-fidelity observations are incorporated at inference by initializing the generative trajectory from a noised observation along the transport path, allowing an unconditional high-fidelity flow model to perform conditional reconstruction without retraining the teacher. We evaluate this distillation strategy on three fluid benchmarks, Smoke Buoyancy, Turbulent Channel Flow, and Kolmogorov Flow, using coarse-to-fine reconstruction as a controlled testbed at field sizes up to $256 \times 256$. Across these settings, the distilled student retains similar performance of the teacher's model on spectrum metrics, while using roughly half as many parameters and achieving a $12\times$ inference speedup over the flow-matching teacher. Under the same training budget, the distilled student also outperforms a one-step consistency model trained directly from scratch by $23.1\%$ in SSIM, showing that teacher distillation improves training efficiency rather than merely accelerating sampling. These results suggest a promising route for turning future high-capacity scientific generative models into compact reconstruction models that are faster to train, cheaper to run, and easier to deploy.

中文摘要翻译: 从低保真观测重建高保真流场是科学机器学习中的核心问题，然而近期的扩散模型和流匹配模型通常依赖迭代采样，使其在集合预报、实时可视化和仿真在环推理等延迟敏感的工作流中成本高昂。本文研究能否将高保真流匹配生成模型压缩为紧凑的单步模型，以实现快速科学流场重建。我们的方法将最优传输流匹配教师模型蒸馏为单步一致性模型。推理时，通过沿传输路径从加噪观测初始化生成轨迹，使无条件高保真流模型无需重新训练教师即可执行条件重建。我们在烟雾浮力、湍流通道流和科尔莫戈罗夫流三个流体基准上评估该蒸馏策略，以粗到细重建作为受控测试平台，场尺寸达256×256。在这些设定下，蒸馏学生模型在频谱指标上保持与教师模型相近的性能，同时参数量减少约一半，推理速度相比流匹配教师模型提升12倍。在相同训练预算下，蒸馏学生模型的SSIM比从头训练的单步一致性模型高23.1%，表明教师蒸馏提升了训练效率，而不仅仅是加速采样。这些结果表明，将未来高容量科学生成模型转化为训练更快、运行更廉、部署更易的紧凑重建模型是一条有前景的路径。

</details>


### [166] [A Fine-Grained Understanding of Uniform Convergence for Halfspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06004)
*Aryeh Kontorovich,Kasper Green Larsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文精细地分析了半空间（halfspaces）的均匀收敛行为，揭示了超出最坏情况VC界的新结果：非齐次半空间在任意维度上紧贴标准VC界；而二维齐次半空间则展现出明显更优的收敛速率，可实现$O(1/n)$泛化误差，且齐次情形下$\ln\ln n$的额外代价是不可避免的。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统VC界给出了均匀收敛的最坏情况上界，但无法刻画不同结构（如齐次与非齐次、维度差异）对收敛速率的精细影响。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，精确刻画半空间在不同维度和结构下的真实均匀收敛行为。

Method: 采用临界楔形局部化（critical-wedge localization）论证，在二进风险带上建立无对数项的偏差界；通过带合并（union over bands）技术引入仅$\ln\ln n$的额外开销；同时构造匹配下界证明该开销的不可回避性。

Result: (1) 非齐次半空间：可实现情形下泛化误差为$\Theta(d\ln(n/d)/n)$；对抗情形下偏差随真实误差$\tau$按$\sqrt{\tau\ln(1/\tau)}$缩放。(2) 二维齐次半空间：可实现情形下误差为$O(1/n)$；对抗情形下建立了无对数的二进风险带偏差界，带合并后仅增加$\ln\ln n$因子，且该因子是紧的。

Conclusion: 给出了半空间均匀收敛的精细且近乎完整的理论图景，揭示了维度（$d\ge2$与$d=2$）和结构（齐次与非齐次）之间的尖锐阈值，证明了标准VC界在非齐次情形下本质紧致，而齐次情形可突破该界限。

Original Abstract: We study the fine-grained uniform convergence behavior of halfspaces beyond worst-case VC bounds. For inhomogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with $d\ge 2$, we show that standard first-order VC bounds are essentially tight: even consistent hypotheses can incur population error $Θ(d\ln(n/d)/n)$, and in the agnostic setting the deviation scales as $\sqrt{τ\ln(1/τ)}$ at true error $τ$. In contrast, homogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^2$ exhibit a markedly different behavior. In the realizable case, every hypothesis consistent with the sample has error $O(1/n)$. In the agnostic case, we prove a bandwise, log-free deviation bound on each dyadic risk band via a critical-wedge localization argument. Unioning over bands incurs only a $\ln\ln n$ overhead, and we establish a matching lower bound showing this overhead is unavoidable. Together, these results give a fine-grained and nearly complete picture of uniform convergence for halfspaces, revealing sharp dimensional and structural thresholds.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了半空间在最坏情况VC界之外的细粒度均匀收敛行为。对于$\mathbb{R}^d$（$d\ge 2$）中的非齐次半空间，我们证明标准的一阶VC界本质上是紧的：即使一致的假设也会产生$\Theta(d\ln(n/d)/n)$的总体误差，而在不可知设置下，偏差在真实误差$\tau$处按$\sqrt{\tau\ln(1/\tau)}$缩放。相比之下，$\mathbb{R}^2$中的齐次半空间表现出显著不同的行为。在可实现情形下，每个与样本一致的假设的误差为$O(1/n)$。在不可知情形下，我们通过临界楔形局部化论证，在每个二进风险带上证明了一个带状的、无对数项的偏差界。对各带进行合并仅引入$\ln\ln n$的额外开销，并且我们建立了一个匹配的下界，表明该开销是不可避免的。综合起来，这些结果给出了半空间均匀收敛的一个细粒度且近乎完整的图景，揭示了尖锐的维度和结构性阈值。

</details>


### [167] [Quantizing With Randomized Hadamard Transforms: Efficient Heuristic Now Proven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06014)
*Ran Ben-Basat,William Kuszmaul,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明两次随机Hadamard变换(RHT)在边际分布上渐近等价于均匀随机旋转(URR)，三次RHT可满足向量量化的弱相关性要求，并提出一种线性时间自适应检查方法动态调整RHT次数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在实际应用中，均匀随机旋转(URR)常被随机Hadamard变换(RHT)替代以加速计算，但单个RHT在最坏情况下性能不佳，其坐标分布远偏离URR的高斯分布。本文旨在研究多次RHT组合能否在理论上匹配URR的性能保证。

Method: 1) 理论分析两次RHT后归一化旋转向量的边际分布与标准高斯分布之间的Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离均为O(d^{-1/2})；2) 将上述界代入DRIVE和QUIC-FL等压缩方案分析；3) 证明三次RHT可实现坐标协方差衰减，确保向量量化码本的期望误差与URR一致；4) 提出线性时间O(d)的输入矩检查方法，动态调整RHT使用次数。

Result: 两次RHT的边际分布在Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离下均以O(d^{-1/2})逼近标准高斯；两次RHT在DRIVE和QUIC-FL中渐近匹配URR性能；三次RHT确保向量量化的期望误差与URR仅相差一个随维度消失的附加项；线性时间检查方法可在运行时动态优化RHT次数。

Conclusion: 组合多次RHT可以在理论上保证与URR渐近等价的性能，其中两次RHT满足边际分布收敛要求，三次RHT满足向量量化的弱相关性要求，且提出的自适应方法可在实际中规避最坏情况输入。

Original Abstract: Uniform random rotations (URRs) are a common preprocessing step in modern quantization approaches used for gradient compression, inference acceleration, KV-cache compression, model weight quantization, and approximate nearest-neighbor search in vector databases. In practice, URRs are often replaced by randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which preserve orthogonality while admitting fast implementations. The remaining issue is the performance for worst-case inputs. With a URR, each coordinate is individually distributed as a shifted beta distribution, which converges to a Gaussian distribution in high dimensions. Generally, one RHT is not suitable in the worst case, as individual coordinates can be far from these distributions. We show that after composing two RHTs on any $d$-sized input vector, the marginal distribution of every fixed coordinate of the normalized rotated vector is within $O(d^{-1/2})$ of a standard Gaussian both in Kolmogorov distance and in $1$-Wasserstein distance. We then plug these bounds into the analyses of modern compression schemes, namely DRIVE and QUIC-FL, and show that two RHTs achieve performance that asymptotically matches URRs.
  However, we show that two RHTs may not be sufficient for Vector Quantization (VQ), which often requires weak correlation across fixed-size blocks of coordinates (as opposed to only marginal distribution convergence for single coordinates). We prove that a composition of three RHTs leads to decaying coordinate covariance. This ensures that any fixed, bounded, multi-dimensional VQ codebook optimized for URRs has the same expected error when using three RHTs, up to an additive term that vanishes with the dimension.
  Finally, because practical inputs are rarely adversarial, we propose a linear-time ${O}(d)$ check on the input's moments to dynamically adapt the number of RHTs used at runtime to improve performance.

中文摘要翻译: 均匀随机旋转(URRs)是现代量化方法中常见的预处理步骤，广泛应用于梯度压缩、推理加速、KV缓存压缩、模型权重量化以及向量数据库中的近似最近邻搜索。在实践中，URRs常被随机Hadamard变换(RHTs)替代，后者保持正交性同时支持快速实现。剩余的问题是最坏情况输入下的性能。使用URR时，每个坐标单独服从偏移贝塔分布，在高维下收敛到高斯分布。一般而言，单个RHT在最坏情况下不适用，因为个别坐标可能远偏离这些分布。我们证明，对任意d维输入向量施加两次RHT后，归一化旋转向量的每个固定坐标的边际分布在Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离下均在O(d^{-1/2})内逼近标准高斯分布。随后将这些界代入现代压缩方案(DRIVE和QUIC-FL)的分析中，表明两次RHT实现的性能渐近匹配URR。然而，我们证明两次RHT可能不足以满足向量量化(VQ)的需求，后者通常需要坐标固定大小块之间的弱相关性（而不仅仅是单个坐标的边际分布收敛）。我们证明三次RHT的组合会导致坐标协方差衰减。这确保任何为URR优化的固定、有界、多维VQ码本在使用三次RHT时具有相同的期望误差，仅相差一个随维度消失的附加项。最后，由于实际输入很少是对抗性的，我们提出一种线性时间O(d)的输入矩检查方法，可在运行时动态调整使用的RHT次数以提升性能。

</details>


### [168] [Multi-agent decision making: A Blackwell's informativeness approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06028)
*Zheng Zhang,Cuong C. Nguyen,Kevin Wells,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Blackwell信息框架对多LLM协作决策（投票与辩论）进行理论分析，证明这些方法的信息量不超过所有智能体私有信息的汇总，并提出基于后验积估计的实用方法，在六个QA基准上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多LLM聚合方法（如投票和辩论）缺乏形式化保证，无法确保决策的信息量。

Method: 使用Blackwell信息结构框架分析多LLM决策；提出基于后验积估计器（product-of-posteriors estimator）的实用方法，近似贝叶斯池化后验。

Result: 理论上证明投票和辩论诱导的信息结构不比所有智能体私有信息汇总更具信息量；实验上在六个QA基准上优于SOTA的多LLM辩论和投票方法。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯池化后验最大化是Blackwell序下的信息理论上界决策规则，所提方法有效提升了多LLM问答性能。

Original Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has motivated research on decision-making in multi-agent systems, where multiple agents collaborate to achieve shared objectives. Existing aggregation approaches, such as voting and debate, are largely ad-hoc and lack formal guarantees regarding the informativeness of the resulting decisions. In this paper, we provide a principled approach to analyse decisions made in the multi-LLM setting using Blackwell's informativeness framework. Within the Blackwell information-structure abstraction, we show that voting and debate induce information structures that are no more informative than the pooled private information of all agents. This result identifies Bayesian pooled posterior maximisation as an information-theoretic upper-bound decision rule under the Blackwell ordering. Motivated by this theoretical analysis, we introduce a practical method for LLM-based question-answering (QA) tasks that estimates each agent's posterior and approximates the pooled posterior using a product-of-posteriors estimator. Extensive experiments on six QA benchmarks demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art multi-LLM debate and voting methods.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型的快速发展推动了多智能体系统中决策制定的研究，其中多个智能体协作以实现共享目标。现有的聚合方法（如投票和辩论）大多是启发式的，缺乏对决策信息性的形式化保证。本文利用Blackwell信息性框架，为多LLM场景下的决策分析提供了原则性方法。在Blackwell信息结构抽象下，我们证明了投票和辩论所诱导的信息结构不比所有智能体私有信息的汇总更具信息性。这一结果将贝叶斯池化后验最大化识别为Blackwell序下的信息理论上界决策规则。受此理论分析启发，我们提出了一种面向基于LLM的问答任务的实用方法，该方法估计每个智能体的后验并通过后验积估计器近似池化后验。在六个问答基准上的大量实验表明，我们的方法优于最先进的多LLM辩论和投票方法。

</details>


### [169] [Does Synthetic Data Help? Empirical Evidence from Deep Learning Time Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06032)
*Hugo Cazaux,Eyjólfur Ingi Ásgeirsson,Hlynur Stefánsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究（9组实验、4218次运行）系统评估了合成数据在时间序列预测中的应用。关键发现：合成数据的效果高度依赖架构——通道混合模型（TimesNet, iTransformer）多数情况下受益，而通道独立模型（DLinear, PatchTST）持续退化。仅在低资源场景下，TimesNet在10%数据+合成增强时超越全数据基线。平均而言，67%的试验中增强带来损害。季节性-趋势生成器是唯一可靠的方法，硬课程切换有害（MSE升高24%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 合成数据在语言模型训练中已发挥重要作用，但其在时间序列预测中的角色尚不清楚。本文旨在系统性地理解合成数据对时间序列预测的影响，提供具体可操作的指导原则。

Method: 大规模实证研究：设置9个实验组，进行4218次系统运行，跨5种架构（TimesNet, iTransformer, DLinear, PatchTST等）、4种合成信号和7个数据集，评估合成时间序列增强的效果。

Result: (1) 合成增强效果高度依赖架构：通道混合模型受益，通道独立模型持续退化；(2) 低资源场景下效果显著：TimesNet在10%数据+合成增强下超越全数据基线（4/16种稀疏-数据集组合）；(3) 平均67%试验中增强有害；(4) 仅季节性-趋势生成器可靠；(5) 硬课程切换导致+24% MSE退化。

Conclusion: 提供具体可操作的指导原则：使用通道混合架构进行合成增强，采用渐进退火调度方案，将低资源增强视为架构和数据集依赖的任务。

Original Abstract: Synthetic data has transformed language model training, yet its role in time series forecasting remains poorly understood. We present a large-scale empirical study: nine experiment groups, 4,218 runs systematically evaluating synthetic time series augmentation across five architectures, four synthetic signals and seven datasets. The effect is sharply architecture-conditional: channel-mixing models (TimesNet, iTransformer) benefit in the majority of trials, while channel-independent models (DLinear, PatchTST) are consistently degraded. In selected low-resource settings the gains are striking: TimesNet trained on only 10\% of Weather data with synthetic augmentation surpasses the full-data baseline (4 of 16 sparsity-dataset combinations). Averaged across all architectures, augmentation hurts in 67\% of trials. We further find that only the Seasonal-Trend generator reliably helps across the tested benchmarks, and that hard curriculum switching is actively harmful (+24\% MSE degradation). These results provide concrete, actionable guidelines on how to use synthetic data: use synthetic augmentation with channel-mixing architectures, use gradual annealing schedules, and treat low-resource augmentation as architecture- and dataset-dependent. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/hugoiscracked/synthetic-ts/tree/main}

中文摘要翻译: 合成数据已经改变了语言模型的训练方式，但其在时间序列预测中的作用仍未被充分理解。我们进行了一项大规模的实证研究：涉及九个实验组、4218次运行，系统评估了跨五种架构、四种合成信号和七个数据集的合成时间序列增强效果。其效果高度依赖于架构：通道混合模型（TimesNet, iTransformer）在大多数试验中受益，而通道独立模型（DLinear, PatchTST）则持续退化。在选定的低资源设置中，增益显著：使用仅10%的Weather数据并结合合成增强训练的TimesNet，超越了使用完整数据的基线（4/16种稀疏度-数据集组合）。平均而言，在所有架构上，增强在67%的试验中造成了损害。我们进一步发现，只有季节性-趋势生成器在所测试的基准上可靠地提供帮助，而硬课程切换则是有害的（MSE退化24%）。这些结果为如何使用合成数据提供了具体且可操作的指导原则：使用通道混合架构进行合成增强，采用渐进退火调度方案，并将低资源增强视为依赖于架构和数据集的任务。代码可在 \href{https://github.com/hugoiscracked/synthetic-ts/tree/main} 获取。

</details>


### [170] [Optimal Transport for LLM Reward Modeling from Noisy Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06036)
*Licheng Pan,Haochen Yang,Haoxuan Li,Yunsheng Lu,Yongqi Tong,Yinuo Wang,Shijian Wang,Zhixuan Chu,Lei Shen,Yuan Lu,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SelectiveRM框架，基于最优传输理论中的部分传输机制（Mass Relaxation）和联合一致性差异（Joint Consistency Discrepancy），自动排除含噪声偏好的样本，从而在RLHF中构建更鲁棒的奖励模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现实RLHF数据集中不可避免地存在噪声偏好，传统训练目标会过度拟合这些错误，而现有去噪方法多基于同质噪声假设，无法捕捉语言偏好的复杂多样性。

Method: 1）提出联合一致性差异（Joint Consistency Discrepancy），对齐模型预测分布与偏好数据分布；2）引入质量松弛（Mass Relaxation）机制，通过部分最优传输允许模型自主排除与语义一致性矛盾的噪声样本，避免严格质量守恒导致拟合离群点。

Result: 理论上证明SelectiveRM优化了未观测干净风险的一个更紧上界；大量实验表明该方法在多个基准上显著超越现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 基于最优传输的部分传输机制能有效处理RLHF中复杂的噪声偏好问题，SelectiveRM为构建鲁棒奖励模型提供了理论支撑和实用框架。

Original Abstract: Reward models are fundamental to Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), yet real-world datasets are inevitably corrupted by noisy preference. Conventional training objectives tend to overfit these errors, while existing denoising approaches often rely on homogeneous noise assumptions that fail to capture the complexity of linguistic preferences. To handle these challenges, we propose SelectiveRM, a framework grounded in optimal transport. We first devise a Joint Consistency Discrepancy to align the distribution of model predictions with preference data. Furthermore, to address the limitation of strict mass conservation which compels the model to fit outliers, we incorporate a Mass Relaxation mechanism via partial transport. This enables the autonomous exclusion of samples with noisy preference that contradict semantic consistency. Theoretically, we demonstrate that SelectiveRM optimizes a tighter upper bound on the unobserved clean risk. Extensive experiments validate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 奖励模型是强化学习从人类反馈（RLHF）中的基础，但真实世界的数据集不可避免地受到噪声偏好的污染。传统的训练目标倾向于过度拟合这些错误，而现有的去噪方法往往依赖于同质噪声假设，无法捕捉语言偏好的复杂性。为应对这些挑战，我们提出SelectiveRM，一个基于最优传输的框架。我们首先设计了一个联合一致性差异（Joint Consistency Discrepancy）来对齐模型预测的分布与偏好数据。此外，为克服严格质量守恒迫使模型拟合离群点的局限，我们通过部分传输（partial transport）引入了一种质量松弛（Mass Relaxation）机制。这使得模型能够自主排除那些与语义一致性相矛盾的噪声偏好样本。理论上，我们证明了SelectiveRM优化了未观测干净风险的一个更紧上界。大量实验验证了我们的方法在多个基准上显著优于现有的最先进基线。

</details>


### [171] [Requests of a Feather Must Flock Together: Batch Size vs. Prefix Homogeneity in LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06046)
*Saksham Rathi,Preeti,Mythili Vutukuru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Feather是一个前缀感知的调度器，通过强化学习在批处理大小与前缀同质性之间寻找最优平衡，并引入分块哈希树（CHT）实现高效前缀检测，相比现有调度器实现2-10倍的端到端吞吐量提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在大语言模型自回归解码过程中，KV缓存访问是内存瓶颈。现有推理引擎虽然通过前缀感知调度来减少KV缓存占用，但仅关注批内前缀复用以节约内存，并未在性能更优的小规模同质前缀批处停止批形成；同时，现有前缀检测依赖基数树遍历，CPU开销巨大，常与GPU执行时间相当。

Method: 提出Feather前缀感知调度器，使用强化学习（RL）学习批大小与前缀同质性的最优权衡；设计分块哈希树（CHT）轻量级数据结构，实现快速前缀检测和高效请求选择，避免昂贵的树遍历。

Result: 在vLLM和SGLang中集成后，相比现有调度器实现2-10倍端到端吞吐量提升；在缺乏前缀共享的工作负载下性能不低于现状；通过减少KV缓存访问总量实现增益，超越了具有相同目标的前缀感知注意力内核。

Conclusion: Feather通过RL驱动的调度策略和CHT轻量级前缀检测机制，有效提升了前缀共享工作负载下的解码吞吐量，验证了更小规模但更同质的前缀批比大规模异构批具有更高性能这一关键洞察。

Original Abstract: Auto-regressive token generation in large language models is memory-bound because it requires "attending to" key and value tensors (KV cache) of all previous tokens. Prior work aims to improve the efficiency of this decode process by batching multiple requests together, and maximizing batch size subject to GPU memory constraints. The key observation of our work is that with prefix-sharing workloads, smaller, prefix-homogeneous batches -- where all requests share a common prefix -- can achieve higher decode throughput than larger, heterogeneous batches, due to better spatial and temporal locality during KV cache accesses. However, prefix-aware schedulers in state-of-the-art inference engines maximize prefix reuse within a batch only to reduce KV cache memory footprint, but do not stop batch formation at smaller homogeneous batches that could have performed better. Further, we show that shared prefix detection in existing schedulers relies on radix-tree traversals, incurring substantial CPU overhead that is often comparable to GPU execution time. This paper presents Feather, a prefix-aware scheduler that uses reinforcement learning (RL) to learn the optimal tradeoff between batch size and prefix homogeneity. We also introduce Chunked Hash Tree (CHT), a lightweight data structure that enables fast prefix detection and efficient request selection for the RL scheduler, avoiding expensive tree traversals. We integrate Feather into vLLM and SGLang, and our evaluation shows that Feather achieves 2--10$\times$ higher end-to-end throughput as compared to existing schedulers, while doing no worse than the status quo when the workload does not have enough prefix sharing. Feather achieves these gains by reducing the total number of KV cache accesses, surpassing the performance of prefix-aware attention kernels that have the same goal.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型中的自回归token生成是内存受限的，因为它需要"关注"所有先前token的键值张量（KV缓存）。先前的工作通过将多个请求批量处理，并在GPU内存约束下最大化批处理大小来提高解码过程的效率。我们的关键观察是，在前缀共享工作负载下，更小规模、前缀同质的批处理——其中所有请求共享共同的前缀——由于KV缓存访问具有更好的空间和时间局部性，可以比更大规模的异构批处理实现更高的解码吞吐量。然而，最先进推理引擎中的前缀感知调度器仅为了减少KV缓存内存占用而最大化批内的前缀复用，并没有在可能表现更好的小规模同质批处停止批形成。此外，我们表明，现有调度器中的共享前缀检测依赖于基数树遍历，导致大量CPU开销，通常与GPU执行时间相当。本文提出Feather，一种使用强化学习（RL）来学习批处理大小与前缀同质性之间最优权衡的前缀感知调度器。我们还引入了分块哈希树（CHT），一种轻量级数据结构，能够为RL调度器实现快速前缀检测和高效请求选择，避免昂贵的树遍历。我们将Feather集成到vLLM和SGLang中，评估结果表明，与现有调度器相比，Feather实现了2-10倍的端到端吞吐量提升，而在工作负载没有足够前缀共享时，其性能不低于现状。Feather通过减少KV缓存访问的总量来实现这些增益，超越了具有相同目标的前缀感知注意力内核的性能。

</details>


### [172] [Towards Generation-Efficient Uncertainty Estimation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06053)
*Mingcheng Zhu,Yu Liu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在LLM自回归生成过程中，利用部分生成甚至仅输入信息进行高效不确定性估计的方法，提出Logit Magnitude（基于早停生成前缀的top-M logit估计）和MetaUE（将基于生成的不确定性蒸馏为轻量级输入仅估计器），实验表明有效不确定性估计所需的生成量远少于通常假设。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有不确定性估计方法需要一次或多次完整的自回归生成，推断成本高且延迟不确定性评估。本文旨在探索能否通过部分生成甚至仅输入信息实现有效的不确定性估计，以降低计算开销。

Method: 首先建立统一框架，将不确定性估计形式化为自回归生成过程中的早期估计问题，按观测信息量对现有及新提出的估计器进行组织。提出两种低开销方法：(1) Logit Magnitude——利用早停生成前缀的top-M logit证据估计不确定性；(2) MetaUE——将基于生成的不确定性分数蒸馏为轻量级输入仅估计器。

Result: 在通用和领域特定基准上，Logit Magnitude表现出强性能，LLM的部分生成通常足以进行有效不确定性估计；MetaUE在多个设置下提供了有竞争力的输入仅近似估计。

Conclusion: 有效的不确定性估计所需的生成量远少于通常假设，可以在生成早期识别不可靠响应，从而降低推断成本和延迟。

Original Abstract: Uncertainty estimation is important for deploying LLMs in high-stakes applications such as healthcare and finance, where hallucinations can appear fluent and plausible while being factually incorrect, making it difficult for users to judge whether an output should be trusted. Existing methods require one or more full autoregressive generations to estimate uncertainty, which introduces substantial inference cost and often delays uncertainty assessment. In this paper, we investigate whether effective uncertainty estimation can be achieved with partial generation or even input-only information. Specifically, we first develop a unified framework that formulates uncertainty estimation as an early estimation problem over the autoregressive generation process of LLMs. This framework organises existing and proposed estimators by the information they observe, ranging from multi-generation to input-only prediction, and clarifies the performance-cost trade-off underlying different uncertainty estimation methods. Building on this view, we study two largely underexplored low-cost settings: estimating uncertainty with part of the generation, and predicting uncertainty from the input prompt. We propose Logit Magnitude, which uses top-M logit evidence to estimate uncertainty from an early-stopped generation prefix, and MetaUE, which distils generation-based uncertainty into a lightweight input-only estimator trained with uncertainty scores. Extensive experiments on general and domain-specific benchmarks show that Logit Magnitude achieves strong performance, and partial generations of LLMs are often sufficient for effective uncertainty estimation. MetaUE further provides a competitive input-only approximation in several settings. These findings suggest that effective uncertainty estimation requires less generation than commonly assumed, enabling unreliable responses to be identified earlier.

中文摘要翻译: 不确定性估计对于在医疗和金融等高安全场景中部署大型语言模型（LLM）至关重要，因为这些场景中的幻觉可能表面流畅且看似合理，但事实上不正确，使得用户难以判断输出是否可信。现有方法需要一次或多次完整的自回归生成来估计不确定性，这带来了显著的推断成本且常常延迟了不确定性评估。本文研究能否通过部分生成甚至仅输入信息实现有效的不确定性估计。具体而言，我们首先开发了一个统一框架，将不确定性估计形式化为LLM自回归生成过程中的早期估计问题。该框架按观测信息量（从多生成到仅输入预测）组织现有及新提出的估计器，阐明了不同不确定性估计方法背后的性能-成本权衡。基于此视角，我们研究了两个尚未充分探索的低成本场景：利用部分生成估计不确定性，以及从输入提示预测不确定性。我们提出了Logit Magnitude，利用早停生成前缀的top-M logit证据估计不确定性；以及MetaUE，将基于生成的不确定性蒸馏为使用不确定性分数训练的轻量级输入仅估计器。在通用和领域特定基准上的大量实验表明，Logit Magnitude实现了强性能，LLM的部分生成通常足以进行有效的不确定性估计。MetaUE在多个设置下进一步提供了有竞争力的输入仅近似估计。这些发现表明，有效的不确定性估计所需的生成量远少于通常假设，从而能够更早地识别不可靠响应。

</details>


### [173] [Towards Self-Explainable Document Visual Question Answering with Chain-of-Explanation Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06058)
*Kjetil Indrehus,Adrian Duric,Changkyu Choi,Ali Ramezani-Kebrya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoExVQA 提出一种自解释的文档视觉问答框架，通过链式解释设计，先识别问题相关证据，再定位答案区域，最后仅从该区域解码答案，在 PFL-DocVQA 上 ANLS 提升 12%，实现了透明可验证的推理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有 DocVQA 模型将证据识别与答案定位纠缠在一起，缺乏可解释性，难以验证预测如何依赖于视觉证据。

Method: 提出 CoExVQA 框架，采用链式解释设计：首先识别与问题相关的视觉证据，然后显式定位答案所在区域，最后仅从该定位区域解码出答案文本。

Result: 在 PFL-DocVQA 数据集上，CoExVQA 相比当前可解释基线方法在 ANLS 指标上提升了 12%，达到可解释 DocVQA 的 SotA 性能。

Conclusion: 限制解码到已定位的视觉证据区域，能够在不牺牲性能的前提下实现透明、可验证的预测，为 DocVQA 提供了有效的自解释范式。

Original Abstract: Document Visual Question Answering (DocVQA) requires vision-language models to reason not only about what information in a document is relevant to a question, but also where the answer is grounded on the page. Existing DocVQA models entangle question-relevant evidence and answer localization and operate largely as black boxes, offering limited means to verify how predictions depend on visual evidence. We propose CoExVQA, a self-explainable DocVQA framework with a grounded reasoning process through a chain-of-explanation design. CoExVQA first identifies question-relevant evidence, then explicitly localizes the answer region, and finally decodes the answer exclusively from the grounded region. Prediction via CoExVQA's chain-of-explanation enables direct inspection and verification of the reasoning process across modalities. Empirical results show that restricting decoding to grounded evidence achieves SotA explainable DocVQA performance on PFL-DocVQA, improving ANLS by 12% over the current explainable baselines while providing transparent and verifiable predictions.

中文摘要翻译: 文档视觉问答（DocVQA）要求视觉语言模型不仅推理文档中哪些信息与问题相关，还要确定答案在页面上的定位。现有的 DocVQA 模型将问题相关证据与答案定位纠缠在一起，本质上是一个黑盒系统，难以验证预测如何依赖于视觉证据。本文提出 CoExVQA，一种具有自解释能力的 DocVQA 框架，通过链式解释设计实现基于证据的推理过程。CoExVQA 首先识别与问题相关的证据，然后显式定位答案区域，最后仅从该定位区域解码答案。通过 CoExVQA 的链式解释进行预测，可以跨模态直接检查并验证推理过程。实验结果表明，将解码限制在已定位的证据区域上，在 PFL-DocVQA 数据集上达到了可解释 DocVQA 的当前最优性能，在 ANLS 指标上相比现有可解释基线方法提升了 12%，同时提供了透明且可验证的预测结果。

</details>


### [174] [Causal Reinforcement Learning for Complex Card Games: A Magic The Gathering Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06066)
*Cristiano da Costa Cunha,Ajmal Mian,Tim French,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MTG-Causal-RL，一个基于《万智牌》构建的因果强化学习基准平台，具备高维部分观测、大规模掩码动作空间和显式结构因果模型（SCM），并提出了CGFA-PPO作为参考因果智能体，为因果RL、世界模型和LLM智能体研究提供了可共享的测试平台。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有因果强化学习缺乏能够同时涵盖序列决策、隐藏信息、大规模掩码动作空间和显式因果结构的复杂系统基准，亟需一个兼具策略丰富性、部分可观测性和明确因果接口的标准化测试平台。

Method: 基于《万智牌》构建MTG-Causal-RL基准环境，包含3077维部分观测、478动作掩码离散空间、5个标准赛制套牌、3种奖励方案以及手写SCM；提出因果图分解优势PPO（CGFA-PPO），利用SCM中胜率变量的父节点作为因子对齐的评论家目标，并引入干预校准损失函数。

Result: Masked PPO和CGFA-PPO在分布内胜率上达到竞争水平并超过随机基线；每个因子的校准轨迹和留一法跨套牌迁移差距揭示了标量胜率无法单独诊断的结构化信息。

Conclusion: MTG-Causal-RL将策略丰富的部分可观测领域与显式因果接口和统计协议相结合，为因果RL、世界模型和LLM智能体研究提供了一个能够同时探究掩码动作空间下因果信用分配、跨套牌结构迁移和SCM驱动的策略可审计性等问题的共享测试平台。

Original Abstract: Causal reinforcement learning (RL) lacks benchmarks for complex systems that combine sequential decision making, hidden information, large masked action spaces, and explicit causal structure. We introduce MTG-Causal-RL, a Gymnasium benchmark built on Magic: The Gathering with a 3,077-dimensional partial observation, a 478-action masked discrete action space, five competitive Standard archetypes, three reward schemes, and a hand-specified Structural Causal Model (SCM) over strategic variables. Every episode exposes causal variables, SCM-predicted intervention effects, and per-factor credit traces, making causal credit assignment, leave-one-out cross-archetype transfer, and policy auditability first-class metrics. We adapt a panel of reference baselines: random, heuristic, masked PPO, a causal-world-model PPO variant, and an architecture-matched scalar control. We propose Causal Graph-Factored Advantage PPO (CGFA-PPO) as a reference causal agent that uses SCM parents of win probability as factor-aligned critic targets with an intervention-calibration loss. All comparisons use paired seeds, paired-bootstrap confidence intervals, and Holm-Bonferroni correction within pre-registered families. Masked PPO and CGFA-PPO reach competitive in-distribution win rates and exceed the random baseline; per-factor calibration trajectories and leave-one-out transfer gaps expose diagnostic structure that scalar win rate alone cannot. We release the benchmark, reference-baseline results, and full evaluation protocol openly. By coupling a strategically rich, partially observed domain with an explicit causal interface and statistical protocol, MTG-Causal-RL gives causal-RL, world-model, and LLM-agent research a shared testbed for questions current benchmarks cannot pose together: causal credit assignment under masked action spaces, structural transfer across archetypes, and SCM-grounded policy auditability.

中文摘要翻译: 因果强化学习（RL）缺乏针对复杂系统的基准——这类系统需要同时涵盖序列决策、隐藏信息、大规模掩码动作空间和显式因果结构。我们提出了MTG-Causal-RL，一个基于《万智牌：聚会》构建的Gymnasium基准平台，具有3077维部分观测、478动作掩码离散空间、五种竞争性标准赛制套牌、三种奖励方案以及一个针对战略变量手工指定的结构因果模型（SCM）。每一轮交互都会暴露因果变量、SCM预测的干预效应以及每个因子的信用痕迹，使得因果信用分配、留一法跨套牌迁移和策略可审计性成为第一类度量指标。我们适配了一组参考基线：随机、启发式、掩码PPO、一种因果世界模型PPO变体以及一个架构匹配的标量控制。我们提出了因果图分解优势PPO（CGFA-PPO）作为参考因果智能体，它使用胜率概率的SCM父节点作为因子对齐的评论家目标，并引入干预校准损失。所有比较均使用配对随机种子、配对自助置信区间以及预注册族内的Holm-Bonferroni校正。Masked PPO和CGFA-PPO在分布内胜率上达到了竞争性水平并超过了随机基线；每个因子的校准轨迹和留一法迁移差距揭示了标量胜率单独无法诊断的结构化信息。我们公开了该基准、参考基线结果和完整的评估协议。通过将策略丰富的部分可观测领域与显式因果接口和统计协议相结合，MTG-Causal-RL为因果RL、世界模型和LLM智能体研究提供了一个共享测试平台，用以解决当前基准无法同时提出的问题：掩码动作空间下的因果信用分配、跨套牌的结构迁移以及基于SCM的策略可审计性。

</details>


### [175] [Normalized Architectures are Natively 4-Bit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06067)
*Maxim Fishman,Brian Chmiel,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: nGPT架构由于权重和隐藏表示被约束在单位超球面上，对低精度（4-bit）算术具有内在鲁棒性，无需额外干预即可实现稳定的NVFP4训练，且这种优势随隐藏维度增大而增强。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在大语言模型中以4-bit精度进行训练对于效率至关重要，但低精度算术通常会损害模型质量，需要额外干预（如随机Hadamard变换、逐张量缩放计算）来维持模型质量，本文旨在寻找一种更简洁、无需额外干预的低精度训练方案。

Method: 利用nGPT架构，该架构将权重和隐藏表示约束在单位超球面上，利用其内在的低精度算术鲁棒性实现端到端NVFP4训练，无需随机Hadamard变换或逐张量缩放计算等额外干预。

Result: 在1.2B稠密模型和高达3B/30B参数的混合（Mamba-Transformer）MoE模型上验证了方法的有效性；理论分析表明，超球面约束增强了元素级乘积中的弱正相关，使信号沿隐藏维度构建性累积而噪声相互抵消，从而获得更高的有效信噪比和更平坦的损失曲面，且该优势随隐藏维度增长而增强。

Conclusion: nGPT架构对低精度训练具有天然鲁棒性，可以消除额外干预措施，在大规模模型上具有越来越大的优势，为高效的大语言模型低精度训练提供了简洁有效的方案。

Original Abstract: Training large language models at 4-bit precision is critical for efficiency. We show that nGPT, an architecture that constrains weights and hidden representations to the unit hypersphere, is inherently more robust to low-precision arithmetic. This removes the need for interventions-such as applying random Hadamard transforms and performing per-tensor scaling calculations-to preserve model quality, and it enables stable end-to-end NVFP4 training. We validate this approach on both a 1.2B dense model and hybrid (Mamba-Transformer) MoE models of up to 3B/30B parameters. We trace this robustness to the dot product: while quantization noise remains largely uncorrelated in both standard and normalized architectures, the signal behaves differently. In nGPT, the hypersphere constraint enhances weak positive correlations among the element-wise products, leading to a constructive accumulation of the signal across the hidden dimension while the noise continues to average out. This yields a higher effective signal-to-noise ratio and a flatter loss landscape, with the effect strengthening as the hidden dimension grows, suggesting increasing advantages at scale. A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/anonymous452026/ngpt-nvfp4

中文摘要翻译: 以4-bit精度训练大语言模型对效率至关重要。我们证明了nGPT——一种将权重和隐藏表示约束在单位超球面上的架构——对低精度算术具有内在更强的鲁棒性。这消除了为保持模型质量而采取的干预措施（如应用随机Hadamard变换和执行逐张量缩放计算）的需求，并实现了稳定的端到端NVFP4训练。我们在1.2B稠密模型和高达3B/30B参数的混合（Mamba-Transformer）MoE模型上验证了该方法。我们将这种鲁棒性追溯到点积运算：虽然量化噪声在标准架构和归一化架构中仍然基本不相关，但信号行为有所不同。在nGPT中，超球面约束增强了元素级乘积中的弱正相关性，使得信号沿隐藏维度构建性累积，而噪声则持续相互抵消。这产生了更高的有效信噪比和更平坦的损失曲面，且该效应随隐藏维度增长而增强，表明在大规模下具有越来越大的优势。参考实现可在https://github.com/anonymous452026/ngpt-nvfp4获取。

</details>


### [176] [Understanding diffusion models requires rethinking (again) generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06077)
*Pierre Marion,Yu-Han Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文认为理解扩散模型的泛化需要超越传统统计学习理论和良性过拟合范式的新理论框架。扩散模型中记忆与泛化不相容，作者主张从研究为何不记忆转向研究预记忆阶段模型实际学到了什么，并通过CIFAR-10实验提炼出关键开放问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 指出扩散模型的泛化机制无法被现有统计学习理论（如良性过拟合范式）充分解释，因为扩散模型中记忆训练数据与泛化到新样本是互斥的。作者希望推动领域从"为什么扩散模型不记忆"转向"模型在预记忆阶段究竟学到了什么"。

Method: 本文为立场论文（position paper），核心方法包括：(1) 理论论证，指出扩散模型泛化需要新理论框架；(2) 在CIFAR-10上对扩散模型进行实证研究；(3) 将实证发现提炼为具体的开放性问题。

Result: 证实扩散模型中记忆与泛化不相容，现有基于容量限制、隐式正则化和归纳偏置的理论解释虽然存在但相互作用尚不清晰；通过CIFAR-10实验揭示了预记忆阶段的学习行为，并转化为一系列关键开放问题。

Conclusion: 理解扩散模型的泛化需要全新的理论框架，未来研究应聚焦于扩散模型在预记忆阶段实际学习的内容，而非仅仅解释其为何不发生过拟合。

Original Abstract: This position paper argues that understanding generalization in diffusion models requires fundamentally new theoretical frameworks that go beyond both classical statistical learning theory and the benign overfitting paradigm developed for supervised learning. In diffusion models, unlike in supervised learning, memorization of training data and generalization to novel samples are incompatible: a model that has fully memorized its training set generates copies rather than novel data. Several theoretical explanations for why practical diffusion models nevertheless generalize have been proposed, based on capacity limitations, implicit regularization from optimization, or architectural inductive biases, but their interactions remain unclear. We argue that the field should pivot from explaining why the diffusion models do not memorize to investigating what the model actually learns during pre-memorization phase. To highlight our stance, we conduct empirical study of diffusion models trained on CIFAR-10, and we distill the findings into concrete open questions that we believe are key to improve understanding of generalization in diffusion models.

中文摘要翻译: 本立场论文认为，理解扩散模型中的泛化需要从根本上建立新的理论框架，这些框架需超越经典统计学习理论以及为监督学习开发的良性过拟合范式。与监督学习不同，在扩散模型中，训练数据的记忆化与对新样本的泛化是不相容的：完全记忆了训练集的模型生成的是复制品而非新数据。已有若干基于容量限制、来自优化的隐式正则化或架构归纳偏置的理论解释来说明为何实际中的扩散模型仍能泛化，但它们之间的相互作用尚不清晰。我们认为，该领域应从解释为何扩散模型不记忆，转向研究模型在预记忆阶段实际学习了什么。为突出我们的立场，我们在CIFAR-10上对扩散模型进行了实证研究，并将实验结果提炼为具体的开放性问题，我们认为这些问题对于增进对扩散模型泛化机制的理解至关重要。

</details>


### [177] [Fast Gauss-Newton for Multiclass Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06081)
*Mikalai Korbit,Mario Zanon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fast Gauss-Newton (FGN)，通过对多分类Softmax交叉熵的广义Gauss-Newton曲率进行精确分解，保留真值vs其余项、丢弃类内协方差项，得到GGN的半正定下界近似，并在阻尼更新中利用共轭梯度法实现无矩阵高效求解。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多分类Softmax交叉熵中，完整的广义Gauss-Newton曲率通过Softmax协方差耦合所有输出logit，导致曲率-向量乘积的计算随类别数增长难以扩展，亟需一种可扩展且保持理论性质的近似方法。

Method: 1) 将多分类GGN精确分解为真值vs其余项（保留）和类内协方差项（丢弃），得到FGN半正定下界近似；2) 利用真值vs其余标量间隔表示Softmax交叉熵，确保损失与梯度不变，近似仅作用于曲率层；3) 将阻尼更新等价转换为白化行空间系统，每小批量样本对应一行；4) 通过共轭梯度法结合标量间隔映射的Jacobian-向量积和向量-Jacobian积实现无矩阵求解。

Result: 机制实验和固定特征多分类头评估验证了分解预测：当竞争类概率集中或阻尼较大时，FGN最接近完整Softmax GGN；当丢弃的类内协方差增大时，偏差随之增大。

Conclusion: FGN通过丢弃类内协方差项实现了对多分类GGN的有效半正定下界近似，在理论上对二分类精确成立，在实践中当竞争类质量集中或阻尼较大时表现良好，为大规模多分类场景下的曲率近似提供了可扩展方案。

Original Abstract: In multiclass softmax cross-entropy, the full generalized Gauss-Newton (GGN) curvature couples all output logits through the softmax covariance, making curvature-vector products harder to scale as the number of classes grows. We show that the standard multiclass GGN can be decomposed exactly into a true-vs-rest term and a positive semidefinite within-competitor covariance term. Fast Gauss-Newton (FGN) retains the first term and drops the second, yielding a positive semidefinite under-approximation of the multiclass GGN that is exact for binary classification. The derivation uses an exact true-vs-rest scalar-margin representation of softmax cross-entropy: the loss and gradient are unchanged, and the approximation enters only at the curvature level. Exploiting the FGN curvature structure, the damped update can be written as an equivalent whitened row-space system with one row per mini-batch example. We solve this system matrix-free by conjugate gradient using Jacobian-vector and vector-Jacobian products of the scalar margin map. Targeted mechanism experiments and an evaluation on a fixed-feature multiclass head support the predictions from the decomposition: FGN stays closest to the full softmax GGN when competitor mass is concentrated or damping is large, and deviates as the dropped within-competitor covariance grows.

中文摘要翻译: 在多分类Softmax交叉熵中，完整的广义Gauss-Newton（GGN）曲率通过Softmax协方差耦合所有输出logit，使得曲率-向量乘积的计算随类别数增长难以扩展。我们证明，标准多分类GGN可以被精确分解为一个真值vs其余项和一个半正定的类内协方差项。Fast Gauss-Newton (FGN) 保留第一项并丢弃第二项，得到多分类GGN的一个半正定下界近似，且该近似对二分类是精确的。推导使用了Softmax交叉熵的精确真值vs其余标量间隔表示：损失和梯度保持不变，近似仅作用于曲率层面。利用FGN曲率结构，阻尼更新可等价写为一个白化行空间系统，每小批量样本对应一行。我们通过共轭梯度法求解该系统，无需显式构建矩阵，仅利用标量间隔映射的Jacobian-向量积和向量-Jacobian积。针对性的机制实验以及在固定特征多分类头上进行的评估支持了分解的预测：当竞争类质量集中或阻尼较大时，FGN最接近完整的Softmax GGN；随着被丢弃的类内协方差增大，FGN的偏差也随之增大。

</details>


### [178] [Beyond Autoregressive RTG: Conditioning via Injection Outside Sequential Modeling in Decision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06104)
*Yongyi Wang,Hanyu Liu,Lingfeng Li,Bozhou Chen,Ang Li,Qirui Zheng,Xionghui Yang,Chucai Wang,Wenxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SlimDT 通过将 RTG 信息注入状态表示而非作为独立 token，将序列长度减少三分之一，在 D4RL 基准上超越标准 DT，达到与 SOTA 方法相当的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准 DT 中 RTG 作为标量 token 与状态、动作向量共享相同计算预算，但信息量远少于后者；且 RTG token 增加了序列长度，导致 Transformer 自注意力计算成本平方级增长。

Method: SlimDT 将 RTG 信息注入到状态表示中，然后在序列建模阶段仅处理紧凑的（状态，动作）序列，从而移除 RTG 作为独立 token。

Result: 在 D4RL 基准上，SlimDT 超越标准 DT，性能与现有 SOTA 方法相当。

Conclusion: 将稀疏的条件信号从信息丰富的序列中解耦，既能提升计算效率，又能提高任务性能。

Original Abstract: Decision Transformer (DT) formulates offline reinforcement learning as autoregressive sequence modeling, achieving promising results by predicting actions from a sequence of Return-to-Go (RTG), state, and action tokens. However, RTG is a scalar that summarizes future rewards, containing far less information than typical state or action vectors, yet it consumes the same computational budget per token. Worse, the self-attention cost of Transformers grows quadratically with sequence length, so including RTG as a separate token adds unnecessary overhead. We propose SlimDT, which removes RTG from the autoregressive sequence. Instead, we inject RTG information into the state representations before the sequential modeling step, allowing the Transformer to process only a compact (state, action) sequence. This reduces the sequence length by one-third, directly improving inference efficiency. On the D4RL benchmark, SlimDT surpasses standard DT across various tasks and achieves performance comparable to existing state-of-the-art methods. Decoupling a sparse conditioning signal from an information-rich sequence thus yields both computational gains and higher task performance.

中文摘要翻译: 决策Transformer（DT）将离线强化学习建模为自回归序列预测问题，通过从包含目标回报（Return-to-Go, RTG）、状态和动作token的序列中预测动作，取得了令人瞩目的成果。然而，RTG是一个总结未来奖励的标量，包含的信息远少于典型的状态或动作向量，但却消耗相同的每个token计算预算。更糟糕的是，Transformer的自注意力成本随序列长度呈二次增长，因此将RTG作为独立token会带来不必要的开销。我们提出SlimDT，将RTG从自回归序列中移除。相反，在序列建模步骤之前，我们将RTG信息注入到状态表示中，使得Transformer仅需处理紧凑的（状态，动作）序列。这使序列长度减少三分之一，直接提高了推理效率。在D4RL基准测试中，SlimDT在各种任务上超越了标准DT，并实现了与现有最先进方法相当的性能。因此，将稀疏的条件信号从信息丰富的序列中解耦既能带来计算收益，又能提升任务性能。

</details>


### [179] [Matrix-Valued Optimism is Matrix-Valued Augmentation: Additive Hybrid Designs for Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06141)
*Jiayi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了增广拉格朗日方法与乐观原始-对偶方法在矩阵值校正下的等价性，揭示了校正矩阵在两种通道间的可加性原理，并据此提出了一种混合设计方法，在非线性等式约束优化中优于纯增广和纯乐观方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究表明增广拉格朗日方法（通过增加约束相关的原始曲率）与乐观原始-对偶方法（通过增加对偶记忆）在标量参数下等价，但矩阵值参数下的等价性尚不明确。本文旨在建立这一等价性并利用设计自由度改进优化性能。

Method: 首先证明了对称矩阵参数下的可加性原理：理想原始轨迹仅依赖于总的校正矩阵，而非其在增广通道与乐观通道之间的分配方式。然后基于此设计自由度，推导了一个闭式混合规则，该规则选择矩阵校正、在两个通道间分配，并使用局部谱权重选择原始和对偶步长。

Result: 在非线性等式约束问题上的实验表明：混合设计优于纯增广和纯乐观方法，紧密追踪网格搜索的混合最优，并在轻度至中度病态条件下与一阶原始-对偶基线方法具有竞争力。实验也揭示了局限性：当约束雅可比矩阵病态时，精确抵消需要越来越大的矩阵校正。

Conclusion: 增广拉格朗日与乐观原始-对偶方法在矩阵值校正下等价，可加性原理提供了设计灵活性；所提出的混合规则在实际中有效，但受限于病态条件下的数值稳定性需求。

Original Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian and optimistic primal--dual methods stabilize equality-constrained optimization through seemingly different mechanisms: the former adds constraint-dependent primal curvature, while the latter adds dual memory. Recent work has shown that these mechanisms are equivalent for scalar parameters. We extend this equivalence to matrix-valued correction. We prove an additivity principle: for symmetric matrix parameters, the ideal primal trajectory depends only on the summed correction matrix, not on how it is split between augmented and optimistic channels. This exposes a design freedom: algebraically equivalent decompositions can have different finite-step feasibility because augmented correction affects primal curvature, whereas optimistic correction affects the scale of the dual memory correction. We formulate the resulting step-size-limited design problem and derive a closed-form hybrid rule that selects a matrix correction, splits it between the two channels, and chooses primal and dual steps using local spectral weights.
